"""River Flow Extraction Fabric — dual-axis, replay-verifiable extraction (PDF §4.3).

Two artifact families:

* **Vertical slice** — the full system state at every timestep of a window.
* **Horizontal slice** — a single parameter trajectory over time.

Guarantees:

* content-addressed Parquet payloads (SHA-256 over the canonical frame),
* idempotent builds (same input -> same ``slice_id``),
* quality flags and gaps preserved verbatim (the fabric never re-judges
  physics; upstream Admissibility owns rejection),
* a **Replay Certificate** that binds slice ids, content hashes and a root
  hash together so the extraction can be verified end-to-end.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import pandas as pd

from chariot.config import (
    RIVER_HORIZONTAL_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    RIVER_VERTICAL_SCHEMA_VERSION,
)
from chariot.hashing import frame_content_hash, hash_obj, short_hash
from chariot.store import Store, new_id, utc_now


def _payload_root() -> Path:
    return Path("var") / "river_flow"


def slice_id_for(kind: str, tenant_id: str, site_id: str, content_hash: str) -> str:
    """Deterministic slice id: identical content -> identical id."""
    return f"slice_{kind}_{short_hash(hash_obj([kind, tenant_id, site_id, content_hash]), 16)}"


def write_payload(frame: pd.DataFrame, content_hash: str) -> str:
    """Write the Parquet payload for a slice and return its relative path."""
    root = _payload_root()
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path = root / f"{content_hash}.parquet"
    if not path.exists():
        frame.to_parquet(path, index=False)
    return str(path)


def build_vertical_slice(
    store: Store,
    frame: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    tenant_id: str,
    site_id: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build (idempotently) a vertical slice: full state over a time window."""
    if "timestamp_utc" not in frame.columns:
        raise ValueError("vertical slice requires a timestamp_utc column")
    ordered = frame.sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)
    content_hash = frame_content_hash(ordered)
    slice_id = slice_id_for("vertical", tenant_id, site_id, content_hash)
    path = write_payload(ordered, content_hash)
    row = {
        "slice_id": slice_id,
        "tenant_id": tenant_id,
        "site_id": site_id,
        "slice_type": "vertical",
        "parameter": None,
        "time_start": str(ordered["timestamp_utc"].min()),
        "time_end": str(ordered["timestamp_utc"].max()),
        "n_rows": int(len(ordered)),
        "content_hash": content_hash,
        "path": path,
        "replay_id": None,
        "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
    }
    store.upsert("river_slices", row)
    return {**row, "schema_version": RIVER_VERTICAL_SCHEMA_VERSION}


def build_horizontal_slice(
    store: Store,
    frame: pd.DataFrame,
    parameter: str,
    *,
    tenant_id: str,
    site_id: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build (idempotently) a horizontal slice: one parameter vs. time."""
    if parameter not in frame.columns:
        raise KeyError(f"parameter {parameter!r} not present in frame")
    ordered = frame.sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)
    content_hash = frame_content_hash(ordered)
    slice_id = slice_id_for("horizontal", tenant_id, site_id, f"{parameter}:{content_hash}")
    path = write_payload(ordered, content_hash)
    row = {
        "slice_id": slice_id,
        "tenant_id": tenant_id,
        "site_id": site_id,
        "slice_type": "horizontal",
        "parameter": parameter,
        "time_start": str(ordered["timestamp_utc"].min()),
        "time_end": str(ordered["timestamp_utc"].max()),
        "n_rows": int(len(ordered)),
        "content_hash": content_hash,
        "path": path,
        "replay_id": None,
        "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
    }
    store.upsert("river_slices", row)
    return {**row, "schema_version": RIVER_HORIZONTAL_SCHEMA_VERSION}


def issue_replay_certificate(
    store: Store, slice_ids: Sequence[str], *, tenant_id: str, site_id: str
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Bind a set of slices into a Replay Certificate with a root hash."""
    if not slice_ids:
        raise ValueError("a replay certificate needs at least one slice")
    rows = []
    for sid in slice_ids:
        row = store.query_one("SELECT * FROM river_slices WHERE slice_id = ?", (sid,))
        if row is None:
            raise KeyError(f"unknown slice {sid}")
        rows.append(row)
    content_hashes = [r["content_hash"] for r in rows]
    root_hash = hash_obj(
        {
            "slice_ids": list(slice_ids),
            "content_hashes": content_hashes,
            "schema_version": RIVER_VERTICAL_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        }
    )
    replay_id = f"replay_{short_hash(root_hash, 20)}"
    record = {
        "replay_id": replay_id,
        "tenant_id": tenant_id,
        "site_id": site_id,
        "slice_ids": list(slice_ids),
        "content_hashes": content_hashes,
        "root_hash": root_hash,
        "schema_version": RIVER_VERTICAL_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
    }
    store.upsert("replay_certificates", record)
    for row in rows:
        store.execute(
            "UPDATE river_slices SET replay_id = ? WHERE slice_id = ?", (replay_id, row["slice_id"])
        )
    store.audit(
        "river_flow.replay",
        "replay_certificate",
        replay_id,
        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        payload={"slice_ids": list(slice_ids), "root_hash": root_hash},
    )
    return record


def verify_replay(store: Store, replay_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Re-verify a Replay Certificate: recompute hashes and compare."""
    record = store.query_one(
        "SELECT * FROM replay_certificates WHERE replay_id = ?", (replay_id,)
    )
    if record is None:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown replay {replay_id}")
    slice_ids = record["slice_ids"]
    content_hashes = record["content_hashes"]
    problems: list[str] = []
    for sid, expected in zip(slice_ids, content_hashes):
        row = store.query_one("SELECT * FROM river_slices WHERE slice_id = ?", (sid,))
        if row is None:
            problems.append(f"missing slice {sid}")
            continue
        path = Path(row["path"])
        if not path.exists():
            problems.append(f"missing payload for {sid}")
            continue
        frame = pd.read_parquet(path)
        actual = frame_content_hash(frame)
        if actual != expected:
            problems.append(f"content hash mismatch for {sid}: {actual} != {expected}")
    recomputed_root = hash_obj(
        {
            "slice_ids": list(slice_ids),
            "content_hashes": content_hashes,
            "schema_version": record["schema_version"],
        }
    )
    if recomputed_root != record["root_hash"]:
        problems.append("root hash mismatch")
    return {
        "replay_id": replay_id,
        "valid": not problems,
        "problems": problems,
        "root_hash": record["root_hash"],
        "recomputed_root_hash": recomputed_root,
        "slice_ids": list(slice_ids),
        "content_hashes": list(content_hashes),
        "n_slices": len(slice_ids),
    }


def load_slice(store: Store, slice_id: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Load the payload of a slice by id."""
    row = store.query_one("SELECT * FROM river_slices WHERE slice_id = ?", (slice_id,))
    if row is None:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown slice {slice_id}")
    return pd.read_parquet(row["path"])


def new_replay_id() -> str:  # pragma: no cover - helper for external callers
    return new_id("replay")

versioned, scoped, audited calibration
file   : chariot-vscode/chariot/calibration.py
lines  : 246

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
"""Calibration Engine — versioned, hierarchical effective physical parameters.

PDF §4.4.  The four primary *effective* parameters are mathematically
effective abstractions, not universal constants and not directly visible
sensor readings:

======================  ========  ==========================================
Parameter               Symbol    Relation
======================  ========  ==========================================
Capacity factor         c_f       E_usable = c_f * E_nominal
Charge loss factor      l_c       eta_c   = 1 - l_c
Discharge loss factor   l_d       eta_d   = 1 - l_d
PV correction factor    gamma_pv  P_eff   = gamma_pv * P_meas
======================  ========  ==========================================

Scope priority (most specific wins): equipment -> site -> fleet -> identity
defaults.  Lifecycle is ``draft -> approved -> retired``; every transition is
audited and approved versions are immutable (retire + create a new version
rather than editing).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from chariot.config import CALIBRATION_DEFAULTS, CalibrationDefaults
from chariot.store import Store, utc_now

SCOPE_ORDER = ("equipment", "site", "fleet")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CalibrationFactors:
    """The four effective parameters plus their effective physical meaning."""

    capacity_factor: float
    charge_loss_factor: float
    discharge_loss_factor: float
    pv_correction_factor: float
    calibration_version: str
    scope_type: str
    scope_id: str

    @property
    def usable_capacity_wh(self) -> float | None:
        return None  # resolved against a site rating at apply time

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "capacity_factor": self.capacity_factor,
            "charge_loss_factor": self.charge_loss_factor,
            "discharge_loss_factor": self.discharge_loss_factor,
            "pv_correction_factor": self.pv_correction_factor,
            "calibration_version": self.calibration_version,
            "scope_type": self.scope_type,
            "scope_id": self.scope_id,
            "effective_charge_efficiency": 1.0 - self.charge_loss_factor,
            "effective_discharge_efficiency": 1.0 - self.discharge_loss_factor,
        }


def identity_factors(scope_type: str = "identity", scope_id: str = "default") -> CalibrationFactors:
    """Identity defaults: (1, 0, 0, 1).  Resolution always succeeds."""
    d = CALIBRATION_DEFAULTS
    return CalibrationFactors(
        capacity_factor=d.capacity_factor,
        charge_loss_factor=d.charge_loss_factor,
        discharge_loss_factor=d.discharge_loss_factor,
        pv_correction_factor=d.pv_correction_factor,
        calibration_version="calibration_identity_v1",
        scope_type=scope_type,
        scope_id=scope_id,
    )


def create_version(
    store: Store,
    *,
    scope_type: str,
    scope_id: str,
    capacity_factor: float = 1.0,
    charge_loss_factor: float = 0.0,
    discharge_loss_factor: float = 0.0,
    pv_correction_factor: float = 1.0,
    method: str = "engineering_declaration",
    notes: str = "",
    tenant_id: str | None = None,
    site_id: str | None = None,
    created_by: str = "system",
) -> str:
    """Create a *draft* calibration version.  Approve privilege is separate."""
    if scope_type not in SCOPE_ORDER:
        raise ValueError(f"scope_type must be one of {SCOPE_ORDER}, got {scope_type!r}")
    if not (0.0 < capacity_factor <= 1.2):
        raise ValueError("capacity_factor must be in (0, 1.2]")
    if not (0.0 <= charge_loss_factor < 0.5):
        raise ValueError("charge_loss_factor must be in [0, 0.5)")
    if not (0.0 <= discharge_loss_factor < 0.5):
        raise ValueError("discharge_loss_factor must be in [0, 0.5)")
    if not (0.0 < pv_correction_factor <= 1.2):
        raise ValueError("pv_correction_factor must be in (0, 1.2]")

    version = f"calib_{scope_type}_{scope_id}_{utc_now().replace(':', '').replace('-', '')}"
    store.insert(
        "calibration_versions",
        {
            "calibration_version": version,
            "scope_type": scope_type,
            "scope_id": scope_id,
            "tenant_id": tenant_id,
            "site_id": site_id,
            "capacity_factor": capacity_factor,
            "charge_loss_factor": charge_loss_factor,
            "discharge_loss_factor": discharge_loss_factor,
            "pv_correction_factor": pv_correction_factor,
            "status": "draft",
            "method": method,
            "notes": notes,
            "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
            "approved_at_utc": None,
            "approved_by": None,
            "retired_at_utc": None,
        },
    )
    store.audit(
        "calibration.create",
        "calibration_version",
        version,
        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        actor=created_by,
        payload={"scope_type": scope_type, "scope_id": scope_id, "method": method},
    )
    return version


def approve_version(
    store: Store, calibration_version: str, approved_by: str = "system"
) -> None:
    """Approve a draft version.  Approval is a privileged, audited action."""
    row = store.query_one(
        "SELECT * FROM calibration_versions WHERE calibration_version = ?",
        (calibration_version,),
    )
    if row is None:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown calibration version {calibration_version}")
    if row["status"] != "draft":
        raise ValueError(f"only draft versions can be approved (got {row['status']})")
    store.execute(
        "UPDATE calibration_versions SET status='approved', approved_at_utc=?, approved_by=? "
        "WHERE calibration_version=?",
        (utc_now(), approved_by, calibration_version),
    )
    store.audit(
        "calibration.approve",
        "calibration_version",
        calibration_version,
        actor=approved_by,
        payload={"calibration_version": calibration_version},
    )


def retire_version(
    store: Store, calibration_version: str, actor: str = "system"
) -> None:
    """Retire an approved version (it stays queryable for lineage)."""
    store.execute(
        "UPDATE calibration_versions SET status='retired', retired_at_utc=? "
        "WHERE calibration_version=?",
        (utc_now(), calibration_version),
    )
    store.audit(
        "calibration.retire",
        "calibration_version",
        calibration_version,
        actor=actor,
        payload={"calibration_version": calibration_version},
    )


def resolve(
    store: Store,
    *,
    tenant_id: str | None = None,
    site_id: str | None = None,
    fleet_id: str | None = None,
    equipment_id: str | None = None,
    at: str | None = None,
) -> CalibrationFactors:
    """Resolve the effective calibration factors for a scope.

    Most specific approved scope wins: equipment -> site -> fleet -> identity.
    """
    candidates: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    if equipment_id:
        candidates.append(("equipment", equipment_id))
    if site_id:
        candidates.append(("site", site_id))
    if fleet_id:
        candidates.append(("fleet", fleet_id))

    for scope_type, scope_id in candidates:
        row = store.query_one(
            "SELECT * FROM calibration_versions "
            "WHERE scope_type=? AND scope_id=? AND status='approved' "
            "ORDER BY approved_at_utc DESC LIMIT 1",
            (scope_type, scope_id),
        )
        if row is not None:
            return CalibrationFactors(
                capacity_factor=float(row["capacity_factor"]),
                charge_loss_factor=float(row["charge_loss_factor"]),
                discharge_loss_factor=float(row["discharge_loss_factor"]),
                pv_correction_factor=float(row["pv_correction_factor"]),
                calibration_version=row["calibration_version"],
                scope_type=scope_type,
                scope_id=scope_id,
            )
    return identity_factors()


def effective_capacity_wh(
    factors: CalibrationFactors, nominal_capacity_wh: float | None
) -> float | None:
    """E_usable = c_f * E_nominal."""
    if nominal_capacity_wh is None:
        return None
    return float(factors.capacity_factor) * float(nominal_capacity_wh)


def stamp_application(
    store: Store,
    calibration_version: str,
    subject_type: str,
    subject_id: str,
    actor: str = "system",
) -> None:
    """Record that a calibration version was applied to an artifact."""
    store.audit(
        "calibration.apply",
        subject_type,
        subject_id,
        actor=actor,
        payload={"calibration_version": calibration_version},
    )

immutable capsule sealing/verification
file   : chariot-vscode/chariot/capsule.py
lines  : 207
bytes  : 7414

"""Immutable Capsule Sealing — earned immutability (PDF §4.5).

A capsule is created *after* admission, calibration and the seal-time
consistency checks.  Its id is

    capsule_id = sha256(payload_bytes || canonical_header)

where the canonical header carries tenant, site, window, schema version,
calibration version, admissibility versions, slice ids and slice content
hashes.  The payload is written atomically (file first, then the database
row) and never updated in place: a correction produces a *new* capsule.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

from chariot.config import CAPSULE_SCHEMA_VERSION
from chariot.hashing import canonical_json, frame_content_hash, hash_bytes_join, sha256_hex
from chariot.store import Store, utc_now


def _capsule_root() -> Path:
    return Path("var") / "capsules"


def seal_time_checks(
    frame: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    ctx: Any,
    calibration_version: str | None,
    require_clear_sky: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Seal-time consistency checks.  Any failure rejects the seal."""
    problems: list[str] = []
    if len(frame) == 0:
        problems.append("EMPTY_PAYLOAD")
    if "timestamp_utc" not in frame.columns:
        problems.append("MISSING_TIMESTAMP")
    else:
        ts = pd.to_datetime(frame["timestamp_utc"], utc=True, errors="coerce")
        if ts.isna().any():
            problems.append("INVALID_TIMESTAMP")
        if ts.notna().any():
            if ts.max() <= ts.min():
                problems.append("INVALID_TIME_RANGE")
            if not ts.is_monotonic_increasing:
                problems.append("NON_MONOTONIC_TIMESTAMP")
    if require_clear_sky and "clear_sky_w" in frame.columns:
        if frame["clear_sky_w"].isna().all():
            problems.append("NO_CLEAR_SKY")
    if ctx is not None and getattr(ctx, "rated_pv_w", None) is None:
        # Allowed: missing rating skips only the scale rules (logged upstream).
        pass
    if calibration_version is None:
        # Identity calibration is allowed by policy; recorded as a note.
        pass
    return {"ok": not problems, "problems": problems}


def seal_capsule(
    store: Store,
    frame: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    tenant_id: str,
    site_id: str,
    calibration_version: str | None,
    admissibility_versions: list[str],
    slice_ids: list[str],
    slice_hashes: list[str],
    ctx: Any = None,
    created_by: str = "system",
    require_clear_sky: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Seal an admitted, calibrated frame into an immutable capsule."""
    ordered = frame.sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)
    checks = seal_time_checks(
        ordered, ctx=ctx, calibration_version=calibration_version, require_clear_sky=require_clear_sky
    )
    if not checks["ok"]:
        raise ValueError(f"seal rejected: {checks['problems']}")

    payload_hash = frame_content_hash(ordered)
    root = _capsule_root()
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    header = {
        "tenant_id": tenant_id,
        "site_id": site_id,
        "window_start": str(ordered["timestamp_utc"].min()),
        "window_end": str(ordered["timestamp_utc"].max()),
        "schema_version": CAPSULE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "calibration_version": calibration_version,
        "admissibility_versions": list(admissibility_versions),
        "slice_ids": list(slice_ids),
        "slice_content_hashes": list(slice_hashes),
        "n_rows": int(len(ordered)),
    }
    payload_bytes = canonical_json(
        {k: header[k] for k in ("tenant_id", "site_id", "window_start", "window_end", "schema_version")}
    ) + b"|" + payload_hash.encode("ascii")
    capsule_id = hash_bytes_join(payload_bytes, canonical_json(header))

    path = root / f"{capsule_id}.parquet"
    if not path.exists():
        ordered.to_parquet(path, index=False)

    manifest = {
        **header,
        "payload_content_hash": payload_hash,
        "capsule_id": capsule_id,
        "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
        "created_by": created_by,
        "seal_checks": checks,
        "site_ratings": {
            "rated_pv_w": getattr(ctx, "rated_pv_w", None),
            "usable_battery_capacity_wh": getattr(ctx, "usable_battery_capacity_wh", None),
            "timezone": getattr(ctx, "timezone", None),
        },
    }
    row = {
        "capsule_id": capsule_id,
        "tenant_id": tenant_id,
        "site_id": site_id,
        "window_start": header["window_start"],
        "window_end": header["window_end"],
        "schema_version": CAPSULE_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "calibration_version": calibration_version,
        "admissibility_versions": list(admissibility_versions),
        "slice_ids": list(slice_ids),
        "content_hash": payload_hash,
        "payload_path": str(path),
        "manifest_json": canonical_json(manifest).decode("utf-8"),
        "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
        "created_by": created_by,
    }
    store.upsert("capsules", row)
    store.audit(
        "capsule.seal",
        "capsule",
        capsule_id,
        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        actor=created_by,
        payload={"content_hash": payload_hash, "slice_ids": list(slice_ids)},
    )
    return {**row, "manifest": manifest}


def load_capsule(store: Store, capsule_id: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Load the sealed payload of a capsule."""
    row = store.query_one("SELECT * FROM capsules WHERE capsule_id = ?", (capsule_id,))
    if row is None:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown capsule {capsule_id}")
    return pd.read_parquet(row["payload_path"])


def verify_capsule(store: Store, capsule_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Recompute the capsule id from the stored payload and header."""
    row = store.query_one("SELECT * FROM capsules WHERE capsule_id = ?", (capsule_id,))
    if row is None:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown capsule {capsule_id}")
    frame = pd.read_parquet(row["payload_path"])
    ordered = frame.sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)
    payload_hash = frame_content_hash(ordered)
    manifest = row.get("manifest_json") or {}
    if isinstance(manifest, str):
        manifest = json.loads(manifest)
    header = {
        "tenant_id": row["tenant_id"],
        "site_id": row["site_id"],
        "window_start": row["window_start"],
        "window_end": row["window_end"],
        "schema_version": row["schema_version"],
        "calibration_version": row["calibration_version"],
        "admissibility_versions": row["admissibility_versions"],
        "slice_ids": row["slice_ids"],
        "slice_content_hashes": manifest.get("slice_content_hashes", []),
        "n_rows": int(len(ordered)),
    }
    payload_bytes = (
        canonical_json(
            {
                k: header[k]
                for k in ("tenant_id", "site_id", "window_start", "window_end", "schema_version")
            }
        )
        + b"|"
        + payload_hash.encode("ascii")
    )
    recomputed = hash_bytes_join(payload_bytes, canonical_json(header))
    return {
        "capsule_id": capsule_id,
        "valid": recomputed == capsule_id,
        "recomputed_id": recomputed,
        "stored_id": capsule_id,
        "payload_hash_matches": payload_hash == row["content_hash"],
    }


def capsule_digest(capsule_id: str) -> str:  # pragma: no cover - helper
    return sha256_hex(capsule_id.encode("ascii"))

==============================================================================
SOURCE: chariot/training_extract.py
==============================================================================
materialised dataset + clear-sky gate + 60/20/20 split
file   : chariot-vscode/chariot/training_extract.py
lines  : 355
bytes  : 12737
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
"""Training Extract — materialized, versioned, model-ready dataset (PDF §4.6).

Design decisions frozen by the master reference:

* **Option B — materialized**: the extract is a versioned Parquet dataset plus
  a manifest and a content hash.  The Data Layer never trains.
* **Clear-sky is mandatory** for a row to be trainable.  A row without a
  clear-sky anchor is kept (with ``is_trainable=false`` and the gate code
  ``MISSING_CLEAR_SKY``) but is masked out of training.
* The build **fails** if zero trainable rows are produced.
* ``R_t = P_solar_meas / max(P_clear, eps)`` is stored as
  ``clear_sky_ratio_r``.

The dataset also carries the deterministic, time-ordered
``train / validate / test`` split assignment used by the CHARIOT model
(60 / 20 / 20 by default).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from chariot.config import FEATURE_SET_ID, TRAINING_EXTRACT_SCHEMA_VERSION
from chariot.hashing import frame_content_hash, hash_obj, short_hash
from chariot.store import Store, utc_now

CLEAR_SKY_EPS = 1.0

#: Canonical output columns of the training extract.
DATASET_COLUMNS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "tenant_id",
    "site_id",
    "timestamp_utc",
    "capsule_id",
    "dataset_version",
    "solar_w",
    "load_w",
    "battery_w",
    "soc_percent",
    "grid_w",
    "clear_sky_power_w",
    "clear_sky_ratio_r",
    "solar_effective_w",
    "rated_pv_w",
    "usable_battery_capacity_wh",
    "timezone",
    "is_gap",
    "quality_flags",
    "is_trainable",
    "train_gate_codes",
    "split",
    "calibration_version",
    "capacity_factor",
    "charge_loss_factor",
    "discharge_loss_factor",
    "pv_correction_factor",
)


def assign_splits(
    frame: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    train_frac: float = 0.60,
    validate_frac: float = 0.20,
    test_frac: float = 0.20,
    time_column: str = "timestamp_utc",
) -> pd.Series:
    """Assign a deterministic, time-ordered train/validate/test split.

    The split is chronological and never shuffles: the first ``train_frac`` of
    the timeline is train, the next ``validate_frac`` is validation, and the
    remainder is test.  Boundary rows are inclusive on the train/validate
    edge and exclusive elsewhere, so a row belongs to exactly one split and
    no timestamp appears in two splits.
    """
    total = train_frac + validate_frac + test_frac
    if not np.isclose(total, 1.0):
        raise ValueError(f"split fractions must sum to 1.0, got {total}")
    ordered = frame.sort_values(time_column).index
    n = len(ordered)
    n_train = int(np.floor(n * train_frac))
    n_val = int(np.floor(n * validate_frac))
    labels = pd.Series("test", index=frame.index, dtype="object")
    labels.loc[ordered[:n_train]] = "train"
    labels.loc[ordered[n_train : n_train + n_val]] = "validate"
    return labels


def _dataset_root() -> Path:
    return Path("var") / "datasets"


def build_training_extract(
    store: Store,
    frame: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    tenant_id: str,
    site_id: str,
    capsule_ids: list[str],
    calibration_version: str | None,
    calibration_factors: dict[str, float],
    admissibility_versions: list[str],
    slice_ids: list[str],
    ctx: Any,
    dataset_version: str | None = None,
    train_frac: float = 0.60,
    validate_frac: float = 0.20,
    test_frac: float = 0.20,
    created_by: str = "system",
    honour_split: bool = False,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build the materialized training extract.

    Returns the manifest.  Raises ``ValueError`` when no trainable row is
    produced (the build must fail closed).
    """
    if len(frame) == 0:
        raise ValueError("training extract build failed: empty admitted frame")

    ds_version = dataset_version or (
        f"dataset_{short_hash(hash_obj([tenant_id, site_id, capsule_ids]), 12)}"
    )

    out = pd.DataFrame(index=frame.index)
    out["tenant_id"] = tenant_id
    out["site_id"] = site_id
    out["timestamp_utc"] = pd.to_datetime(frame["timestamp_utc"], utc=True)
    out["capsule_id"] = capsule_ids[0] if capsule_ids else ""
    out["dataset_version"] = ds_version

    for col in ("solar_w", "load_w", "battery_w", "soc_percent", "grid_w"):
        out[col] = (
            pd.to_numeric(frame[col], errors="coerce").astype("float64")
            if col in frame.columns
            else np.nan
        )

    clear = (
        pd.to_numeric(frame["clear_sky_w"], errors="coerce").astype("float64")
        if "clear_sky_w" in frame.columns
        else np.nan
    )
    out["clear_sky_power_w"] = clear
    ratio = out["solar_w"] / clear.clip(lower=CLEAR_SKY_EPS)
    out["clear_sky_ratio_r"] = ratio.where(clear > CLEAR_SKY_EPS)

    pv_corr = float(calibration_factors.get("pv_correction_factor", 1.0))
    out["solar_effective_w"] = out["solar_w"] * pv_corr

    out["rated_pv_w"] = getattr(ctx, "rated_pv_w", None)
    out["usable_battery_capacity_wh"] = getattr(ctx, "usable_battery_capacity_wh", None)
    out["timezone"] = getattr(ctx, "timezone", "UTC")

    out["is_gap"] = (
        frame["is_gap"].astype(bool).to_numpy()
        if "is_gap" in frame.columns
        else False
    )
    out["quality_flags"] = (
        frame["quality_flags"].apply(list).to_numpy()
        if "quality_flags" in frame.columns
        else [[] for _ in range(len(frame))]
    )

    # -- trainability gates -------------------------------------------------
    codes: list[list[str]] = []
    for idx, row in out.iterrows():
        row_codes: list[str] = []
        if not np.isfinite(row["clear_sky_power_w"]) or row["clear_sky_power_w"] <= CLEAR_SKY_EPS:
            row_codes.append("MISSING_CLEAR_SKY")
        if not np.isfinite(row["solar_w"]):
            row_codes.append("MISSING_SOLAR")
        if bool(row["is_gap"]):
            row_codes.append("GAP_ROW")
        if row["solar_w"] < 0:
            row_codes.append("NEGATIVE_SOLAR")
        codes.append(row_codes)
    out["train_gate_codes"] = codes
    out["is_trainable"] = [len(c) == 0 for c in codes]

    if honour_split and "split" in frame.columns:
        # The 2022 -> 2023 protocol assigns the split *upstream* (train and
        # validate live in 2022, the report-only test lives in 2023), so the
        # chronological re-split must be skipped.  The assignment is opt-in:
        # with the default the behaviour is bit-identical to v3.
        pre = frame["split"].astype(str).str.strip().str.lower()
        if pre.isin(["train", "validate", "test"]).all():
            out["split"] = pre.to_numpy()
            split_policy = "pre_assigned (upstream protocol)"
        else:  # pragma: no cover - defensive
            out["split"] = assign_splits(
                out,
                train_frac=train_frac,
                validate_frac=validate_frac,
                test_frac=test_frac,
            )
            split_policy = "time_ordered_60_20_20"
    else:
        out["split"] = assign_splits(
            out,
            train_frac=train_frac,
            validate_frac=validate_frac,
            test_frac=test_frac,
        )
        split_policy = "time_ordered_60_20_20"

    out["calibration_version"] = calibration_version or "calibration_identity_v1"
    out["capacity_factor"] = float(calibration_factors.get("capacity_factor", 1.0))
    out["charge_loss_factor"] = float(calibration_factors.get("charge_loss_factor", 0.0))
    out["discharge_loss_factor"] = float(calibration_factors.get("discharge_loss_factor", 0.0))
    out["pv_correction_factor"] = pv_corr

    out = out.sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)

    trainable = int(out["is_trainable"].sum())
    if trainable == 0:
        raise ValueError(
            "training extract build failed: zero trainable rows produced "
            "(check MISSING_CLEAR_SKY gates)"
        )

    split_counts = out["split"].value_counts().to_dict()
    trainable_counts = out.loc[out["is_trainable"], "split"].value_counts().to_dict()

    content_hash = frame_content_hash(out, columns=list(DATASET_COLUMNS))
    root = _dataset_root()
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path = root / f"{ds_version}.parquet"
    out.to_parquet(path, index=False)

    manifest = {
        "dataset_version": ds_version,
        "feature_set_id": FEATURE_SET_ID,
        "schema_version": TRAINING_EXTRACT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "tenant_id": tenant_id,
        "site_id": site_id,
        "capsule_ids": list(capsule_ids),
        "calibration_version": calibration_version,
        "admissibility_versions": list(admissibility_versions),
        "slice_ids": list(slice_ids),
        "trainable_row_count": trainable,
        "total_row_count": int(len(out)),
        "content_hash": content_hash,
        "path": str(path),
        "time_start": str(out["timestamp_utc"].min()),
        "time_end": str(out["timestamp_utc"].max()),
        "split_policy": split_policy,
        "split_fractions": {
            "train": train_frac,
            "validate": validate_frac,
            "test": test_frac,
        },
        "split_row_counts": {k: int(v) for k, v in split_counts.items()},
        "split_boundaries": {
            str(k): {
                "min": str(v["min"]),
                "max": str(v["max"]),
                "count": int(v["count"]),
            }
            for k, v in out.groupby("split")["timestamp_utc"]
            .agg(["min", "max", "count"])
            .to_dict("index")
            .items()
        },
        "trainable_row_counts_by_split": {k: int(v) for k, v in trainable_counts.items()},
        "train_gate_code_counts": _gate_counts(out),
        "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
        "created_by": created_by,
        "site_ratings": {
            "rated_pv_w": getattr(ctx, "rated_pv_w", None),
            "usable_battery_capacity_wh": getattr(ctx, "usable_battery_capacity_wh", None),
            "timezone": getattr(ctx, "timezone", "UTC"),
            "fleet_id": getattr(ctx, "fleet_id", None),
        },
    }
    store.upsert(
        "datasets",
        {
            "dataset_version": ds_version,
            "tenant_id": tenant_id,
            "site_id": site_id,
            "feature_set_id": FEATURE_SET_ID,
            "capsule_ids": list(capsule_ids),
            "calibration_version": calibration_version,
            "admissibility_versions": list(admissibility_versions),
            "trainable_row_count": trainable,
            "total_row_count": int(len(out)),
            "content_hash": content_hash,
            "path": str(path),
            "time_start": manifest["time_start"],
            "time_end": manifest["time_end"],
            "split_json": {
                "policy": "time_ordered_60_20_20",
                "fractions": manifest["split_fractions"],
                "row_counts": manifest["split_row_counts"],
            },
            "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
            "created_by": created_by,
        },
    )
    store.audit(
        "training_extract.build",
        "dataset",
        ds_version,
        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        actor=created_by,
        payload={"content_hash": content_hash, "trainable_row_count": trainable},
    )
    return manifest


def _gate_counts(out: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, int]:
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for codes in out["train_gate_codes"]:
        for code in codes:
            counts[code] = counts.get(code, 0) + 1
    return dict(sorted(counts.items()))


def load_dataset(store: Store, dataset_version: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Load a materialized dataset by version."""
    row = store.query_one(
        "SELECT * FROM datasets WHERE dataset_version = ?", (dataset_version,)
    )
    if row is None:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown dataset {dataset_version}")
    return pd.read_parquet(row["path"])


def get_manifest(store: Store, dataset_version: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the manifest of a dataset version."""
    row = store.query_one(
        "SELECT * FROM datasets WHERE dataset_version = ?", (dataset_version,)
    )
    if row is None:
        raise KeyError(f"unknown dataset {dataset_version}")
    manifest = {
        "dataset_version": row["dataset_version"],
        "feature_set_id": row["feature_set_id"],
        "capsule_ids": row["capsule_ids"],
        "calibration_version": row["calibration_version"],
        "admissibility_versions": row["admissibility_versions"],
        "trainable_row_count": row["trainable_row_count"],
        "total_row_count": row["total_row_count"],
        "content_hash": row["content_hash"],
        "time_start": row["time_start"],
        "time_end": row["time_end"],
        "created_at_utc": row["created_at_utc"],
    }
    return manifest

bus-balance solver (the Tribunal's energy accounting)

"""Deterministic power-flow solver shared by the scenario generator.

This module encodes the *site physics* of the CHARIOT platform:

* PV generation (measured or modelled),
* site load (measured or declared),
* battery storage with charge/discharge efficiencies,
* grid import/export as the residual of the bus balance,
* state-of-charge as an energy-conserving state.

The solver is deliberately kept separate from the Reality Tribunal: the
Tribunal re-derives the energy balance **independently** in
``chariot.tribunal`` so that a bug in this solver cannot silently certify
itself.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import numpy as np


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BatterySpec:
    """Certified battery ratings and operating reserves."""

    usable_capacity_wh: float
    max_charge_w: float
    max_discharge_w: float
    charge_efficiency: float = 0.95
    discharge_efficiency: float = 0.95
    #: Operational reserve band the dispatch policy targets (percent).
    soc_floor_pct: float = 5.0
    soc_ceiling_pct: float = 95.0

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "usable_capacity_wh": self.usable_capacity_wh,
            "max_charge_w": self.max_charge_w,
            "max_discharge_w": self.max_discharge_w,
            "charge_efficiency": self.charge_efficiency,
            "discharge_efficiency": self.discharge_efficiency,
            "soc_floor_pct": self.soc_floor_pct,
            "soc_ceiling_pct": self.soc_ceiling_pct,
        }


def solve_flow(
    solar_w: np.ndarray,
    load_w: np.ndarray,
    soc_initial_pct: float,
    battery: BatterySpec,
    dt_h: float = 0.25,
) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    """Solve the bus balance for a trajectory.

    Dispatch policy (declared operating policy, v1):

    * surplus (``solar - load > 0``) charges the battery up to
      ``max_charge_w`` while the modelled SOC is below the reserve ceiling;
      the remainder is exported,
    * a deficit discharges the battery up to ``max_discharge_w`` while the
      modelled SOC is above the reserve floor; the remainder is imported.

    Grid limits are **not** enforced here: the grid flow is the residual of
    the balance and the Reality Tribunal checks it against the certified
    interconnection ratings.

    Sign conventions: ``battery_w`` is positive when discharging to the bus,
    ``grid_w`` is positive when importing from the grid.
    """
    solar = np.asarray(solar_w, dtype=float)
    load = np.asarray(load_w, dtype=float)
    n = solar.size
    battery_w = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    grid_w = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    soc = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    soc[0] = float(np.clip(soc_initial_pct, -1e9, 1e9))

    cap = float(battery.usable_capacity_wh)
    eta_c = float(battery.charge_efficiency)
    eta_d = float(battery.discharge_efficiency)
    floor = float(battery.soc_floor_pct)
    ceiling = float(battery.soc_ceiling_pct)

    for t in range(n):
        net = float(solar[t] - load[t])
        soc_prev = soc[t]
        if net >= 0.0:
            charge = min(net, battery.max_charge_w)
            if soc_prev >= ceiling:
                charge = 0.0
            else:
                # Do not charge past the certified ceiling.
                headroom_w = (ceiling - soc_prev) / 100.0 * cap / max(dt_h * eta_c, 1e-9)
                charge = min(charge, max(headroom_w, 0.0))
            battery_w[t] = -charge
            grid_w[t] = -(net - charge)  # export (negative)
        else:
            deficit = -net
            discharge = min(deficit, battery.max_discharge_w)
            if soc_prev <= floor:
                discharge = 0.0
            else:
                # Do not discharge past the certified floor.
                headroom_w = (soc_prev - floor) / 100.0 * cap * eta_d / max(dt_h, 1e-9)
                discharge = min(discharge, max(headroom_w, 0.0))
            battery_w[t] = discharge
            grid_w[t] = deficit - discharge  # import (positive)

        net_to_batt = float(solar[t] + grid_w[t] - load[t])
        if net_to_batt >= 0.0:
            delta_wh = net_to_batt * dt_h * eta_c
        else:
            delta_wh = net_to_batt * dt_h / eta_d
        soc[t + 1] = soc_prev + delta_wh / cap * 100.0 if cap > 0 else soc_prev

    return {
        "battery_w": battery_w,
        "grid_w": grid_w,
        "soc_percent": soc[1:],
        "soc_initial_percent": np.array([soc[0]]),
    }


def balance_residual_wh(
    solar_w: np.ndarray,
    load_w: np.ndarray,
    grid_w: np.ndarray,
    soc_start_pct: np.ndarray,
    soc_end_pct: np.ndarray,
    usable_capacity_wh: float,
    charge_efficiency: float,
    discharge_efficiency: float,
    dt_h: float = 0.25,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Per-step absolute energy-balance residual (Wh).

    ``residual_t = | ΔE_reported - ΔE_recomputed |`` where ``ΔE_recomputed``
    is derived from the reported bus flows.
    """
    solar = np.asarray(solar_w, dtype=float)
    load = np.asarray(load_w, dtype=float)
    grid = np.asarray(grid_w, dtype=float)
    soc_start = np.asarray(soc_start_pct, dtype=float)
    soc_end = np.asarray(soc_end_pct, dtype=float)

    delta_reported = (soc_end - soc_start) / 100.0 * float(usable_capacity_wh)
    net_to_batt = solar + grid - load
    delta_recomputed = np.where(
        net_to_batt >= 0.0,
        net_to_batt * dt_h * charge_efficiency,
        net_to_batt * dt_h / discharge_efficiency,
    )
    return np.abs(delta_reported - delta_recomputed)

==============================================================================

the OPERATIONAL NumPy clearness state-space model
file   : chariot-vscode/chariot/model.py
lines  : 1289
bytes  : 49111
