"""Scenario Genesis Engine — trajectory fan for the Tribunal (PDF §4.9).

Modes:

* ``simple_monte_carlo``    — latent-state simulation with fixed parameters,
* ``hybrid_hierarchical``    — hierarchical parameter draws (default),
* ``reference_replay``       — replay of a stored posterior reference path.

For ``s = 1..S`` (default 500-1000):

    draw the base path from the CHARIOT model
    if the residual chamber is active:   tau = base + w * r_hat
    else:                                 tau = base
    attach scenario_id + lineage

The **entire** batch is sent to the Tribunal — Genesis never pre-drops an
"ugly" scenario.  That is the whole point: the Tribunal is the only judge.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from chariot.hashing import hash_obj, short_hash
from chariot.model import ChariotModel, clear_sky_series, simulate
from chariot.physics import BatterySpec, solve_flow

GENESIS_MODES = ("simple_monte_carlo", "hybrid_hierarchical", "reference_replay")


@dataclass
class ScenarioBatch:
    """A generated scenario ensemble awaiting certification."""

    batch_id: str
    tenant_id: str
    site_id: str
    origin_utc: str
    horizon_steps: int
    dt_h: float
    n_scenarios: int
    model_version: str
    residual_version: str | None
    residual_weight: float
    dataset_version: str
    solar_w: np.ndarray
    load_w: np.ndarray
    battery_w: np.ndarray
    grid_w: np.ndarray
    soc_percent: np.ndarray
    soc_initial_percent: np.ndarray
    timestamps: list[str]
    scenario_ids: list[str]
    lineage: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    content_hash: str = ""
    extra: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "batch_id": self.batch_id,
            "tenant_id": self.tenant_id,
            "site_id": self.site_id,
            "origin_utc": self.origin_utc,
            "horizon_steps": self.horizon_steps,
            "dt_h": self.dt_h,
            "n_scenarios": self.n_scenarios,
            "model_version": self.model_version,
            "residual_version": self.residual_version,
            "residual_weight": self.residual_weight,
            "dataset_version": self.dataset_version,
            "content_hash": self.content_hash,
            "lineage": self.lineage,
        }


def _battery_from_context(ctx: Any, calibration: dict[str, float] | None = None) -> BatterySpec:
    """Build the certified battery spec from the site context + calibration.

    The *calibration version* is the authority for the loss factors: if the
    resolved calibration carries them, they override the site declaration so
    that the generator and the Tribunal solve the same energy balance.
    """
    meta = getattr(ctx, "metadata", {}) or {}
    calibration = calibration or {}
    capacity = getattr(ctx, "usable_battery_capacity_wh", None)
    if capacity is None:
        capacity = float(meta.get("usable_battery_capacity_wh", 0.0) or 0.0)
    eta_c = 1.0 - float(
        calibration.get("charge_loss_factor", meta.get("charge_loss_factor", 0.05)) or 0.0
    )
    eta_d = 1.0 - float(
        calibration.get("discharge_loss_factor", meta.get("discharge_loss_factor", 0.05)) or 0.0
    )
    max_charge = getattr(ctx, "max_charge_w", None)
    max_discharge = getattr(ctx, "max_discharge_w", None)
    if max_charge is None:
        max_charge = float(meta.get("max_charge_w", capacity) or capacity)
    if max_discharge is None:
        max_discharge = float(meta.get("max_discharge_w", capacity) or capacity)
    return BatterySpec(
        usable_capacity_wh=float(capacity),
        max_charge_w=float(max_charge),
        max_discharge_w=float(max_discharge),
        charge_efficiency=float(np.clip(eta_c, 0.5, 1.0)),
        discharge_efficiency=float(np.clip(eta_d, 0.5, 1.0)),
        soc_floor_pct=float(meta.get("soc_floor_pct", 5.0)),
        soc_ceiling_pct=float(meta.get("soc_ceiling_pct", 95.0)),
    )


def generate_batch(
    model: ChariotModel,
    site_id: str,
    future: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    tenant_id: str,
    n_scenarios: int = 500,
    seed: int = 7,
    ctx: Any,
    calibration: dict[str, float] | None = None,
    residual_version: str | None = None,
    residual_weight: float = 0.0,
    residual_artifact: Any = None,
    state: dict[str, float] | None = None,
    mode: str = "hybrid_hierarchical",
    dt_h: float = 0.25,
    paths: dict[str, np.ndarray] | None = None,
) -> ScenarioBatch:
    """Generate a scenario batch and solve the physics for every path.

    ``paths`` is the **adapter seam** for the offline PyMC reference: when it
    is supplied, the deterministic simulation is skipped and the supplied
    ``solar_w`` / ``load_w`` ensembles are used verbatim.  Everything
    downstream — the physics solver, the content hash, the lineage and the
    Tribunal — is identical, so a reference scenario is certified by exactly
    the same gate as an operational one.  ``None`` (the default) keeps the
    operational behaviour byte-for-byte.
    """
    if mode not in GENESIS_MODES:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown genesis mode {mode!r}; known: {GENESIS_MODES}")

    frame = future.sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)
    battery = _battery_from_context(ctx, calibration)
    meta = getattr(ctx, "metadata", {}) or {}
    declared_load_sigma = float(meta.get("load_relative_sigma", 0.0) or 0.0)

    if paths is None:
        sim = simulate(
            model,
            site_id,
            frame,
            n_scenarios=n_scenarios,
            seed=seed,
            state=state,
            load_profile=None,
            declared_load_sigma=declared_load_sigma,
            dt_h=dt_h,
        )
    else:
        sim = dict(paths)
        sim.setdefault("dt_h", np.array([dt_h]))
    solar = sim["solar_w"]
    load = sim["load_w"]

    # -- residual augmentation (bounded, never bypasses the Tribunal) ------
    if residual_artifact is not None and residual_weight > 0.0:
        from chariot.residual import build_features, propose_paths

        clear = clear_sky_series(frame)
        clear = np.nan_to_num(clear, nan=0.0)
        expected_ratio = solar / np.maximum(clear, 1.0)[None, :]
        ctx_meta = (getattr(ctx, "metadata", {}) or {}) if ctx is not None else {}
        last_solar = float((state or {}).get("last_solar", 0.0))
        last_load = float((state or {}).get("last_load", 0.0))
        features, _ = build_features(
            frame,
            mu_solar=solar,
            mu_load=load,
            clear_sky=clear,
            expected_ratio=expected_ratio,
            last_solar=last_solar,
            last_load=last_load,
            calibration=calibration or {},
        )
        corrections = np.asarray(propose_paths(residual_artifact, features), dtype=float)
        corrections = corrections.reshape(solar.shape)
        solar = np.maximum(solar + float(residual_weight) * corrections, 0.0)

    # -- deterministic physics for every path ------------------------------
    n, t_steps = solar.shape
    battery_w = np.zeros_like(solar)
    grid_w = np.zeros_like(solar)
    soc = np.zeros_like(solar)
    soc0 = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        flow = solve_flow(
            solar[i],
            load[i],
            soc_initial_pct=float(state.get("soc_percent", 50.0)) if state else 50.0,
            battery=battery,
            dt_h=dt_h,
        )
        battery_w[i] = flow["battery_w"]
        grid_w[i] = flow["grid_w"]
        soc[i] = flow["soc_percent"]
        soc0[i] = float(flow["soc_initial_percent"][0])

    timestamps = [str(ts) for ts in frame["timestamp_utc"]]
    lineage = {
        "model_version": model.model_version,
        "model_name": model.model_name,
        "dataset_version": model.dataset_version,
        "dataset_content_hash": model.dataset_content_hash,
        "residual_version": residual_version,
        "residual_weight": float(residual_weight),
        "calibration": calibration or {},
        "genesis_mode": mode,
        "site_context": ctx.to_dict() if hasattr(ctx, "to_dict") else {},
        "battery_spec": battery.to_dict(),
        "seed": seed,
    }
    content_hash = hash_obj(
        {
            "solar": np.round(solar, 3).tolist(),
            "load": np.round(load, 3).tolist(),
            "grid": np.round(grid_w, 3).tolist(),
            "soc": np.round(soc, 3).tolist(),
            "lineage": lineage,
        }
    )
    batch_id = f"batch_{short_hash(content_hash, 20)}"
    scenario_ids = [f"{batch_id}_s{i:04d}" for i in range(n)]

    return ScenarioBatch(
        batch_id=batch_id,
        tenant_id=tenant_id,
        site_id=site_id,
        origin_utc=timestamps[0] if timestamps else "",
        horizon_steps=int(t_steps),
        dt_h=dt_h,
        n_scenarios=int(n),
        model_version=model.model_version,
        residual_version=residual_version,
        residual_weight=float(residual_weight),
        dataset_version=model.dataset_version,
        solar_w=solar,
        load_w=load,
        battery_w=battery_w,
        grid_w=grid_w,
        soc_percent=soc,
        soc_initial_percent=soc0,
        timestamps=timestamps,
        scenario_ids=scenario_ids,
        lineage=lineage,
        content_hash=content_hash,
        extra={"sim": sim},
    )

Reality Tribunal + Rejection Echo Memory
file   : chariot-vscode/chariot/tribunal.py

"""Reality Tribunal + Rejection Echo Memory — fail-closed certification (PDF §4.10).

The Tribunal is the *sole energy-accounting authority* for retained futures.
Every retained scenario must pass strict physics validation at **every**
modelled timestep.  A single timestep failure rejects the entire scenario.

Tribunal checks (every timestep):

* energy-balance residual <= ``energy_balance_tol_wh`` (default 0.1 Wh),
* ``SOC_min <= SOC <= SOC_max``,
* power / equipment limits from the Site Master + policy,
* finite values; optional ramp limits.

The Rejection Echo Memory is an append-only record of every rejection.  Echo
durability is required for batch success: if the echo write fails, the batch
fails closed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

from chariot.config import TRIBUNAL_POLICY, TribunalPolicy
from chariot.genesis import ScenarioBatch
from chariot.hashing import hash_obj
from chariot.physics import balance_residual_wh
from chariot.store import Store, new_id, utc_now


@dataclass
class TribunalResult:
    """Outcome of validating one scenario batch."""

    batch_id: str
    n_proposed: int
    retained_idx: list[int]
    rejections: list[dict[str, Any]]
    survival_rate: float
    policy_version: str
    solver_version: str
    constraint_counts: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    batch_result: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def n_retained(self) -> int:
        return len(self.retained_idx)

    @property
    def n_rejected(self) -> int:
        return self.n_proposed - len(self.retained_idx)


def _certified_envelope(ctx: Any) -> dict[str, float | None]:
    meta = (getattr(ctx, "metadata", {}) or {}) if ctx is not None else {}
    return {
        "rated_pv_w": getattr(ctx, "rated_pv_w", None),
        "max_charge_w": getattr(ctx, "max_charge_w", None),
        "max_discharge_w": getattr(ctx, "max_discharge_w", None),
        "max_grid_import_w": meta.get("max_grid_import_w"),
        "max_grid_export_w": meta.get("max_grid_export_w"),
        "certified_ac_pv_w": meta.get("certified_ac_pv_w"),
        "solar_ramp_w_per_step": meta.get("solar_ramp_w_per_step"),
        "load_ramp_w_per_step": meta.get("load_ramp_w_per_step"),
        "usable_battery_capacity_wh": getattr(ctx, "usable_battery_capacity_wh", None),
    }


def validate_batch(
    batch: ScenarioBatch,
    *,
    ctx: Any,
    calibration: dict[str, float] | None = None,
    policy: TribunalPolicy = TRIBUNAL_POLICY,
    store: Store | None = None,
) -> TribunalResult:
    """Validate every scenario in the batch, fail-closed.

    When ``store`` is provided, every rejection is written to the Rejection
    Echo Memory and the batch result is persisted.  A failure to persist the
    echo raises — the system fails closed.
    """
    calibration = calibration or {}
    env = _certified_envelope(ctx)
    meta = (getattr(ctx, "metadata", {}) or {}) if ctx is not None else {}

    solar = np.asarray(batch.solar_w, dtype=float)
    load = np.asarray(batch.load_w, dtype=float)
    battery = np.asarray(batch.battery_w, dtype=float)
    grid = np.asarray(batch.grid_w, dtype=float)
    soc = np.asarray(batch.soc_percent, dtype=float)
    soc0 = np.asarray(batch.soc_initial_percent, dtype=float)

    n, t_steps = solar.shape
    dt_h = batch.dt_h

    capacity = float(env["usable_battery_capacity_wh"] or 0.0)
    capacity *= float(calibration.get("capacity_factor", 1.0))
    eta_c = float(np.clip(1.0 - float(calibration.get("charge_loss_factor", 0.0)), 0.5, 1.0))
    eta_d = float(np.clip(1.0 - float(calibration.get("discharge_loss_factor", 0.0)), 0.5, 1.0))

    # ---- per-check boolean masks (n, T) --------------------------------
    checks: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}

    finite = (
        np.isfinite(solar) & np.isfinite(load) & np.isfinite(grid) & np.isfinite(soc)
    )
    checks["NON_FINITE"] = ~finite
    checks["SOLAR_NEGATIVE"] = solar < -1e-6
    checks["LOAD_NEGATIVE"] = load < -1e-6

    rated = env["rated_pv_w"]
    if rated is not None:
        checks["SOLAR_OVERSCALE"] = solar > policy.solar_scale_kappa * float(rated)
    ac_rating = env["certified_ac_pv_w"]
    if ac_rating is not None:
        checks["SOLAR_ABOVE_CERTIFIED_AC"] = solar > float(ac_rating)

    checks["SOC_BOUNDS"] = (soc < policy.soc_min - 1e-9) | (soc > policy.soc_max + 1e-9)

    if capacity > 0:
        soc_start = np.concatenate([soc0[:, None], soc[:, :-1]], axis=1)
        residual = balance_residual_wh(
            solar, load, grid, soc_start, soc, capacity, eta_c, eta_d, dt_h=dt_h
        )
        checks["ENERGY_BALANCE"] = residual > policy.energy_balance_tol_wh
        max_balance_error = residual.max(axis=1)
    else:
        residual = np.zeros_like(solar)
        max_balance_error = np.zeros(n)
        checks["ENERGY_BALANCE"] = np.zeros_like(solar, dtype=bool)

    max_charge = env["max_charge_w"]
    max_discharge = env["max_discharge_w"]
    if max_charge is not None:
        checks["BATTERY_CHARGE_LIMIT"] = battery < -policy.power_limit_tolerance * float(max_charge) - 1e-6
    if max_discharge is not None:
        checks["BATTERY_DISCHARGE_LIMIT"] = battery > policy.power_limit_tolerance * float(max_discharge) + 1e-6

    imp_limit = env["max_grid_import_w"]
    if imp_limit is not None:
        checks["GRID_IMPORT_LIMIT"] = grid > float(imp_limit) + 1e-6
    exp_limit = env["max_grid_export_w"]
    if exp_limit is not None:
        checks["GRID_EXPORT_LIMIT"] = grid < -float(exp_limit) - 1e-6

    if policy.solar_ramp_w_per_step is not None:
        d_solar = np.abs(np.diff(solar, axis=1, prepend=solar[:, :1]))
        checks["SOLAR_RAMP_LIMIT"] = d_solar > float(policy.solar_ramp_w_per_step) + 1e-6
    else:
        site_ramp = env["solar_ramp_w_per_step"]
        if site_ramp is not None:
            d_solar = np.abs(np.diff(solar, axis=1, prepend=solar[:, :1]))
            checks["SOLAR_RAMP_LIMIT"] = d_solar > float(site_ramp) + 1e-6
    if policy.load_ramp_w_per_step is not None:
        d_load = np.abs(np.diff(load, axis=1, prepend=load[:, :1]))
        checks["LOAD_RAMP_LIMIT"] = d_load > float(policy.load_ramp_w_per_step) + 1e-6

    # ---- fail-closed per-scenario judgement ----------------------------
    code_names = list(checks.keys())
    combined = np.zeros((n, t_steps), dtype=bool)
    for mask in checks.values():
        combined |= mask

    retained_idx: list[int] = []
    rejections: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    constraint_counts: dict[str, int] = {}

    for i in range(n):
        failing = np.where(combined[i])[0]
        if failing.size == 0:
            retained_idx.append(i)
            continue
        failed_timestep = int(failing[0])
        codes = [name for name in code_names if bool(checks[name][i, failed_timestep])]
        for name in code_names:
            if bool(checks[name][i, failed_timestep]):
                constraint_counts[name] = constraint_counts.get(name, 0) + 1
        magnitude = _violation_magnitude(checks, i, failed_timestep, policy, residual)
        rejection = {
            "scenario_id": batch.scenario_ids[i],
            "scenario_index": i,
            "failed_timestep": failed_timestep,
            "constraint_codes": codes,
            "max_balance_error_wh": float(max_balance_error[i]),
            "violation_magnitude": float(magnitude),
            "scenario_content_hash": hash_obj(
                {
                    "solar": np.round(solar[i], 3).tolist(),
                    "load": np.round(load[i], 3).tolist(),
                    "grid": np.round(grid[i], 3).tolist(),
                    "soc": np.round(soc[i], 3).tolist(),
                }
            ),
        }
        rejections.append(rejection)

    survival = (len(retained_idx) / n) if n else 0.0
    result = TribunalResult(
        batch_id=batch.batch_id,
        n_proposed=n,
        retained_idx=retained_idx,
        rejections=rejections,
        survival_rate=survival,
        policy_version=policy.policy_version,
        solver_version=policy.solver_version,
        constraint_counts=constraint_counts,
    )

    if store is not None:
        _persist(store, batch, result, ctx)

    result.batch_result = {
        "batch_id": batch.batch_id,
        "n_proposed": n,
        "n_retained": result.n_retained,
        "n_rejected": result.n_rejected,
        "survival_rate": survival,
        "retained_scenario_ids": [batch.scenario_ids[i] for i in retained_idx],
        "tribunal_policy_version": policy.policy_version,
        "constraint_counts": constraint_counts,
    }
    return result


def _violation_magnitude(
    checks: dict[str, np.ndarray],
    i: int,
    t: int,
    policy: TribunalPolicy,
    residual: np.ndarray,
) -> float:
    """How badly the scenario violated the constraint (worst margin)."""
    worst = 0.0
    if bool(checks["ENERGY_BALANCE"][i, t]):
        worst = max(worst, float(residual[i, t]))
    if bool(checks["SOC_BOUNDS"][i, t]):
        worst = max(worst, 1.0)
    for name in (
        "SOLAR_OVERSCALE",
        "SOLAR_ABOVE_CERTIFIED_AC",
        "BATTERY_CHARGE_LIMIT",
        "BATTERY_DISCHARGE_LIMIT",
        "GRID_IMPORT_LIMIT",
        "GRID_EXPORT_LIMIT",
        "SOLAR_RAMP_LIMIT",
        "LOAD_RAMP_LIMIT",
    ):
        if name in checks and bool(checks[name][i, t]):
            worst = max(worst, 1.0)
    return worst


def _persist(store: Store, batch: ScenarioBatch, result: TribunalResult, ctx: Any) -> None:
    """Write the batch result and every rejection into the Echo Memory."""
    try:
        for rejection in result.rejections:
            row = {
                "rejection_id": new_id("rej"),
                "batch_id": batch.batch_id,
                "scenario_id": rejection["scenario_id"],
                "tenant_id": batch.tenant_id,
                "site_id": batch.site_id,
                "chariot_model_version": batch.model_version,
                "residual_version": batch.residual_version,
                "residual_weight_w": float(batch.residual_weight),
                "solver_version": result.solver_version,
                "tribunal_policy_version": result.policy_version,
                "failed_timestep": rejection["failed_timestep"],
                "constraint_codes": json.dumps(rejection["constraint_codes"]),
                "max_balance_error_wh": rejection["max_balance_error_wh"],
                "violation_magnitude": rejection["violation_magnitude"],
                "exception_fingerprint": hash_obj(
                    {
                        "codes": rejection["constraint_codes"],
                        "timestep": rejection["failed_timestep"],
                    }
                ),
                "lineage_json": json.dumps(batch.lineage, sort_keys=True, default=str),
                "scenario_content_hash": rejection["scenario_content_hash"],
                "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
            }
            store.insert("tribunal_rejections", row)
        store.upsert(
            "tribunal_batches",
            {
                "batch_id": batch.batch_id,
                "n_proposed": result.n_proposed,
                "n_retained": result.n_retained,
                "n_rejected": result.n_rejected,
                "survival_rate": result.survival_rate,
                "retained_scenario_ids": json.dumps(
                    [batch.scenario_ids[i] for i in result.retained_idx]
                ),
                "rejection_ids": json.dumps(
                    [r["scenario_id"] for r in result.rejections]
                ),
                "tribunal_policy_version": result.policy_version,
                "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
            },
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - durability guard
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Rejection Echo Memory write failed; the batch fails closed"
        ) from exc

Residual Stability Governor (sole runtime authority for w)

"""Residual Stability Governor — the sole runtime authority for ``w`` (PDF §4.12).

Signals monitored:

    c   = E[|r_hat|] / E[|y_hat|]
    rho = corr(|r_hat|, reject_indicator)
    per-regime reject rates
    residual-on vs. residual-off survival delta

State machine: ``active`` <-> ``attenuated`` <-> ``quarantined``.

Genesis always reads ``get_effective_w()``.  The Dual Feedback Cortex cannot
bypass the Governor to manipulate production weights.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

from chariot.config import GOVERNOR_POLICY, RESIDUAL_POLICY, GovernorPolicy
from chariot.store import Store, new_id, utc_now

STATE_ACTIVE = "active"
STATE_ATTENUATED = "attenuated"
STATE_QUARANTINED = "quarantined"


@dataclass
class GovernorState:
    """Current runtime state of the Residual Stability Governor."""

    state: str = STATE_ACTIVE
    w: float = 0.0
    w_max: float = RESIDUAL_POLICY.w_max
    breaches: int = 0
    healthy_streak: int = 0
    history: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "state": self.state,
            "w": self.w,
            "w_max": self.w_max,
            "breaches": self.breaches,
            "healthy_streak": self.healthy_streak,
            "policy_version": GOVERNOR_POLICY.policy_version,
            "history": list(self.history[-20:]),
        }


class ResidualStabilityGovernor:
    """Runtime authority for the effective residual weight."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        store: Store | None = None,
        policy: GovernorPolicy = GOVERNOR_POLICY,
        w_max: float | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.store = store
        self.policy = policy
        self.state = GovernorState(w_max=w_max or policy.w_max)
        if store is not None:
            self._load_events()

    # -- persistence --------------------------------------------------------
    def _load_events(self) -> None:
        rows = self.store.query(
            "SELECT * FROM governor_events ORDER BY created_at_utc DESC LIMIT 50"
        )
        if not rows:
            return
        latest = rows[0]
        self.state.state = latest["state"]
        self.state.w = float(latest["w"])

    def _record(self, batch_id: str, action: str, signals: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        event = {
            "event_id": new_id("gov"),
            "batch_id": batch_id,
            "previous_state": self.state.state,
            "state": self.state.state,
            "w": float(self.state.w),
            "c": float(signals.get("c", 0.0)),
            "rho": float(signals.get("rho", 0.0)),
            "signals_json": _dumps(signals),
            "action": action,
            "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
        }
        self.state.history.append(event)
        if self.store is not None:
            self.store.insert("governor_events", event)

    # -- API ----------------------------------------------------------------
    def get_effective_w(self) -> float:
        """The weight Genesis is allowed to use.  Quarantine forces ``w = 0``."""
        if self.state.state == STATE_QUARANTINED:
            return 0.0
        return float(np.clip(self.state.w, 0.0, self.state.w_max))

    def status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            **self.state.to_dict(),
            "effective_w": self.get_effective_w(),
        }

    def set_weight(self, w: float, actor: str = "system") -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Audited force-set of the residual weight."""
        w = float(np.clip(w, 0.0, self.state.w_max))
        self.state.w = w
        if w > 0 and self.state.state == STATE_QUARANTINED:
            self.state.state = STATE_ACTIVE
        self.state.healthy_streak = 0
        self._record("manual", "force_set_weight", {"w": w, "actor": actor})
        return self.status()

    def quarantine(self, actor: str = "system", reason: str = "manual") -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Quarantine the residual chamber (``w = 0``)."""
        previous = self.state.state
        self.state.state = STATE_QUARANTINED
        self.state.w = 0.0
        self.state.breaches = self.policy.breach_threshold
        self._record("manual", "quarantine", {"actor": actor, "reason": reason, "previous": previous})
        return self.status()

    def release(self, actor: str = "system", reason: str = "manual") -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Release from quarantine back to an attenuated state."""
        previous = self.state.state
        self.state.state = STATE_ATTENUATED
        self.state.healthy_streak = 0
        self._record("manual", "release", {"actor": actor, "reason": reason, "previous": previous})
        return self.status()

    def evaluate(
        self,
        signals: dict[str, Any],
        *,
        batch_id: str = "manual",
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Evaluate the governor signals and move the state machine."""
        c = float(signals.get("c", 0.0))
        rho = float(signals.get("rho", 0.0))
        survival_delta = float(signals.get("survival_delta", 0.0))
        survival = float(signals.get("survival_rate", 1.0))
        regime_rejects = signals.get("regime_reject_rates", {}) or {}

        breach_reasons: list[str] = []
        if c > self.policy.c_max:
            breach_reasons.append(f"contribution ratio c={c:.3f} > c_max {self.policy.c_max:.2f}")
        if rho > self.policy.rho_max:
            breach_reasons.append(f"reject correlation rho={rho:.3f} > rho_max {self.policy.rho_max:.2f}")
        if survival < self.policy.survival_floor:
            breach_reasons.append(
                f"survival {survival:.3f} < floor {self.policy.survival_floor:.2f}"
            )
        if survival_delta < self.policy.survival_delta_min:
            breach_reasons.append(
                f"survival delta {survival_delta:.3f} < {self.policy.survival_delta_min:.2f}"
            )
        if regime_rejects and max(regime_rejects.values()) > 0.25:
            worst = max(regime_rejects, key=lambda k: regime_rejects[k])
            breach_reasons.append(f"regime {worst} reject rate {regime_rejects[worst]:.3f} > 0.25")

        previous = self.state.state
        action = "hold"

        if breach_reasons:
            self.state.breaches += 1
            self.state.healthy_streak = 0
            if self.state.breaches >= self.policy.breach_threshold:
                self.state.state = STATE_QUARANTINED
                self.state.w = 0.0
                action = "quarantine"
            else:
                self.state.state = STATE_ATTENUATED
                self.state.w = float(np.clip(self.state.w * self.policy.beta, 0.0, self.state.w_max))
                action = "attenuate"
        else:
            self.state.healthy_streak += 1
            self.state.breaches = 0
            if self.state.state == STATE_QUARANTINED:
                action = "hold_quarantined"
            elif self.state.healthy_streak >= self.policy.recovery_threshold:
                self.state.state = STATE_ACTIVE
                self.state.w = float(np.clip(self.state.w, 0.0, self.state.w_max))
                action = "restore"
            else:
                action = "healthy"

        payload = {
            "c": c,
            "rho": rho,
            "survival_rate": survival,
            "survival_delta": survival_delta,
            "regime_reject_rates": regime_rejects,
            "breach_reasons": breach_reasons,
            "previous_state": previous,
            "action": action,
        }
        self._record(batch_id, action, payload)
        return {**self.status(), "evaluation": payload}


def governor_signals(
    residual_magnitude: np.ndarray,
    target_magnitude: np.ndarray,
    reject_indicator: np.ndarray | None = None,
    *,
    survival_rate: float = 1.0,
    survival_delta: float = 0.0,
    regime_reject_rates: dict[str, float] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Compute the governor's monitored signals."""
    residual_magnitude = np.asarray(residual_magnitude, dtype=float)
    target_magnitude = np.asarray(target_magnitude, dtype=float)
    c = float(
        np.mean(np.abs(residual_magnitude))
        / max(np.mean(np.abs(target_magnitude)), 1e-9)
    )
    rho = 0.0
    if reject_indicator is not None and len(reject_indicator) == len(residual_magnitude):
        r = np.asarray(reject_indicator, dtype=float)
        if np.std(r) > 0 and np.std(residual_magnitude) > 0:
            rho = float(np.corrcoef(np.abs(residual_magnitude), r)[0, 1])
    return {
        "c": c,
        "rho": rho,
        "survival_rate": float(survival_rate),
        "survival_delta": float(survival_delta),
        "regime_reject_rates": regime_reject_rates or {},
    }


def _dumps(payload: Any) -> str:
    import json

    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, default=str)

Dual Feedback Cortex
file   : chariot-vscode/chariot/feedback.py

"""Dual Feedback Cortex — structured learning signals from the Echo memory.

PDF §4.11.

Path A — CHARIOT model:

* sample re-weighting near recurrent failures,
* physics-informed penalty terms,
* dataset window proposals,
* lands as new train jobs -> new ``model_version`` (no silent in-place hack).

Path B — Residual:

* quarantine requests to the Governor,
* healthy -> maintain ``w``,
* breach -> attenuate ``w <- beta * w``,
* severe / persistent instability -> quarantine (``w = 0``),
* recovery -> gradual restore under policy,
* human -> audited force-set / quarantine / release.

Promotion discipline: new versions are only deployed if the held-out survival
does not degrade, the residual ``w`` stays within the ceiling, all diagnostics
pass and the lineage remains complete.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from chariot.governor import ResidualStabilityGovernor, governor_signals
from chariot.store import Store, new_id, utc_now


@dataclass
class FeedbackJob:
    """A structured learning signal produced by the cortex."""

    job_id: str
    batch_id: str
    path: str  # "model" | "residual"
    payload: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at_utc: str = ""

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "job_id": self.job_id,
            "batch_id": self.batch_id,
            "path": self.path,
            "payload": self.payload,
            "created_at_utc": self.created_at_utc,
        }


def analyse_rejections(
    store: Store, batch_id: str, limit: int = 500
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Summarise the Rejection Echo Memory for one batch."""
    rows = store.rejections_for_batch(batch_id)[:limit]
    code_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    timestep_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    for row in rows:
        import json

        for code in json.loads(row["constraint_codes"] or "[]"):
            code_counts[code] = code_counts.get(code, 0) + 1
        key = str(row["failed_timestep"])
        timestep_counts[key] = timestep_counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    return {
        "n_rejections": len(rows),
        "constraint_code_counts": dict(sorted(code_counts.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1])),
        "failed_timestep_counts": dict(
            sorted(timestep_counts.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1])[:20]
        ),
        "max_balance_error_wh": max((row["max_balance_error_wh"] for row in rows), default=0.0),
    }


def run_cortex(
    store: Store,
    *,
    batch_id: str,
    governor: ResidualStabilityGovernor,
    survival_rate: float,
    survival_delta: float = 0.0,
    residual_magnitude: list[float] | None = None,
    target_magnitude: list[float] | None = None,
    reject_indicator: list[float] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Run the Dual Feedback Cortex over one batch.

    Produces a *model* learning job and a *residual* learning job, and lets
    the Governor act on the residual signals.
    """
    rejection_summary = analyse_rejections(store, batch_id)

    # -- Path A: CHARIOT model learning signals ----------------------------
    model_job = FeedbackJob(
        job_id=new_id("fbjob"),
        batch_id=batch_id,
        path="model",
        payload={
            "recommendation": "retrain_with_reweighting",
            "survival_rate": survival_rate,
            "rejection_summary": rejection_summary,
            "sample_reweighting": _reweighting(rejection_summary),
            "penalty_terms": _penalties(rejection_summary),
            "dataset_window_proposal": _window_proposal(rejection_summary),
            "promotion_discipline": {
                "held_out_survival_must_not_degrade": True,
                "lineage_must_be_complete": True,
                "diagnostics_must_pass": True,
            },
        },
        created_at_utc=utc_now(),
    )
    store.insert(
        "feedback_jobs",
        {
            "job_id": model_job.job_id,
            "batch_id": batch_id,
            "path": model_job.path,
            "payload_json": _dumps(model_job.payload),
            "created_at_utc": model_job.created_at_utc,
        },
    )

    # -- Path B: residual learning signals + Governor action ----------------
    if residual_magnitude and target_magnitude:
        signals = governor_signals(
            np.asarray(residual_magnitude, dtype=float),
            np.asarray(target_magnitude, dtype=float),
            np.asarray(reject_indicator, dtype=float) if reject_indicator else None,
            survival_rate=survival_rate,
            survival_delta=survival_delta,
            regime_reject_rates=_regime_rates(rejection_summary),
        )
        governor_result = governor.evaluate(signals, batch_id=batch_id)
    else:
        signals = {}
        governor_result = governor.status()

    residual_job = FeedbackJob(
        job_id=new_id("fbjob"),
        batch_id=batch_id,
        path="residual",
        payload={
            "signals": signals,
            "governor": governor_result,
            "action_taken": governor_result.get("evaluation", {}).get("action", "hold"),
            "recommendation": _residual_recommendation(governor_result),
        },
        created_at_utc=utc_now(),
    )
    store.insert(
        "feedback_jobs",
        {
            "job_id": residual_job.job_id,
            "batch_id": batch_id,
            "path": residual_job.path,
            "payload_json": _dumps(residual_job.payload),
            "created_at_utc": residual_job.created_at_utc,
        },
    )
    store.audit(
        "feedback.cortex.run",
        "batch",
        batch_id,
        payload={"jobs": [model_job.job_id, residual_job.job_id]},
    )
    return {
        "batch_id": batch_id,
        "model_job": model_job.to_dict(),
        "residual_job": residual_job.to_dict(),
        "governor": governor_result,
        "rejection_summary": rejection_summary,
    }


def _reweighting(summary: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Sample re-weighting factors derived from recurrent failure modes."""
    total = max(summary.get("n_rejections", 0), 1)
    counts = summary.get("constraint_code_counts", {})
    weights = {}
    for code, count in counts.items():
        weights[code] = float(1.0 + count / total)
    return weights


def _penalties(summary: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    """Physics-informed penalty terms to add to the model objective."""
    penalties: list[str] = []
    counts = summary.get("constraint_code_counts", {})
    if counts.get("ENERGY_BALANCE"):
        penalties.append("penalise_energy_balance_residual")
    if counts.get("SOC_BOUNDS"):
        penalties.append("penalise_soc_envelope_excursion")
    if counts.get("SOLAR_OVERSCALE") or counts.get("SOLAR_ABOVE_CERTIFIED_AC"):
        penalties.append("penalise_pv_scale_overrun")
    if counts.get("GRID_IMPORT_LIMIT") or counts.get("GRID_EXPORT_LIMIT"):
        penalties.append("penalise_interconnection_overrun")
    if counts.get("SOLAR_RAMP_LIMIT"):
        penalties.append("penalise_ramp_overrun")
    return penalties


def _window_proposal(summary: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Propose a dataset window that emphasises the failure region."""
    counts = summary.get("failed_timestep_counts", {})
    if not counts:
        return {"action": "keep_window"}
    worst = max(counts, key=lambda k: counts[k])
    return {
        "action": "propose_window",
        "hotspot_index": int(worst),
        "rejections_at_hotspot": counts[worst],
        "rationale": "concentrate training samples near the recurrent failure timestep",
    }


def _regime_rates(summary: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    counts = summary.get("constraint_code_counts", {})
    total = max(summary.get("n_rejections", 0), 1)
    return {code: float(count / total) for code, count in counts.items()}


def _residual_recommendation(governor_result: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    action = governor_result.get("evaluation", {}).get("action", "hold")
    return {
        "attenuate": "residual_attenuated_by_governor",
        "quarantine": "residual_quarantined_by_governor",
        "restore": "residual_weight_restored",
        "healthy": "residual_weight_maintained",
        "hold_quarantined": "residual_stays_quarantined",
        "hold": "residual_weight_maintained",
    }.get(action, "residual_weight_maintained")


def _dumps(payload: Any) -> str:
    import json

    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, default=str)

Physics-Survival Confidence Engine
file   : chariot-vscode/chariot/confidence.py
lines  : 197
bytes  : 6572

"""Physics-Survival Confidence Engine (PDF §4.13).

    s = N_retained / N_proposed
    u = aggregated normalized P90-P10 width (RETAINED scenarios only)
    C = g(1 - u) * s            (default g = identity)

Status policy (versioned thresholds):

* ``s < s_min``                        -> ``abstained``
* ``C < C_low``                        -> ``reduced_confidence``
* ``u`` high with ``s < 1``            -> ``reduced_confidence``
* otherwise                            -> ``eligible_for_human_review``

Survival dominates sharpness: the residual may not inflate confidence while
survival ``s`` drops.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

from chariot.config import CONFIDENCE_POLICY, ConfidencePolicy
from chariot.genesis import ScenarioBatch
from chariot.store import Store, new_id, utc_now

STATUS_ELIGIBLE = "eligible_for_human_review"
STATUS_REDUCED = "reduced_confidence"
STATUS_ABSTAINED = "abstained"


@dataclass
class ConfidenceReport:
    """Joint statistical-uncertainty / physics-survival confidence report."""

    report_id: str
    batch_id: str
    status: str
    survival_rate: float
    uncertainty: float
    confidence: float
    n_proposed: int
    n_retained: int
    n_rejected: int
    policy_version: str
    reasons: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    regime: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    residual_context: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    signal_widths: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "report_id": self.report_id,
            "batch_id": self.batch_id,
            "status": self.status,
            "survival_rate": self.survival_rate,
            "uncertainty": self.uncertainty,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "n_proposed": self.n_proposed,
            "n_retained": self.n_retained,
            "n_rejected": self.n_rejected,
            "policy_version": self.policy_version,
            "reasons": list(self.reasons),
            "regime": dict(self.regime),
            "residual_context": dict(self.residual_context),
            "signal_widths": dict(self.signal_widths),
        }


def normalized_width(p10: np.ndarray, p50: np.ndarray, p90: np.ndarray, floor: float) -> float:
    """Aggregate the P90-P10 band, normalized by the signal magnitude."""
    p10 = np.asarray(p10, dtype=float)
    p50 = np.asarray(p50, dtype=float)
    p90 = np.asarray(p90, dtype=float)
    if p10.size == 0:
        return 1.0
    width = float(np.mean(p90 - p10))
    scale = float(np.mean(np.abs(p50))) + floor
    return float(width / max(scale, floor))


def evaluate(
    batch: ScenarioBatch,
    retained_idx: list[int],
    *,
    survival_rate: float,
    policy: ConfidencePolicy = CONFIDENCE_POLICY,
    residual_context: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    store: Store | None = None,
) -> ConfidenceReport:
    """Evaluate the joint confidence metric for a certified batch."""
    idx = np.asarray(retained_idx, dtype=int)
    reasons: list[str] = []
    widths: dict[str, float] = {}

    if idx.size == 0:
        u = 1.0
        widths = {"solar_w": 1.0, "load_w": 1.0, "soc_percent": 1.0}
    else:
        u_parts: list[float] = []
        for name, arr, floor in (
            ("solar_w", batch.solar_w, 1000.0),
            ("load_w", batch.load_w, 500.0),
            ("soc_percent", batch.soc_percent, 1.0),
        ):
            values = np.asarray(arr[idx], dtype=float)
            p10 = np.quantile(values, 0.10, axis=0)
            p50 = np.quantile(values, 0.50, axis=0)
            p90 = np.quantile(values, 0.90, axis=0)
            width = normalized_width(p10, p50, p90, floor)
            widths[name] = float(width)
            u_parts.append(width)
        u = float(np.mean(u_parts))

    u = float(np.clip(u, 0.0, 10.0))
    if policy.sharpness == "identity":
        c = (1.0 - u) * survival_rate
    else:
        c = survival_rate
    c = float(np.clip(c, 0.0, 1.0))

    if survival_rate < policy.s_min:
        status = STATUS_ABSTAINED
        reasons.append(
            f"survival_rate {survival_rate:.3f} < s_min {policy.s_min:.2f}: physics survival too low"
        )
    elif c < policy.c_low:
        status = STATUS_REDUCED
        reasons.append(f"confidence {c:.3f} < C_low {policy.c_low:.2f}")
    elif u > policy.u_high and survival_rate < 1.0:
        status = STATUS_REDUCED
        reasons.append(
            f"uncertainty {u:.3f} > u_high {policy.u_high:.2f} with survival {survival_rate:.3f} < 1"
        )
    else:
        status = STATUS_ELIGIBLE

    report = ConfidenceReport(
        report_id=new_id("conf"),
        batch_id=batch.batch_id,
        status=status,
        survival_rate=float(survival_rate),
        uncertainty=float(u),
        confidence=float(c),
        n_proposed=batch.n_scenarios,
        n_retained=int(idx.size),
        n_rejected=int(batch.n_scenarios - idx.size),
        policy_version=policy.policy_version,
        reasons=reasons,
        regime=_regime_report(batch, idx),
        residual_context=residual_context or {},
        signal_widths=widths,
    )
    if store is not None:
        store.insert(
            "confidence_reports",
            {
                "report_id": report.report_id,
                "batch_id": batch.batch_id,
                "status": report.status,
                "survival_rate": report.survival_rate,
                "uncertainty": report.uncertainty,
                "confidence": report.confidence,
                "policy_version": report.policy_version,
                "report_json": _dumps(report.to_dict()),
                "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
            },
        )
    return report


def _regime_report(batch: ScenarioBatch, idx: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Regime-wise survival and confidence (daylight / night)."""
    if idx.size == 0:
        return {}
    clear = batch.extra.get("clear_sky") if batch.extra else None
    out: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if clear is None:
        return out
    daylight = np.asarray(clear, dtype=float) > 1.0
    for name, mask in (("daylight", daylight), ("night", ~daylight)):
        if not mask.any():
            continue
        out[name] = {
            "n_steps": int(mask.sum()),
            "retained_fraction": float(np.mean(idx < len(idx))),
        }
    return out


def _dumps(payload: Any) -> str:
    import json

    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, default=str)

role modes, quantiles, events, abstention
file   : chariot-vscode/chariot/advisory.py

"""Advisory API — multi-horizon, multi-role, advisory-only publication (PDF §4.14).

Role modes:

* ``research``       — short horizon, full transparency, lineage, residual context,
* ``grid_engineer``  — operational horizon, risk events, actionable confidence,
* ``investor``       — long horizon, planning aggregates, site comparison context.

Every response carries ``advisory_only: true``, the confidence status, and the
full lineage (batch, model, residual, dataset, capsules, policies).

Abstain behaviour: empty series, explicit reasons, HTTP 200 with an abstained
payload.  A P50 is **never** fabricated.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Sequence

import numpy as np

from chariot.config import (
    HORIZON_HOURS,
    HORIZONS,
    ROLE_DEFAULT_HORIZON,
    ROLE_MODES,
)
from chariot.confidence import ConfidenceReport
from chariot.genesis import ScenarioBatch

ADVISORY_ONLY = True


def _bands(values: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, list[float]]:
    """P10/P50/P90 bands from the RETAINED scenarios only."""
    if values.size == 0:
        return {"p10": [], "p50": [], "p90": []}
    return {
        "p10": [round(float(v), 3) for v in np.quantile(values, 0.10, axis=0)],
        "p50": [round(float(v), 3) for v in np.quantile(values, 0.50, axis=0)],
        "p90": [round(float(v), 3) for v in np.quantile(values, 0.90, axis=0)],
    }


def _event_probability(values: np.ndarray, predicate) -> float:
    if values.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.mean(predicate(values)))


def build_events(
    batch: ScenarioBatch,
    retained_idx: Sequence[int],
    *,
    ctx: Any = None,
    horizon_steps: int | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Empirical event probabilities computed on the retained set."""
    idx = np.asarray(list(retained_idx), dtype=int)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return {
            "p_soc_below_20_within_horizon": 0.0,
            "p_soc_above_90_within_horizon": 0.0,
            "p_grid_import_limit_exceeded": 0.0,
            "p_grid_export_limit_exceeded": 0.0,
            "p_pv_above_certified_ac": 0.0,
            "n_retained": 0,
        }
    steps = horizon_steps or batch.horizon_steps
    soc = np.asarray(batch.soc_percent[idx], dtype=float)[:, :steps]
    grid = np.asarray(batch.grid_w[idx], dtype=float)[:, :steps]
    solar = np.asarray(batch.solar_w[idx], dtype=float)[:, :steps]

    meta = (getattr(ctx, "metadata", {}) or {}) if ctx is not None else {}
    imp_limit = meta.get("max_grid_import_w")
    exp_limit = meta.get("max_grid_export_w")
    ac_rating = meta.get("certified_ac_pv_w")

    events: dict[str, Any] = {
        "p_soc_below_20_within_horizon": _event_probability(soc, lambda v: np.min(v, axis=1) < 20.0),
        "p_soc_above_90_within_horizon": _event_probability(soc, lambda v: np.max(v, axis=1) > 90.0),
        "n_retained": int(idx.size),
        "horizon_steps": int(steps),
    }
    if imp_limit is not None:
        events["p_grid_import_limit_exceeded"] = _event_probability(
            grid, lambda v: np.max(v, axis=1) > float(imp_limit)
        )
        events["grid_import_limit_w"] = float(imp_limit)
    if exp_limit is not None:
        events["p_grid_export_limit_exceeded"] = _event_probability(
            grid, lambda v: np.min(v, axis=1) < -float(exp_limit)
        )
        events["grid_export_limit_w"] = float(exp_limit)
    if ac_rating is not None:
        events["p_pv_above_certified_ac"] = _event_probability(
            solar, lambda v: np.max(v, axis=1) > float(ac_rating)
        )
        events["certified_ac_pv_w"] = float(ac_rating)
    return events


def build_forecast(
    batch: ScenarioBatch,
    retained_idx: Sequence[int],
    report: ConfidenceReport,
    *,
    role_mode: str,
    horizon: str | None = None,
    lineage: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ctx: Any = None,
    horizon_steps: int | None = None,
    baselines: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    rejection_sample: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build one role-mode advisory payload."""
    if role_mode not in ROLE_MODES:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown role mode {role_mode!r}; known: {list(ROLE_MODES)}")

    horizon = horizon or ROLE_DEFAULT_HORIZON[role_mode]
    idx = np.asarray(list(retained_idx), dtype=int)
    certified_steps = batch.horizon_steps
    requested = horizon_steps or HORIZONS.get(horizon, certified_steps)
    if requested > certified_steps:
        requested = certified_steps
    steps = int(min(requested, certified_steps))

    common: dict[str, Any] = {
        "advisory_only": ADVISORY_ONLY,
        "not_a_controller": True,
        "role_mode": role_mode,
        "horizon": horizon,
        "horizon_hours": HORIZON_HOURS.get(horizon),
        "horizon_steps": steps,
        "certified_trajectory_steps": certified_steps,
        "status": report.status,
        "confidence": {
            "C": round(float(report.confidence), 4),
            "s_survival": round(float(report.survival_rate), 4),
            "u_uncertainty": round(float(report.uncertainty), 4),
            "policy_version": report.policy_version,
            "reasons": list(report.reasons),
        },
        "survival_rate": round(float(report.survival_rate), 4),
        "n_proposed": report.n_proposed,
        "n_retained": report.n_retained,
        "n_rejected": report.n_rejected,
        "lineage": lineage or {},
        "timestamps": batch.timestamps[:steps],
    }

    if report.status == "abstained" or idx.size == 0:
        abstain = {
            **common,
            "abstained": True,
            "message": (
                "Advisory abstained: physics survival too low. "
                "No certified quantiles are published."
            ),
            "solar_w": {"p10": [], "p50": [], "p90": []},
            "load_w": {"p10": [], "p50": [], "p90": []},
            "soc_percent": {"p10": [], "p50": [], "p90": []},
            "grid_w": {"p10": [], "p50": [], "p90": []},
            "events": {},
        }
        if role_mode == "research":
            abstain["rejection_sample"] = (rejection_sample or [])[:12]
            abstain["baselines"] = baselines or []
        return abstain

    solar = np.asarray(batch.solar_w[idx], dtype=float)[:, :steps]
    load = np.asarray(batch.load_w[idx], dtype=float)[:, :steps]
    soc = np.asarray(batch.soc_percent[idx], dtype=float)[:, :steps]
    grid = np.asarray(batch.grid_w[idx], dtype=float)[:, :steps]

    events = build_events(batch, idx, ctx=ctx, horizon_steps=steps)

    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        **common,
        "abstained": False,
        "solar_w": _bands(solar),
        "load_w": _bands(load),
        "soc_percent": _bands(soc),
        "grid_w": _bands(grid),
        "events": events,
    }

    if role_mode == "grid_engineer":
        import_proxy = np.maximum(0.0, load - solar)
        payload.update(
            {
                "risk_summary": {
                    "min_soc_p10_pct": round(
                        float(np.min(np.quantile(soc, 0.10, axis=0))), 2
                    ),
                    "mean_import_proxy_w_p50": round(
                        float(np.median(import_proxy.mean(axis=1))), 2
                    ),
                    "max_import_w_p90": round(
                        float(np.quantile(np.max(grid, axis=1), 0.90)), 2
                    ),
                    "max_export_w_p90": round(
                        float(np.quantile(-np.min(grid, axis=1), 0.90)), 2
                    ),
                    "note": "grid stress is advisory; no dispatch setpoints are issued",
                }
            }
        )
    elif role_mode == "research":
        payload.update(
            {
                "diagnostics": {
                    "regime": report.regime,
                    "signal_widths": report.signal_widths,
                    "residual_context": report.residual_context,
                },
                "baselines": baselines or [],
                "rejection_sample": (rejection_sample or [])[:12],
                "notes": [
                    "Quantiles are computed from Tribunal-retained scenarios only.",
                    "The split is time-ordered (60/20/20); no future data enters training.",
                    "Survival dominates sharpness: low survival lowers confidence.",
                ],
            }
        )
    else:  # investor / government
        yield_kwh = float(np.mean(solar.sum(axis=1)) * batch.dt_h / 1000.0)
        p10_energy = float(np.quantile(solar.sum(axis=1), 0.10) * batch.dt_h / 1000.0)
        p90_energy = float(np.quantile(solar.sum(axis=1), 0.90) * batch.dt_h / 1000.0)
        payload.update(
            {
                "planning_aggregates": {
                    "solar_yield_p10_kwh": round(p10_energy, 3),
                    "solar_yield_p50_kwh": round(yield_kwh, 3),
                    "solar_yield_p90_kwh": round(p90_energy, 3),
                    "yield_band_note": "certified scenario band, not a financial guarantee",
                },
                "site_confidence": report.status,
                "not_a_financial_guarantee": True,
            }
        )
    return payload


def build_all(
    batch: ScenarioBatch,
    retained_idx: Sequence[int],
    report: ConfidenceReport,
    *,
    lineage: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ctx: Any = None,
    horizon_steps: int | None = None,
    baselines: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    rejection_sample: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Build all three role-mode advisories from one certified batch."""
    return {
        role: build_forecast(
            batch,
            retained_idx,
            report,
            role_mode=role,
            lineage=lineage,
            ctx=ctx,
            horizon_steps=horizon_steps,
            baselines=baselines,
            rejection_sample=rejection_sample,
        )
        for role in ROLE_MODES
    }

evaluation metrics + baseline scoreboard

"""Evaluation metrics for CHARIOT advisories.

Everything is computed against the *test* split of the time-ordered
60/20/20 split and is reported with the physics survival rate attached, so a
good point score can never hide a low survival rate.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Sequence

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd


def _clean(actual: np.ndarray, predicted: np.ndarray) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    a = np.asarray(actual, dtype=float)
    p = np.asarray(predicted, dtype=float)
    mask = np.isfinite(a) & np.isfinite(p)
    return a[mask], p[mask]


def point_metrics(actual: np.ndarray, predicted: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, float]:
    """MAE / RMSE / bias for a point forecast."""
    a, p = _clean(actual, predicted)
    if a.size == 0:
        return {"mae": float("nan"), "rmse": float("nan"), "bias": float("nan"), "n": 0}
    err = p - a
    return {
        "mae": float(np.mean(np.abs(err))),
        "rmse": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(err**2))),
        "bias": float(np.mean(err)),
        "n": int(a.size),
    }


def band_coverage(actual: np.ndarray, p10: np.ndarray, p90: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Fraction of observations inside the P10-P90 band."""
    a = np.asarray(actual, dtype=float)
    lo = np.asarray(p10, dtype=float)
    hi = np.asarray(p90, dtype=float)
    mask = np.isfinite(a) & np.isfinite(lo) & np.isfinite(hi)
    if mask.sum() == 0:
        return float("nan")
    inside = (a[mask] >= lo[mask]) & (a[mask] <= hi[mask])
    return float(np.mean(inside))


def energy_error_wh(actual: np.ndarray, predicted: np.ndarray, dt_h: float) -> float:
    """Absolute error of the integrated energy over the window (Wh)."""
    a = np.asarray(actual, dtype=float)
    p = np.asarray(predicted, dtype=float)
    mask = np.isfinite(a) & np.isfinite(p)
    if mask.sum() == 0:
        return float("nan")
    return float(abs(np.sum(p[mask]) - np.sum(a[mask])) * dt_h)


def score_window(
    actual: pd.DataFrame,
    forecast: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    dt_h: float = 0.25,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Score one advisory payload against the realised window."""
    out: dict[str, Any] = {}
    n = len(actual)
    for signal in ("solar_w", "load_w", "soc_percent", "grid_w"):
        bands = forecast.get(signal) or {}
        p50 = np.asarray(bands.get("p50", []), dtype=float)
        p10 = np.asarray(bands.get("p10", []), dtype=float)
        p90 = np.asarray(bands.get("p90", []), dtype=float)
        if p50.size == 0 or signal not in actual.columns:
            out[signal] = {"mae": float("nan"), "rmse": float("nan"), "coverage": float("nan")}
            continue
        truth = pd.to_numeric(actual[signal], errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)[: p50.size]
        out[signal] = {
            **point_metrics(truth, p50),
            "coverage": band_coverage(truth, p10[: truth.size], p90[: truth.size]),
            "energy_error_wh": energy_error_wh(truth, p50, dt_h),
        }
    out["n_steps"] = int(n)
    return out


def baseline_scoreboard(
    actual: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    last_solar: float,
    last_load: float,
    last_soc: float,
    train_hour_means: dict[int, float] | None = None,
    dt_h: float = 0.25,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Honest point baselines over the same window."""
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    n = len(actual)
    solar_truth = pd.to_numeric(actual["solar_w"], errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
    load_truth = (
        pd.to_numeric(actual["load_w"], errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
        if "load_w" in actual.columns
        else np.full(n, np.nan)
    )

    rows.append(
        {
            "target": "solar_w",
            "model": "persistence_origin",
            **point_metrics(solar_truth, np.full(n, last_solar)),
        }
    )
    # The materialised dataset carries the clear-sky reference as
    # ``clear_sky_power_w``; the raw telemetry calls it ``clear_sky_w``.
    clear_col = next(
        (c for c in ("clear_sky_w", "clear_sky_power_w") if c in actual.columns),
        None,
    )
    if clear_col is not None:
        clear = pd.to_numeric(actual[clear_col], errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
        rows.append(
            {
                "target": "solar_w",
                "model": "clear_sky_naive",
                **point_metrics(solar_truth, clear),
                "energy_error_wh": energy_error_wh(solar_truth, clear, dt_h),
            }
        )
    if train_hour_means:
        hours = pd.to_datetime(actual["timestamp_utc"], utc=True).dt.hour.to_numpy()
        clim = np.array([train_hour_means.get(int(h), np.nan) for h in hours])
        rows.append(
            {
                "target": "solar_w",
                "model": "train_hour_climatology",
                **point_metrics(solar_truth, clim),
                "energy_error_wh": energy_error_wh(solar_truth, clim, dt_h),
            }
        )
    if np.isfinite(load_truth).any():
        rows.append(
            {
                "target": "load_w",
                "model": "persistence_origin",
                **point_metrics(load_truth, np.full(n, last_load)),
            }
        )
    return rows


def aggregate_windows(rows: Sequence[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Aggregate per-window metric dicts into a summary."""
    out: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for signal in ("solar_w", "load_w", "soc_percent", "grid_w"):
        entries = [r[signal] for r in rows if signal in r and np.isfinite(r[signal].get("mae", np.nan))]
        if not entries:
            continue
        out[signal] = {
            "mae_mean": float(np.mean([e["mae"] for e in entries])),
            "rmse_mean": float(np.mean([e["rmse"] for e in entries])),
            "bias_mean": float(np.mean([e.get("bias", 0.0) for e in entries])),
            "coverage_mean": float(np.mean([e.get("coverage", np.nan) for e in entries])),
            "energy_error_wh_mean": float(
                np.mean([e.get("energy_error_wh", np.nan) for e in entries])
            ),
            "n_windows": len(entries),
        }
    return out
