    # ------------------------------------------------------ training extract
    @app.post("/training-extract/build", status_code=201)
    def training_extract_build(request: ExtractRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        ctx = runtime.context_for(request.tenant_id, request.site_id)
        frame = runtime.frame_for(request.tenant_id, request.site_id)
        factors = {
            k: float(v)
            for k, v in (request.calibration_factors or {}).items()
            if isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool)
        }
        manifest = tx.build_training_extract(
            store,
            frame,
            tenant_id=request.tenant_id,
            site_id=request.site_id,
            capsule_ids=request.capsule_ids,
            calibration_version=request.calibration_version,
            calibration_factors=factors,
            admissibility_versions=request.admissibility_versions,
            slice_ids=request.slice_ids,
            ctx=ctx,
            train_frac=request.train_frac,
            validate_frac=request.validate_frac,
            test_frac=request.test_frac,
            created_by=request.created_by,
        )
        return manifest

    @app.get("/training-extract/datasets/{dataset_version}")
    def training_extract_dataset(dataset_version: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        manifest = tx.get_manifest(store, dataset_version)
        frame = tx.load_dataset(store, dataset_version)
        return {
            "manifest": manifest,
            "rows": int(len(frame)),
            "split_counts": frame["split"].value_counts().to_dict(),
        }

    # ----------------------------------------------------------- chariot model
    @app.post("/model/chariot/train", status_code=201)
    def model_train(request: TrainRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        ctx = runtime.context_for(request.tenant_id, request.site_id)
        if request.dataset_version:
            dataset = tx.load_dataset(store, request.dataset_version)
        else:
            frame = runtime.frame_for(request.tenant_id, request.site_id)
            manifest = tx.build_training_extract(
                store,
                frame,
                tenant_id=request.tenant_id,
                site_id=request.site_id,
                capsule_ids=[],
                calibration_version=None,
                calibration_factors={},
                admissibility_versions=[],
                slice_ids=[],
                ctx=ctx,
                created_by=request.created_by,
            )
            dataset = tx.load_dataset(store, manifest["dataset_version"])
        train = dataset.loc[dataset["split"] == "train"].reset_index(drop=True)
        validate = dataset.loc[dataset["split"] == "validate"].reset_index(drop=True)
        model = mdl.fit(
            train,
            ctx,
            tenant_id=request.tenant_id,
            site_id=request.site_id,
            fleet_id=request.fleet_id,
            dataset_version=request.dataset_version or "",
            validation=validate if len(validate) else None,
        )
        runtime.models[model.model_version] = model
        store.upsert(
            "model_versions",
            {
                "model_version": model.model_version,
                "model_name": model.model_name,
                "dataset_version": request.dataset_version or "",
                "dataset_content_hash": "",
                "params_json": json.dumps(model.to_dict(), sort_keys=True, default=str),
                "diagnostics_json": json.dumps(model.diagnostics, sort_keys=True, default=str),
                "status": "candidate",
                "created_at_utc": utc_now(),
            },
        )
        return {
            "model_version": model.model_version,
            "model_name": model.model_name,
            "solar_channel": model.sites[request.site_id].solar.to_dict(),
            "diagnostics": model.diagnostics,
        }

    @app.post("/model/chariot/infer")
    def model_infer(request: ScenarioRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        model = _model_for(runtime, request.model_version, request.site_id)
        ctx = runtime.context_for(request.tenant_id, request.site_id)
        frame = (
            _frame_from_records(request.records)
            if request.records
            else runtime.frame_for(request.tenant_id, request.site_id)
        )
        prediction = mdl.one_step_prediction(model, request.site_id, frame)
        truth = mdl.solar_series(frame)
        mask = np.isfinite(truth) & np.isfinite(prediction)
        return {
            "model_version": model.model_version,
            "n_rows": int(mask.sum()),
            "one_step_mae_w": float(np.mean(np.abs(prediction[mask] - truth[mask])))
            if mask.any()
            else None,
            "one_step_rmse_w": float(np.sqrt(np.mean((prediction[mask] - truth[mask]) ** 2)))
            if mask.any()
            else None,
            "timestamps": [str(v) for v in frame["timestamp_utc"][mask].tolist()],
            "prediction_w": [round(float(v), 3) for v in prediction[mask]],
        }

    @app.post("/model/chariot/scenarios")
    def model_scenarios(request: ScenarioRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return _generate_batch(runtime, request)

    @app.get("/model/chariot/versions")
    def model_versions() -> dict[str, Any]:
        rows = store.query("SELECT * FROM model_versions ORDER BY created_at_utc DESC")
        return {"count": len(rows), "versions": rows}

    @app.post("/model/chariot/promote")
    def model_promote(model_version: str = Body(..., embed=True)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        store.execute(
            "UPDATE model_versions SET status = 'active' WHERE model_version = ?",
            (model_version,),
        )
        store.execute(
            "UPDATE model_versions SET status = 'superseded' "
            "WHERE model_version != ? AND status = 'active'",
            (model_version,),
        )
        return {"model_version": model_version, "status": "active"}

    # -------------------------------------------------------------- residual
    @app.post("/model/residual/train", status_code=201)
    def residual_train(request: ResidualTrainRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if request.dataset_version:
            dataset = tx.load_dataset(store, request.dataset_version)
        else:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="dataset_version is required")
        train = dataset.loc[dataset["split"] == "train"].reset_index(drop=True)
        validate = dataset.loc[dataset["split"] == "validate"].reset_index(drop=True)
        ctx = runtime.context_for(request.tenant_id, request.site_id)
        model = _model_for(runtime, None, request.site_id)
        mu = mdl.one_step_prediction(model, request.site_id, train)
        clear = mdl.clear_sky_series(train)
        y = mdl.solar_series(train)
        expected = np.divide(
            mu, np.maximum(clear, 1.0), out=np.full_like(mu, np.nan), where=clear > 1.0
        )
        X, names = res.build_features(
            train,
            mu_solar=mu,
            mu_load=np.zeros(len(train)),
            clear_sky=clear,
            expected_ratio=expected,
            last_solar=float(y[0]) if len(y) else 0.0,
        )
        artifact = res.fit_ridge(
            X,
            y,
            alpha=request.alpha,
            feature_names=names,
            target=request.target,
            dataset_version=request.dataset_version,
            w_max=request.w_max,
        )
        res.register(store, artifact, status="draft")
        return {"residual_version": artifact.residual_version, "diagnostics": artifact.diagnostics}

    @app.post("/model/residual/propose")
    def residual_propose(
        artifact: dict[str, Any] = Body(...), X: list[list[float]] = Body(...)
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        from chariot.residual import ResidualArtifact

        rebuilt = ResidualArtifact(
            residual_version=artifact.get("residual_version", "ad_hoc"),
            family=artifact.get("family", "ridge"),
            target=artifact.get("target", "solar_w"),
            feature_names=artifact.get("feature_names", []),
            coefficients=artifact.get("coefficients", []),
            intercept=float(artifact.get("intercept", 0.0)),
            w_max=float(artifact.get("w_max", RESIDUAL_POLICY.w_max)),
            diagnostics=artifact.get("diagnostics", {}),
        )
        paths = res.propose_paths(rebuilt, np.asarray(X, dtype=float))
        return {"residual_version": rebuilt.residual_version, "paths": paths.tolist()}

    @app.get("/model/residual/versions")
    def residual_versions() -> dict[str, Any]:
        rows = store.query("SELECT * FROM residual_versions ORDER BY created_at_utc DESC")
        return {"count": len(rows), "versions": rows}

    # -------------------------------------------------------------- scenarios
    @app.post("/model/scenarios/generate", status_code=201)
    def scenarios_generate(request: ScenarioRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return _generate_batch(runtime, request)

    @app.get("/model/scenarios/batches/{batch_id}")
    def scenarios_batch(batch_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        batch = runtime.batches.get(batch_id)
        if batch is None:
            row = store.query_one(
                "SELECT * FROM scenario_batches WHERE batch_id = ?", (batch_id,)
            )
            if row is None:
                raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"unknown batch {batch_id}")
            return {"batch_id": batch_id, "persisted": row}
        return {
            "batch_id": batch.batch_id,
            "origin_utc": batch.origin_utc,
            "n_scenarios": batch.n_scenarios,
            "horizon_steps": batch.horizon_steps,
            "content_hash": batch.content_hash,
            "timestamps": [str(v) for v in batch.timestamps],
        }

    # --------------------------------------------------------------- tribunal
    @app.post("/tribunal/validate-batch")
    def tribunal_validate(request: TribunalRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        batch = runtime.batches.get(request.batch_id)
        if batch is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"unknown batch {request.batch_id}")
        ctx = runtime.context_for(batch.tenant_id, batch.site_id)
        policy = runtime.tribunal_policy
        result = trib.validate_batch(
            batch,
            ctx=ctx,
            calibration=batch.lineage.get("calibration"),
            policy=policy,
            store=store,
        )
        runtime.retained[batch.batch_id] = result
        return {
            "batch_id": batch.batch_id,
            "n_proposed": result.n_proposed,
            "n_retained": result.n_retained,
            "n_rejected": result.n_rejected,
            "survival_rate": result.survival_rate,
            "constraint_counts": result.constraint_counts,
            "policy_version": policy.policy_version,
        }

    @app.get("/tribunal/batches/{batch_id}/retained")
    def tribunal_retained(batch_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        result = runtime.retained.get(batch_id)
        if result is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"no tribunal result for {batch_id}")
        return {
            "batch_id": batch_id,
            "n_retained": result.n_retained,
            "retained": [batch_id + "_" + str(i) for i in result.retained_idx],
        }

    @app.get("/tribunal/rejections")
    def tribunal_rejections(
        batch_id: str | None = Query(default=None),
        limit: int = Query(default=100, le=1000),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """The Rejection Echo — append-only, never pruned."""
        if batch_id:
            rows = store.rejections_for_batch(batch_id)[:limit]
        else:
            rows = store.all_rejections(limit=limit)
        return {"batch_id": batch_id, "count": len(rows), "rejections": rows}

    @app.get("/tribunal/rejections/{rejection_id}")
    def tribunal_rejection(rejection_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row = store.get_rejection(rejection_id)
        if row is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"unknown rejection {rejection_id}")
        return row

    @app.get("/tribunal/batches/{batch_id}/rejections")
    def tribunal_batch_rejections(
        batch_id: str, limit: int = Query(default=100, le=1000)
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        rows = store.rejections_for_batch(batch_id)[:limit]
        return {"batch_id": batch_id, "count": len(rows), "rejections": rows}

    @app.get("/tribunal/policy")
    def tribunal_policy() -> dict[str, Any]:
        policy = runtime.tribunal_policy
        return {
            **policy.to_dict(),
            "effective": True,
            "versions": [row["policy_version"] for row in store.policy_versions("tribunal")],
        }

    @app.put("/tribunal/policy")
    def tribunal_policy_put(request: PolicyRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Mint a new versioned Tribunal policy.

        The current policy is never mutated in place — a change creates the
        next version, records it, and makes it effective.
        """
        current = runtime.tribunal_policy
        updates = {
            key: value
            for key, value in request.model_dump(exclude_none=True).items()
            if key in {
                "energy_balance_tol_wh",
                "soc_min",
                "soc_max",
                "solar_scale_kappa",
                "power_limit_tolerance",
                "solar_ramp_w_per_step",
                "load_ramp_w_per_step",
                "min_survival_for_certified",
            }
        }
        if not updates:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="no policy fields supplied")
        if updates.get("soc_min") is not None and updates.get("soc_max") is not None:
            if float(updates["soc_min"]) >= float(updates["soc_max"]):
                raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="soc_min must be < soc_max")
        version = next_policy_version(current.policy_version, "tribunal")
        new_policy = replace(current, policy_version=version, **updates)
        store.record_policy_version(
            "tribunal",
            version,
            {**new_policy.to_dict(), "reason": request.reason},
            actor=request.actor,
        )
        runtime.tribunal_policy = new_policy
        return {
            **new_policy.to_dict(),
            "previous_version": current.policy_version,
            "superseded": True,
        }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- feedback
    @app.post("/feedback/cortex/run")
    def feedback_run(request: CortexRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        result = runtime.retained.get(request.batch_id)
        survival = (
            request.survival_rate
            if request.survival_rate is not None
            else (result.survival_rate if result is not None else 0.0)
        )
        job = fb.run_cortex(
            store,
            batch_id=request.batch_id,
            governor=runtime.ensure_governor(),
            survival_rate=survival,
            survival_delta=request.survival_delta,
        )
        return job

    @app.get("/feedback/jobs")
    def feedback_jobs(limit: int = Query(default=50, le=500)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        rows = store.query(
            "SELECT * FROM feedback_jobs ORDER BY created_at_utc DESC LIMIT ?", (limit,)
        )
        return {"count": len(rows), "jobs": rows}

    # --------------------------------------------------------------- governor
    @app.get("/residual/governor/status")
    def governor_status() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return runtime.ensure_governor().status()

    @app.post("/residual/governor/evaluate")
    def governor_evaluate(request: GovernorRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        batch = runtime.batches.get(request.batch_id or "")
        if batch is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="batch_id is required")
        ctx = runtime.context_for(batch.tenant_id, batch.site_id)
        return gov.governor_signals(
            runtime.ensure_governor(),
            batch=batch,
            ctx=ctx,
            store=store,
        )

    @app.post("/residual/governor/quarantine")
    def governor_quarantine(request: GovernorRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        runtime.ensure_governor().quarantine(
            batch_id=request.batch_id or "", reason=request.reason, actor=request.actor
        )
        return runtime.ensure_governor().status()

    @app.post("/residual/governor/set-weight")
    def governor_set_weight(request: GovernorRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if request.w is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="w is required")
        runtime.ensure_governor().set_weight(float(request.w), actor=request.actor)
        return runtime.ensure_governor().status()

    # -------------------------------------------------------------- confidence
    @app.post("/confidence/evaluate")
    def confidence_evaluate(request: ConfidenceRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        batch = runtime.batches.get(request.batch_id)
        result = runtime.retained.get(request.batch_id)
        if batch is None or result is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="batch has not been validated")
        policy = runtime.confidence_policy
        report = conf.evaluate(
            batch,
            list(result.retained_idx),
            survival_rate=result.survival_rate,
            policy=policy,
            residual_context=request.residual_context or None,
            store=store,
        )
        runtime.reports[report.report_id] = report
        return report.to_dict()

    @app.get("/confidence/reports/{report_id}")
    def confidence_report(report_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        report = runtime.reports.get(report_id)
        if report is not None:
            return report.to_dict()
        row = store.query_one("SELECT * FROM confidence_reports WHERE report_id = ?", (report_id,))
        if row is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"unknown report {report_id}")
        return row

    @app.get("/confidence/policy")
    def confidence_policy() -> dict[str, Any]:
        policy = runtime.confidence_policy
        return {
            **policy.to_dict(),
            "effective": True,
            "versions": [row["policy_version"] for row in store.policy_versions("confidence")],
        }

    @app.put("/confidence/policy")
    def confidence_policy_put(request: PolicyRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Mint a new versioned Confidence Engine policy."""
        current = runtime.confidence_policy
        updates = {
            key: value
            for key, value in request.model_dump(exclude_none=True).items()
            if key in {"s_min", "c_low", "u_high", "sharpness", "uncertainty_reference"}
        }
        if not updates:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="no policy fields supplied")
        if updates.get("s_min") is not None and not 0.0 <= float(updates["s_min"]) <= 1.0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="s_min must be in [0, 1]")
        version = next_policy_version(current.policy_version, "confidence")
        new_policy = replace(current, policy_version=version, **updates)
        store.record_policy_version(
            "confidence",
            version,
            {**new_policy.to_dict(), "reason": request.reason},
            actor=request.actor,
        )
        runtime.confidence_policy = new_policy
        return {
            **new_policy.to_dict(),
            "previous_version": current.policy_version,
            "superseded": True,
        }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- advisory
    @app.post("/advisory/forecast")
    def advisory_forecast(request: AdvisoryRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        batch = runtime.batches.get(request.batch_id)
        result = runtime.retained.get(request.batch_id)
        report = None
        for candidate in runtime.reports.values():
            if candidate.batch_id == request.batch_id:
                report = candidate
                break
        if batch is None or result is None or report is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=404, detail="batch has not been validated/confidenced"
            )
        ctx = runtime.context_for(batch.tenant_id, batch.site_id)
        payload = adv.build_forecast(
            batch,
            list(result.retained_idx),
            report,
            role_mode=request.role_mode,
            horizon=request.horizon,
            ctx=ctx,
            horizon_steps=request.horizon_steps,
            lineage=_advisory_lineage(runtime, batch, result, report),
        )
        runtime.advisories[request.batch_id] = payload
        return payload

    @app.get("/advisory/events")
    def advisory_events(batch_id: str = Query(...)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        batch = runtime.batches.get(batch_id)
        if batch is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"unknown batch {batch_id}")
        ctx = runtime.context_for(batch.tenant_id, batch.site_id)
        result = runtime.retained.get(batch_id)
        retained_idx = list(result.retained_idx) if result is not None else []
        return adv.build_events(batch, retained_idx, ctx=ctx)

    @app.get("/advisory/confidence")
    def advisory_confidence(batch_id: str = Query(...)) -> dict[str, Any]:
        for report in runtime.reports.values():
            if report.batch_id == batch_id:
                return report.to_dict()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"no confidence report for {batch_id}")

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------- meta
    @app.get("/health")
    def health() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "status": "ok",
            "api_version": API_VERSION,
            "store": str(path),
            "policies": policy_snapshot(),
            "horizons": dict(HORIZONS),
            "advisory_only": True,
        }

    return app


def _model_for(
    runtime: Runtime, model_version: str | None, site_id: str
) -> mdl.ChariotModel:
    if model_version:
        model = runtime.models.get(model_version)
        if model is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"unknown model {model_version}")
        return model
    for model in reversed(list(runtime.models.values())):
        if site_id in model.sites:
            return model
    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="no trained model in memory")


def _advisory_lineage(
    runtime: Runtime,
    batch: gen.ScenarioBatch,
    result: Any,
    report: conf.ConfidenceReport,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Full lineage chain carried on every advisory response (PDF §4.14).

    telemetry → admissibility → river slice → calibration → capsule →
    dataset → model → residual → scenario batch → tribunal result →
    confidence report → advisory
    """
    store = runtime.store
    batch_lineage = batch.lineage or {}
    calibration = batch_lineage.get("calibration") or {}

    slice_ids: list[str] = []
    capsule_ids: list[str] = []
    try:
        for row in store.query(
            "SELECT slice_id FROM river_slices WHERE tenant_id = ? AND site_id = ?"
            " ORDER BY slice_id",
            (batch.tenant_id, batch.site_id),
        ):
            slice_ids.append(row["slice_id"])
        for row in store.query(
            "SELECT capsule_id FROM capsules WHERE tenant_id = ? AND site_id = ?"
            " ORDER BY capsule_id",
            (batch.tenant_id, batch.site_id),
        ):
            capsule_ids.append(row["capsule_id"])
    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - lineage is best-effort, never fatal
        slice_ids, capsule_ids = [], []

    return {
        "tenant_id": batch.tenant_id,
        "site_id": batch.site_id,
        "admissibility": {
            "rule_set_version": ADMISSIBILITY_POLICY.rule_set_version,
        },
        "river_slice": {"slice_ids": slice_ids},
        "calibration_version": calibration.get("calibration_version"),
        "calibration_factors": {
            key: calibration.get(key)
            for key in (
                "capacity_factor",
                "charge_loss_factor",
                "discharge_loss_factor",
                "pv_correction_factor",
            )
        },
        "capsule_id": capsule_ids[-1] if capsule_ids else None,
        "capsule_ids": capsule_ids,
        "dataset_version": batch.dataset_version,
        "chariot_model_version": batch.model_version,
        "residual_version": batch.residual_version,
        "residual_weight": batch.residual_weight,
        "scenario_batch": {
            "batch_id": batch.batch_id,
            "origin_utc": batch.origin_utc,
            "n_scenarios": batch.n_scenarios,
            "horizon_steps": batch.horizon_steps,
            "content_hash": batch.content_hash,
        },
        "tribunal_result": {
            "n_proposed": result.n_proposed,
            "n_retained": result.n_retained,
            "n_rejected": result.n_rejected,
            "survival_rate": round(float(result.survival_rate), 6),
            "constraint_counts": result.constraint_counts,
            "policy_version": runtime.tribunal_policy.policy_version,
            "solver_version": runtime.tribunal_policy.solver_version,
        },
        "confidence_report": {
            "report_id": report.report_id,
            "status": report.status,
            "confidence": round(float(report.confidence), 6),
            "policy_version": report.policy_version,
        },
        "policies": policy_snapshot(),
    }


def _generate_batch(runtime: Runtime, request: ScenarioRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
    model = _model_for(runtime, request.model_version, request.site_id)
    ctx = runtime.context_for(request.tenant_id, request.site_id)
    frame = (
        _frame_from_records(request.records)
        if request.records
        else runtime.frame_for(request.tenant_id, request.site_id)
    )
    steps = min(HORIZONS.get(request.horizon, 96), len(frame))
    future = frame.iloc[:steps].reset_index(drop=True)
    # The generator and the Tribunal must see the *same* calibration, or the
    # energy balance law cannot hold (the Tribunal is fail-closed).
    factors = cal.resolve(
        runtime.store, tenant_id=request.tenant_id, site_id=request.site_id
    )
    batch = gen.generate_batch(
        model,
        request.site_id,
        future,
        tenant_id=request.tenant_id,
        n_scenarios=request.n_scenarios,
        seed=request.seed,
        ctx=ctx,
        calibration=factors.to_dict(),
        dt_h=0.25,
    )
    runtime.batches[batch.batch_id] = batch
    return {
        "batch_id": batch.batch_id,
        "origin_utc": batch.origin_utc,
        "n_scenarios": batch.n_scenarios,
        "horizon_steps": batch.horizon_steps,
        "content_hash": batch.content_hash,
    }


app = create_app(os.environ.get("CHARIOT_STORE", "var/chariot.db"))


def main() -> None:  # pragma: no cover - manual entry point
    import uvicorn

    uvicorn.run(
        "chariot.api:app",
        host=os.environ.get("CHARIOT_HOST", "0.0.0.0"),
        port=int(os.environ.get("CHARIOT_PORT", "8000")),
        reload=False,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":  # pragma: no cover
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Prepare the DKASC Alice Springs telemetry for a CHARIOT run.

Two protocols are supported.

``single`` (default, unchanged)
    One window, split chronologically into 60/20/20 train/validate/test.
    This is what the v3 reference run used.

``year2022_2023``
    The **2022 → 2023** evaluation protocol:

    ==================  ====================================================
    ``train_2022``      the first 60 % of the 2022 window (training only)
    ``validate_2022``   the remaining 40 % of the 2022 window (selection only)
    ``test_2023``       the whole 2023 window (report only, never fitted)
    ==================  ====================================================

    The 2022 window is *not* split 60/20/20: the 2023 year replaces the test
    split, so no 2022 row is ever used as a test target and nothing is fitted
    or selected on 2023.

The prepared slice is written to ``var/data/`` (git-ignored) together with a
manifest that records the content hash and the exact split boundaries, so the
split can be verified without shipping the bulk payload into git.

Usage::

    python scripts/prepare_dataset.py --source <dka_alice_springs_solar_only_15min_2022.csv>

    python scripts/prepare_dataset.py --protocol year2022_2023 \
        --source      <dka_alice_springs_solar_only_15min_2022.csv> \
        --source-test <dka_alice_springs_solar_only_15min_2023.csv> \
        --out runs/w1_data_2022_2023
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from chariot.dataset_qc import qc_frame, render_qc_markdown, summarise  # noqa: E402
from chariot.hashing import frame_content_hash  # noqa: E402
from chariot.training_extract import assign_splits  # noqa: E402

DEFAULT_START = "2022-01-01"
DEFAULT_END = "2023-01-01"
DEFAULT_OUT = ROOT / "var" / "data" / "dka_alice_springs_2022_15min.parquet"

#: The 2022 → 2023 protocol windows.
PROTOCOL_YEAR_2022 = ("2022-01-01", "2023-01-01")
PROTOCOL_YEAR_2023 = ("2023-01-01", "2024-01-01")
PROTOCOL_DATA_DIR = ROOT / "var" / "data"


def _read_source(path: Path, start: str, end: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Read a CSV export and slice it to [start, end)."""
    frame = pd.read_csv(path)
    frame["timestamp_utc"] = pd.to_datetime(frame["timestamp_utc"], utc=True)
    mask = (frame["timestamp_utc"] >= pd.Timestamp(start, tz="UTC")) & (
        frame["timestamp_utc"] < pd.Timestamp(end, tz="UTC")
    )
    sliced = frame.loc[mask].sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)
    if sliced.empty:
        raise SystemExit(f"no rows in {start}..{end} for {path}")
    if "is_gap" in sliced.columns:
        sliced["is_gap"] = sliced["is_gap"].astype(str).str.lower().isin(["1", "true", "yes"])
    else:
        sliced["is_gap"] = False
    return sliced


def _boundaries(frame: pd.DataFrame, labels: pd.Series) -> dict[str, Any]:
    out: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for name in ("train", "validate", "test"):
        sel = frame.loc[labels == name, "timestamp_utc"]
        if len(sel) == 0:
            continue
        out[name] = {
            "min": str(sel.min()),
            "max": str(sel.max()),
            "count": int(len(sel)),
        }
    return out


def prepare_single(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """The original single-window 60/20/20 protocol."""
    source = Path(args.source)
    if not source.exists():
        print(f"source not found: {source}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

    frame = pd.read_csv(source)
    frame["timestamp_utc"] = pd.to_datetime(frame["timestamp_utc"], utc=True)
    mask = (frame["timestamp_utc"] >= pd.Timestamp(args.start, tz="UTC")) & (
        frame["timestamp_utc"] < pd.Timestamp(args.end, tz="UTC")
    )
    sliced = frame.loc[mask].sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)
    if sliced.empty:
        print(f"no rows in {args.start}..{args.end}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 3

    if "is_gap" in sliced.columns:
        sliced["is_gap"] = sliced["is_gap"].astype(str).str.lower().isin(["1", "true", "yes"])
    else:
        sliced["is_gap"] = False

    sliced["split"] = assign_splits(
        sliced,
        train_frac=args.train_frac,
        validate_frac=args.validate_frac,
        test_frac=args.test_frac,
    )

    out = Path(args.out)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    sliced.to_parquet(out, index=False)

    boundaries = _boundaries(sliced, sliced["split"])
    manifest = {
        "source": str(source),
        "source_rows": int(len(frame)),
        "source_window": [str(frame["timestamp_utc"].min()), str(frame["timestamp_utc"].max())],
        "slice_window": [args.start, args.end],
        "season": "full year" if (args.start, args.end) == (DEFAULT_START, DEFAULT_END) else "custom window",
        "rows": int(len(sliced)),
        "cadence_min": 15,
        "split_policy": "time_ordered_60_20_20",
        "split_fractions": {
            "train": args.train_frac,
            "validate": args.validate_frac,
            "test": args.test_frac,
        },
        "split_boundaries": boundaries,
        "content_hash": frame_content_hash(sliced),
        "output": str(out),
        "columns": list(sliced.columns),
    }
    manifest_path = out.with_suffix(".manifest.json")
    manifest_path.write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    print(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2))
    return 0


def prepare_year2022_2023(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Build train_2022 / validate_2022 / test_2023 and the QC report."""
    src_2022 = Path(args.source)
    src_2023 = Path(args.source_test)
    for p in (src_2022, src_2023):
        if not p.exists():
            print(f"source not found: {p}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2

    data_dir = Path(args.data_dir)
    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    run_dir = Path(args.out)
    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # -- the 2022 training window (train + validation only) ---------------
    f22 = _read_source(src_2022, *PROTOCOL_YEAR_2022)
    labels = assign_splits(
        f22,
        train_frac=args.train_frac,
        validate_frac=1.0 - args.train_frac,
        test_frac=0.0,
    )
    f22["split"] = labels
    f22["split_year"] = 2022
    train_2022 = f22.loc[labels == "train"].reset_index(drop=True)
    validate_2022 = f22.loc[labels == "validate"].reset_index(drop=True)

    # -- the 2023 report-only window --------------------------------------
    f23 = _read_source(src_2023, *PROTOCOL_YEAR_2023)
    f23["split"] = "test"
    f23["split_year"] = 2023
    test_2023 = f23.reset_index(drop=True)

    # -- the clear-sky reference (W2) -------------------------------------
    # "export" keeps the reference the DKASC export shipped (the 2022 and
    # the 2023 exports do not share it -- that is the defect W2 repairs).
    # "chariot_v2" rebuilds one documented reference for both years, with
    # the geometry inferred from the *training* year's measured POA.
    clear_sky_info: dict[str, Any] = {"source": args.clearsky}
    if args.clearsky == "chariot_v2":
        from chariot.clearsky import (  # noqa: PLC0415
            CLEAR_SKY_VERSION,
            clearness_diagnostics,
            compute_clear_sky,
            infer_fixed_plane_geometry,
            tracker_reference_rmse,
        )

        inference = infer_fixed_plane_geometry(train_2022)
        geometry = inference["geometry"]
        tracker_rmse = tracker_reference_rmse(train_2022, geometry)
        # The clearness diagnostics of the *exported* reference, captured
        # before it is replaced.
        before = {
            name: clearness_diagnostics(
                frame["solar_w"].to_numpy(dtype=float),
                frame["clear_sky_w"].to_numpy(dtype=float),
            )
            for name, frame in (
                ("train_2022", train_2022),
                ("validate_2022", validate_2022),
                ("test_2023", test_2023),
            )
        }
        for frame in (train_2022, validate_2022, test_2023):
            reference = compute_clear_sky(
                frame, geometry, rated_pv_w=args.rated_pv_w
            )
            frame["clear_sky_poa_w_m2"] = reference["clear_sky_poa_w_m2"].to_numpy()
            frame["clear_sky_w"] = reference["clear_sky_w"].to_numpy()
            frame["kt"] = reference["kt"].to_numpy()
            frame["clear_sky_version"] = CLEAR_SKY_VERSION
        clear_sky_info = {
            "source": "chariot_v2",
            "clear_sky_version": CLEAR_SKY_VERSION,
            "geometry": geometry.as_dict(),
            "geometry_inference": {
                "target": "measured poa_w_m2 on the 2022 train slice",
                "best": inference["best"],
                "grid_top": inference["grid"],
            },
            "tracker_reference_rmse_w_m2": tracker_rmse,
            "clearness_before": before,
            "note": (
                "The array layout is not in the export, so no tracker mix is "
                "invented: the documented equivalent is the single fixed plane "
                "that best reproduces the measured POA of the training year."
            ),
        }

    slices = {
        "train_2022": train_2022,
        "validate_2022": validate_2022,
        "test_2023": test_2023,
    }
    paths: dict[str, str] = {}
    hashes: dict[str, str] = {}
    for name, frame in slices.items():
        path = data_dir / f"dka_alice_springs_{name}.parquet"
        frame.to_parquet(path, index=False)
        paths[name] = str(path)
        hashes[name] = frame_content_hash(frame)

    # -- the combined slice the spine consumes ----------------------------
    combined = (
        pd.concat([train_2022, validate_2022, test_2023], ignore_index=True)
        .sort_values("timestamp_utc")
        .reset_index(drop=True)
    )
    combined_path = data_dir / "dka_alice_springs_2022_2023.parquet"
    combined.to_parquet(combined_path, index=False)
    combined_hash = frame_content_hash(combined)

    manifest = {
        "protocol": "year2022_2023",
        "protocol_version": "chariot_protocol_2022_2023_v1.0",
        "description": (
            "TRAIN = the first 60 % of the 2022 window; VALIDATE = the remaining "
            "40 % of the 2022 window; TEST = the whole 2023 window (report only, "
            "never fitted, never selected on).  The 2022 test tail of the legacy "
            "60/20/20 protocol is discarded on purpose."
        ),
        "sources": {
            "train_validate_2022": str(src_2022),
            "test_2023": str(src_2023),
        },
        "windows": {
            "train_validate_2022": list(PROTOCOL_YEAR_2022),
            "test_2023": list(PROTOCOL_YEAR_2023),
        },
        "split_fractions": {
            "train_2022": args.train_frac,
            "validate_2022": 1.0 - args.train_frac,
        },
        "split_boundaries": {
            "train_2022": _boundaries(train_2022, train_2022["split"]),
            "validate_2022": _boundaries(validate_2022, validate_2022["split"]),
            "test_2023": _boundaries(test_2023, test_2023["split"]),
        },
        "content_hashes": {
            **{name: hashes[name] for name in slices},
            "combined": combined_hash,
        },
        "rows": {name: int(len(frame)) for name, frame in slices.items()},
        "rows_combined": int(len(combined)),
        "outputs": {**paths, "combined": str(combined_path)},
        "cadence_min": 15,
        "columns": list(combined.columns),
        "selection_split": "validate_2022",
        "test_split_usage": "report only",
        "clear_sky": clear_sky_info,
    }
    manifest_path = data_dir / "dka_alice_springs_2022_2023.manifest.json"
    manifest_path.write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    # -- QC ----------------------------------------------------------------
    qc_slices = {
        "train_2022": qc_frame(train_2022, rated_pv_w=args.rated_pv_w, label="train_2022"),
        "validate_2022": qc_frame(validate_2022, rated_pv_w=args.rated_pv_w, label="validate_2022"),
        "test_2023": qc_frame(test_2023, rated_pv_w=args.rated_pv_w, label="test_2023"),
    }
    qc = {
        "stage": "W1",
        "protocol": "year2022_2023",
        "slices": qc_slices,
        "content_hashes": manifest["content_hashes"],
        "split_boundaries": manifest["split_boundaries"],
        "notes": [
            "The two years use **different clear-sky models**: the 2022 export was "
            "built with a 15 degree tilt, the 2023 export with a 17.5 degree tilt "
            "(both single-plane equivalents inferred from the measured POA).  That "
            "is a change of the *reference*, not of the site, and it is the first "
            "suspect for the year-to-year shift in the clearness ratio.",
            "The clearness ratio above 1.0 (the 'clear-sky under-binding') is the "
            "known defect this stage quantifies; W2 (the clear-sky upgrade) is "
            "gated on the 2022 validation slice and the 2023 slice is reported once.",
            "The load, battery and SOC channels are **declared** site-card "
            "assumptions; nothing in this stage measures them.",
        ],
    }
    (run_dir / "qc.json").write_text(json.dumps(qc, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8")
    (run_dir / "summary.md").write_text(
        render_qc_markdown(
            qc,
            title="W1 — data + the 2022→2023 protocol (QC)",
            extra=(
                "Protocol: **2022 train → 2022 validate → 2023 test**.\n\n"
                f"Rows: train_2022 {manifest['rows']['train_2022']}, "
                f"validate_2022 {manifest['rows']['validate_2022']}, "
                f"test_2023 {manifest['rows']['test_2023']}.\n\n"
                "Content hashes: "
                + ", ".join(f"`{k}` {v[:16]}…" for k, v in manifest["content_hashes"].items())
            ),
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    print(json.dumps({"manifest": str(manifest_path), "qc": summarise(qc)}, indent=2))
    print(f"wrote {run_dir / 'summary.md'} and {run_dir / 'qc.json'}")
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter
    )
    parser.add_argument("--protocol", default="single", choices=["single", "year2022_2023"])
    parser.add_argument("--source", required=True, help="source CSV (2022 export)")
    parser.add_argument("--source-test", help="source CSV (2023 export, year2022_2023 only)")
    parser.add_argument("--start", default=DEFAULT_START)
    parser.add_argument("--end", default=DEFAULT_END)
    parser.add_argument("--out", default=str(DEFAULT_OUT))
    parser.add_argument("--data-dir", default=str(PROTOCOL_DATA_DIR))
    parser.add_argument("--rated-pv-w", type=float, default=220_000.0)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--clearsky",
        default="chariot_v2",
        choices=["export", "chariot_v2"],
        help=(
            "clear-sky reference: 'chariot_v2' (default, promoted in W2) rebuilds "
            "one documented reference for both years, with the geometry inferred "
            "from the training year's measured POA; 'export' keeps the reference "
            "the DKASC export shipped (the 2022 and 2023 exports do not share it) "
            "and reproduces the W1 baseline"
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--train-frac", type=float, default=0.60, help="train fraction (default 0.60)"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--validate-frac", type=float, default=0.20, help="validate fraction (single protocol)"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--test-frac", type=float, default=0.20, help="test fraction (single protocol)"
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.protocol == "year2022_2023":
        if not args.source_test:
            print("--source-test is required for the year2022_2023 protocol", file=sys.stderr)
            return 2
        return prepare_year2022_2023(args)
    return prepare_single(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run the CHARIOT v3 end-to-end spine.

The spine is:

    Telemetry → Site Master → Admissibility → River Flow → Calibration →
    Capsule → Training Extract → CHARIOT model → Residual (capped) →
    Scenarios → Reality Tribunal / Rejection Echo → Confidence → Advisory

Usage (full DKASC Alice Springs 2022 year, prepared with
``scripts/prepare_dataset.py``):

    python scripts/run_chariot3.py

    python scripts/run_chariot3.py \\
        --source var/data/dka_alice_springs_2022_15min.parquet \\
        --out    runs/chariot3_alice_springs_2022 \\
        --scenarios 500 --max-windows 40
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from chariot.pipeline import PipelineConfig, run  # noqa: E402

DEFAULT_SOURCE = ROOT / "var" / "data" / "dka_alice_springs_2022_15min.parquet"
DEFAULT_OUT = ROOT / "runs" / "chariot3_alice_springs_2022"
DEFAULT_STORE = ROOT / "var" / "chariot.db"


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--source",
        default=str(DEFAULT_SOURCE),
        help="prepared parquet slice (or the raw CSV export)",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--out", default=str(DEFAULT_OUT), help="run output directory")
    parser.add_argument("--store", default=str(DEFAULT_STORE), help="SQLite lineage store")
    parser.add_argument("--scenarios", type=int, default=500, help="scenarios per batch")
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=7)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--horizon",
        default="operational",
        choices=["short", "operational", "long"],
        help="advisory horizon (must not exceed the certified trajectory length)",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--max-windows", type=int, default=None, help="cap the number of windows")
    parser.add_argument("--no-residual", action="store_true", help="disable the Residual Chamber")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-spread-calibration",
        action="store_true",
        help="skip validation-fitted ensemble spread calibration",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--keep-parquet", action="store_true", help="keep the materialised dataset")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--evaluate-split",
        default="test",
        choices=["test", "validate", "train"],
        help="split the rolling-origin evaluation windows are drawn from",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--start", default=None, help="analysis window start (ISO)")
    parser.add_argument("--end", default=None, help="analysis window end (ISO, exclusive)")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--honour-split",
        action="store_true",
        help=(
            "honour the split assigned in the prepared slice instead of "
            "re-splitting it chronologically (the 2022 -> 2023 protocol: "
            "train/validate in 2022, report-only test in 2023)"
        ),
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    config = PipelineConfig(
        source_csv=args.source,
        **({"start": args.start} if args.start else {}),
        **({"end": args.end} if args.end else {}),
        out_dir=args.out,
        store_path=args.store,
        n_scenarios=args.scenarios,
        seed=args.seed,
        horizon=args.horizon,
        max_windows=args.max_windows,
        use_residual=not args.no_residual,
        calibrate_spread=not args.no_spread_calibration,
        keep_parquet=args.keep_parquet,
        honour_split=args.honour_split,
        evaluate_split=args.evaluate_split,
    )
    manifest = run(config)
    print(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, default=str))
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Diagnostics for the CHARIOT clearness channel fit.

Prints the fitted state-space parameters, the implied vs. observed spread of
the clear-sky ratio, the one-step-ahead error, and the ramp statistics of the
simulated ensemble versus the observed record.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from chariot.model import (  # noqa: E402
    clear_sky_series,
    fit,
    filter_state_series,
    one_step_prediction,
    simulate,
    solar_series,
)
from chariot.pipeline import alice_springs_site  # noqa: E402
from chariot.config import SiteContext  # noqa: E402


def main() -> int:
    src = Path(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "var/data/dka_alice_springs_spring2022_15min.parquet")
    frame = pd.read_parquet(src) if src.suffix == ".parquet" else pd.read_csv(src)
    frame["timestamp_utc"] = pd.to_datetime(frame["timestamp_utc"], utc=True)

    site = alice_springs_site()
    ctx = SiteContext(
        tenant_id=site["tenant_id"],
        site_id=site["site_id"],
        rated_pv_w=site["rated_pv_w"],
        usable_battery_capacity_wh=site["usable_battery_capacity_wh"],
        max_charge_w=site["max_charge_w"],
        max_discharge_w=site["max_discharge_w"],
        timezone=site["timezone"],
        latitude=site["latitude"],
        longitude=site["longitude"],
        fleet_id=site["fleet_id"],
        metadata=site["metadata"],
        source="site_master",
        missing_rating=False,
    )

    train = frame.iloc[: int(len(frame) * 0.6)].reset_index(drop=True)
    model = fit(train, ctx, tenant_id=ctx.tenant_id, site_id=ctx.site_id, fleet_id="dkasc")
    ch = model.sites[ctx.site_id].solar
    print("=== fitted clearness channel ===")
    for key in ("mu", "rho", "sigma", "gamma", "obs_scale", "n_obs"):
        print(f"  {key:12s} {getattr(ch, key):.6f}")
    print("  se:", {k: round(v, 6) for k, v in ch.se.items()})
    print("=== diagnostics ===")
    for key, value in ch.diagnostics.items():
        print(f"  {key:28s} {value}")

    # --- one step ahead check over the validation window -----------------
    filt = filter_state_series(model, ctx.site_id, frame)
    clear = clear_sky_series(frame)
    solar = solar_series(frame)
    mu1 = one_step_prediction(model, ctx.site_id, frame)
    mask = (clear > 1.0) & np.isfinite(solar)
    err = solar[mask] - mu1[mask]
    print("=== one-step-ahead (all rows) ===")
    print(f"  MAE  {np.mean(np.abs(err)):,.0f} W")
    print(f"  RMSE {np.sqrt(np.mean(err**2)):,.0f} W")
    print(f"  bias {np.mean(err):,.0f} W")
    print(f"  persistence MAE {np.mean(np.abs(np.diff(solar[mask]))):,.0f} W (proxy)")

    # --- simulate an ensemble and compare the ramp statistics ------------
    future = frame.iloc[-96:].reset_index(drop=True)
    origin_state = {
        "v_mean": float(filt["filtered"][-97]),
        "v_var": float(filt["filtered_var"][-97]),
    }
    sim = simulate(model, ctx.site_id, future, n_scenarios=300, seed=3, state=origin_state)
    sim_solar = sim["solar_w"]
    ramps = np.abs(np.diff(sim_solar, axis=1))
    obs_ramps = np.abs(np.diff(solar_series(frame)))
    print("=== ramp statistics ===")
    print(f"  observed   p50 {np.percentile(obs_ramps,50):,.0f}  p90 {np.percentile(obs_ramps,90):,.0f}  max {obs_ramps.max():,.0f}")
    print(f"  simulated  p50 {np.percentile(ramps,50):,.0f}  p90 {np.percentile(ramps,90):,.0f}  max {ramps.max():,.0f}")
    print(f"  simulated solar max {sim_solar.max():,.0f} W  (ceiling 1.2*rated = {1.2*220000:,.0f})")
    print(f"  simulated clearness u: p50 {np.percentile(sim['clearness_u'],50):.3f} p99 {np.percentile(sim['clearness_u'],99):.3f} max {sim['clearness_u'].max():.3f}")
    fut_clear = clear_sky_series(future)
    ratio = sim["solar_w"] / np.maximum(fut_clear[None, :], 1.0)
    day = np.broadcast_to(fut_clear[None, :] > 1.0, ratio.shape)
    print(f"  simulated R (daylight): p50 {np.percentile(ratio[day],50):.3f} p99 {np.percentile(ratio[day],99):.3f}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

the ops-vs-PyMC reference comparison harness
