#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compare the operational NumPy clearness model with the offline PyMC reference.

Both models are fitted on the **same** training split of the **same** Alice
Springs 2022 training extract, both are rolled forward over the **same**
rolling-origin windows on the validation and test splits, and both are
certified by the **same** Tribunal.  The comparison is therefore about the
inference, not about the plumbing.

Rules honoured by this script
-----------------------------
* **The test split is report-only.**  Every choice (the seasonal harmonic,
  the ensemble spread factor, the number of chains, the draws) is made on the
  training or validation split.  Nothing is fitted on the test split and no
  test target ever reaches a fit.
* **The residual chamber is OFF** for this comparison, for both models, so
  the numbers describe the clearness model and not the residual corrector.
  The v3 operational run keeps its own residual policy; nothing here changes
  it.
* **The Tribunal is not loosened.**  Reference scenarios are generated
  through the documented adapter seam in :func:`chariot.genesis.generate_batch`
  and then go through exactly the same physics gate, the same content hash
  and the same lineage as operational scenarios.
* **The ops model stays the default.**  This script never writes an active
  model version and never changes the operational configuration.

Usage::

    python scripts/run_pymc_reference.py \
        --source /path/to/dka_alice_springs_solar_only_15min_2022.csv \
        --out runs/pymc_reference \
        --draws 500 --tune 500 --chains 2

Outputs (in ``--out``):

* ``comparison.json`` — the full machine-readable comparison;
* ``summary.md`` — the side-by-side table (small, safe to commit);
* ``stdout`` — the same table.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from chariot.admissibility import evaluate_frame  # noqa: E402
from chariot.advisory import build_all  # noqa: E402
from chariot.calibration import approve_version, create_version  # noqa: E402
from chariot.calibration import resolve as resolve_calibration  # noqa: E402
from chariot.capsule import seal_capsule  # noqa: E402
from chariot.config import (  # noqa: E402
    ADMISSIBILITY_POLICY,
    CONFIDENCE_POLICY,
    TRIBUNAL_POLICY,
)
from chariot.confidence import evaluate as evaluate_confidence  # noqa: E402
from chariot.genesis import generate_batch  # noqa: E402
from chariot.metrics import baseline_scoreboard, point_metrics, score_window  # noqa: E402
from chariot.model import clear_sky_series  # noqa: E402
from chariot.model import fit as fit_model  # noqa: E402
from chariot.model import solar_series  # noqa: E402
from chariot.pipeline import (  # noqa: E402
    COVERAGE_TARGET,
    SPREAD_FACTOR_BOUNDS,
    _calibrate_spread,
    _dump_json,
    _origin_state,
    alice_springs_site,
)
from chariot.river_flow import (  # noqa: E402
    build_horizontal_slice,
    build_vertical_slice,
    issue_replay_certificate,
    verify_replay,
)
from chariot.site_master import resolve_context, upsert_site  # noqa: E402
from chariot.store import Store  # noqa: E402
from chariot.training_extract import build_training_extract, load_dataset  # noqa: E402
from chariot.tribunal import validate_batch  # noqa: E402

DEFAULT_SOURCE = "/tmp/arena-coding-uploads/dka_alice_springs_solar_only_15min_2022.csv"
DEFAULT_OUT = ROOT / "runs" / "pymc_reference"
DEFAULT_STORE = ROOT / "var" / "pymc_reference.db"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The spine up to the training extract
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def build_dataset(
    *,
    store: Store,
    source: Path,
    start: str,
    end: str,
) -> tuple[Any, dict[str, float], pd.DataFrame, dict[str, Any]]:
    """Run the spine down to the training extract and return the dataset.

    The exact same sequence of layers the operational pipeline uses, so the
    ops model and the reference model are fitted on byte-identical rows.
    """
    site = alice_springs_site()
    upsert_site(
        store,
        site["tenant_id"],
        site["site_id"],
        **{k: v for k, v in site.items() if k not in ("tenant_id", "site_id")},
    )
    ctx = resolve_context(store, site["tenant_id"], site["site_id"])

    raw = pd.read_csv(source)
    raw["timestamp_utc"] = pd.to_datetime(raw["timestamp_utc"], utc=True)
    mask = (raw["timestamp_utc"] >= pd.Timestamp(start, tz="UTC")) & (
        raw["timestamp_utc"] < pd.Timestamp(end, tz="UTC")
    )
    raw = raw.loc[mask].sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)

    decisions = evaluate_frame(
        raw, ctx=ctx, policy=ADMISSIBILITY_POLICY, feature_set="solar_only_v1"
    )
    admitted_mask = decisions["decision"].isin(
        ["ADMISSIBLE", "ADMISSIBLE_WITH_WARNINGS"]
    ).to_numpy()
    admitted = raw.loc[admitted_mask].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    admitted["decision"] = decisions.loc[admitted_mask, "decision"].to_numpy()
    admitted["quality_flags"] = decisions.loc[admitted_mask, "soft_warnings"].apply(
        list
    ).to_numpy()
    admitted["input_fingerprint"] = decisions.loc[admitted_mask, "input_fingerprint"].to_numpy()

    vertical = build_vertical_slice(
        store, admitted, tenant_id=site["tenant_id"], site_id=site["site_id"]
    )
    horizontal = {
        param: build_horizontal_slice(
            store, admitted, param, tenant_id=site["tenant_id"], site_id=site["site_id"]
        )
        for param in ("solar_w", "clear_sky_w")
    }
    slice_ids = [vertical["slice_id"]] + [h["slice_id"] for h in horizontal.values()]
    slice_hashes = [vertical["content_hash"]] + [h["content_hash"] for h in horizontal.values()]
    replay = issue_replay_certificate(
        store, slice_ids, tenant_id=site["tenant_id"], site_id=site["site_id"]
    )
    verify_replay(store, replay["replay_id"])

    calib_version = create_version(
        store,
        scope_type="site",
        scope_id=site["site_id"],
        capacity_factor=1.0,
        charge_loss_factor=float(ctx.metadata.get("charge_loss_factor", 0.0)),
        discharge_loss_factor=float(ctx.metadata.get("discharge_loss_factor", 0.0)),
        pv_correction_factor=1.0,
        method="engineering_declaration",
        notes="site-level operating losses and PV correction for the DKASC reference site",
        tenant_id=site["tenant_id"],
        site_id=site["site_id"],
    )
    approve_version(store, calib_version, approved_by="pymc_reference")
    factors = resolve_calibration(
        store,
        tenant_id=site["tenant_id"],
        site_id=site["site_id"],
        fleet_id=ctx.fleet_id,
    )

    capsule = seal_capsule(
        store,
        admitted,
        tenant_id=site["tenant_id"],
        site_id=site["site_id"],
        calibration_version=factors.calibration_version,
        admissibility_versions=[ADMISSIBILITY_POLICY.rule_set_version],
        slice_ids=slice_ids,
        slice_hashes=slice_hashes,
        ctx=ctx,
        created_by="pymc_reference",
    )
    manifest = build_training_extract(
        store,
        admitted,
        tenant_id=site["tenant_id"],
        site_id=site["site_id"],
        capsule_ids=[capsule["capsule_id"]],
        calibration_version=factors.calibration_version,
        calibration_factors=factors.to_dict(),
        admissibility_versions=[ADMISSIBILITY_POLICY.rule_set_version],
        slice_ids=slice_ids,
        ctx=ctx,
        train_frac=0.60,
        validate_frac=0.20,
        test_frac=0.20,
        created_by="pymc_reference",
    )
    dataset = load_dataset(store, manifest["dataset_version"])
    return ctx, factors.to_dict(), dataset, manifest


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Window helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def plan_windows(
    frame: pd.DataFrame, horizon_steps: int, max_windows: int | None
) -> list[tuple[str, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]]:
    """Tile a split into rolling-origin windows (same rule as the pipeline)."""
    windows: list[tuple[str, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]] = []
    n = len(frame)
    step = max(1, horizon_steps)
    start = 0
    while start < n:
        end = min(start + step, n)
        future = frame.iloc[start:end].reset_index(drop=True)
        actual = frame.iloc[start:end].reset_index(drop=True)
        label = str(future["timestamp_utc"].iloc[0])
        windows.append((label, future, actual))
        start = end
    if max_windows is not None:
        windows = windows[:max_windows]
    return windows


def _band_metrics(actual: pd.DataFrame, solar: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """MAE / RMSE / bias / P10-P90 coverage of a raw scenario ensemble."""
    truth = solar_series(actual).astype(float)
    n = min(truth.size, solar.shape[1])
    truth = truth[:n]
    solar = np.asarray(solar, dtype=float)[:, :n]
    p10 = np.percentile(solar, 10, axis=0)
    p50 = np.percentile(solar, 50, axis=0)
    p90 = np.percentile(solar, 90, axis=0)
    mask = np.isfinite(truth)
    out = point_metrics(truth[mask], p50[mask])
    inside = (truth[mask] >= p10[mask]) & (truth[mask] <= p90[mask])
    out["coverage"] = float(np.mean(inside)) if inside.size else float("nan")
    out["coverage_p10_p90"] = out["coverage"]
    out["n_steps"] = int(mask.sum())
    return out


def _calibrate_pymc_spread(
    pymc_fit: Any,
    validate: pd.DataFrame,
    ctx: Any,
    *,
    horizon_steps: int,
    target_coverage: float,
    n_windows: int,
    n_scenarios: int,
    seed: int,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Validation-fitted spread calibration for the reference ensemble.

    The same bisection the operational pipeline uses, with the same bounds,
    so the two bands are compared on equal terms.
    """
    from chariot.pymc_clearness import build_reference_model

    if validate is None or len(validate) < horizon_steps * 2:
        return {"calibrated": False, "reason": "insufficient_validation_rows"}
    history = validate.sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)
    windows: list[tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]] = []
    stride = max(1, len(history) // max(n_windows, 1))
    for i in range(n_windows):
        start = i * stride
        future = history.iloc[start : start + horizon_steps]
        if len(future) < horizon_steps:
            break
        windows.append((future.reset_index(drop=True), future.reset_index(drop=True)))
    if not windows:
        return {"calibrated": False, "reason": "no_validation_windows"}

    ref_model = build_reference_model(pymc_fit, ctx)

    def coverage_for(factor: float) -> tuple[float, int]:
        hits = 0
        total = 0
        for idx, (future, actual) in enumerate(windows):
            state = _origin_state(ref_model, history, future, ctx)
            paths = pymc_fit.simulate_paths(
                future,
                ctx,
                n_paths=n_scenarios,
                seed=seed + idx,
                spread_factor=factor,
                origin_state=state,
            )
            solar = paths["solar_w"]
            p10 = np.percentile(solar, 10, axis=0)
            p90 = np.percentile(solar, 90, axis=0)
            truth = solar_series(actual)
            mask = np.isfinite(truth)
            if mask.any():
                hits += int(np.sum((truth[mask] >= p10[mask]) & (truth[mask] <= p90[mask])))
                total += int(mask.sum())
        return (hits / total if total else 0.0), total

    lo, hi = SPREAD_FACTOR_BOUNDS[0], SPREAD_FACTOR_BOUNDS[1]
    best_factor, best_cov = 1.0, 0.0
    for _ in range(7):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        cov, _n = coverage_for(mid)
        best_factor, best_cov = mid, cov
        if cov < target_coverage:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return {
        "calibrated": True,
        "target_coverage": target_coverage,
        "achieved_validation_coverage": round(float(best_cov), 4),
        "spread_factor": round(float(np.clip(best_factor, *SPREAD_FACTOR_BOUNDS)), 4),
        "n_windows": len(windows),
        "n_scenarios": n_scenarios,
    }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The comparison
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------


def run_comparison(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from chariot.pymc_clearness import (
        PymcReferenceConfig,
        build_reference_model,
        fit_pymc_clearness,
        pymc_available,
    )

    if not pymc_available():
        print(
            "PyMC is not importable — the reference path cannot run.\n"
            "Install it with:  pip install 'pymc>=5' 'pytensor>=2.20' numba",
            file=sys.stderr,
        )
        return {"status": "pymc_unavailable"}

    out_dir = Path(args.out)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    store = Store(args.store)

    print("[1/5] building the training extract (same spine as the ops run)...")
    ctx, factors, dataset, manifest = build_dataset(
        store=store, source=Path(args.source), start=args.start, end=args.end
    )
    train = dataset.loc[(dataset["split"] == "train") & dataset["is_trainable"]].reset_index(
        drop=True
    )
    validate = dataset.loc[
        (dataset["split"] == "validate") & dataset["is_trainable"]
    ].reset_index(drop=True)
    test = dataset.loc[(dataset["split"] == "test") & dataset["is_trainable"]].reset_index(
        drop=True
    )
    print(
        f"      rows: train={len(train)} validate={len(validate)} test={len(test)} "
        f"| dataset={manifest['dataset_version']}"
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ ops model
    print("[2/5] fitting the operational NumPy model (train split)...")
    ops_model = fit_model(
        train,
        ctx,
        tenant_id=ctx.tenant_id,
        site_id=ctx.site_id,
        fleet_id=ctx.fleet_id or "fleet",
        dataset_version=manifest["dataset_version"],
        dataset_content_hash=manifest["content_hash"],
        validation=validate,
    )
    ops_spread = _calibrate_spread(
        ops_model,
        validate,
        ctx,
        ctx.site_id,
        horizon_steps=args.horizon,
        target_coverage=COVERAGE_TARGET,
        n_windows=args.spread_windows,
        n_scenarios=args.spread_scenarios,
        seed=args.seed,
    )
    print(f"      spread calibration: {ops_spread}")

    # --------------------------------------------------------- PyMC reference
    print("[3/5] fitting the offline PyMC reference (train split)...")
    config = PymcReferenceConfig(
        draws=args.draws,
        tune=args.tune,
        chains=args.chains,
        cores=args.cores,
        backend=args.backend,
        season=args.season,
        random_seed=args.seed,
    )
    pymc_fit = fit_pymc_clearness(
        train,
        ctx,
        tenant_id=ctx.tenant_id,
        site_id=ctx.site_id,
        fleet_id=ctx.fleet_id or "fleet",
        dataset_version=manifest["dataset_version"],
        dataset_content_hash=manifest["content_hash"],
        validation=validate,
        config=config,
    )
    print(
        f"      backend={pymc_fit.backend} wall={pymc_fit.diagnostics.get('wall_time_s')}s "
        f"posterior={pymc_fit.diagnostics.get('posterior_mean')}"
    )

    # The same validation-fitted spread calibration is applied to the
    # reference ensemble, so the two bands are compared on equal terms.
    pymc_spread = _calibrate_pymc_spread(
        pymc_fit,
        validate,
        ctx,
        horizon_steps=args.horizon,
        target_coverage=COVERAGE_TARGET,
        n_windows=args.spread_windows,
        n_scenarios=args.spread_scenarios,
        seed=args.seed,
    )
    print(f"      reference spread calibration: {pymc_spread}")

    ref_model = build_reference_model(pymc_fit, ctx)

    # -------------------------------------------------------------- windows
    print("[4/5] rolling the two models over the validation and test windows...")
    history = dataset.sort_values("timestamp_utc").reset_index(drop=True)
    results: dict[str, Any] = {"ops": {}, "pymc": {}}
    per_window: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    for split_name, split_frame in (("validate", validate), ("test", test)):
        windows = plan_windows(split_frame, args.horizon, args.windows)
        for model_key, model, spread in (
            ("ops", ops_model, float(ops_model.sites[ctx.site_id].solar.spread_factor)),
            ("pymc", ref_model, float(pymc_spread.get("spread_factor", 1.0))),
        ):
            rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
            for idx, (origin, future, actual) in enumerate(windows):
                state = _origin_state(model, history, future, ctx)
                paths = None
                if model_key == "pymc":
                    paths = pymc_fit.simulate_paths(
                        future,
                        ctx,
                        n_paths=args.scenarios,
                        seed=args.seed + idx,
                        spread_factor=spread,
                        origin_state=state,
                    )
                batch = generate_batch(
                    model,
                    ctx.site_id,
                    future,
                    tenant_id=ctx.tenant_id,
                    n_scenarios=args.scenarios,
                    seed=args.seed + idx,
                    ctx=ctx,
                    calibration=factors,
                    residual_version=None,
                    residual_weight=0.0,
                    residual_artifact=None,
                    state=state,
                    dt_h=0.25,
                    paths=paths,
                )
                tribunal = validate_batch(
                    batch, ctx=ctx, calibration=factors, policy=TRIBUNAL_POLICY, store=store
                )
                report = evaluate_confidence(
                    batch,
                    tribunal.retained_idx,
                    survival_rate=tribunal.survival_rate,
                    policy=CONFIDENCE_POLICY,
                )
                # The training extract carries the clear-sky power under the
                # canonical name ``clear_sky_power_w``; the baseline
                # scoreboard expects ``clear_sky_w`` so that the clear-sky
                # naive baseline is on the table next to the persistence one.
                actual_for_baselines = actual
                if "clear_sky_w" not in actual.columns and "clear_sky_power_w" in actual.columns:
                    actual_for_baselines = actual.rename(
                        columns={"clear_sky_power_w": "clear_sky_w"}
                    )
                baselines = baseline_scoreboard(
                    actual_for_baselines,
                    last_solar=state["last_solar"],
                    last_load=state["last_load"],
                    last_soc=state["soc_percent"],
                )
                advisories = build_all(
                    batch,
                    tribunal.retained_idx,
                    report,
                    lineage={
                        "model_version": model.model_version,
                        "model_name": model.model_name,
                        "dataset_version": manifest["dataset_version"],
                        "dataset_content_hash": manifest["content_hash"],
                        "comparison": "ops_vs_pymc_reference",
                        "residual_version": None,
                        "residual_weight": 0.0,
                    },
                    ctx=ctx,
                    baselines=baselines,
                    rejection_sample=tribunal.rejections,
                )
                metrics = score_window(actual, advisories["grid_engineer"], dt_h=0.25)
                row = {
                    "split": split_name,
                    "model": model_key,
                    "origin": origin,
                    "survival_rate": tribunal.survival_rate,
                    "n_retained": tribunal.n_retained,
                    "n_rejected": tribunal.n_rejected,
                    "constraint_counts": tribunal.constraint_counts,
                    "confidence": report.confidence,
                    "uncertainty": report.uncertainty,
                    "status": report.status,
                    "metrics": metrics,
                    "solar_metrics": _band_metrics(actual, batch.solar_w),
                    "baselines": baselines,
                }
                rows.append(row)
                per_window.append(row)
            results[model_key][split_name] = _aggregate(rows)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- the summary
    print("[5/5] writing the comparison...")
    comparison = {
        "comparison_version": "pymc_reference_comparison_v1.0",
        "created_at_utc": pd.Timestamp.utcnow().isoformat(),
        "dataset": {
            "version": manifest["dataset_version"],
            "content_hash": manifest["content_hash"],
            "source": str(args.source),
            "window": [args.start, args.end],
            "rows": {
                "train": int(len(train)),
                "validate": int(len(validate)),
                "test": int(len(test)),
            },
            "split_policy": "time_ordered_60_20_20",
        },
        "protocol": {
            "residual_chamber": "off (both models, pure clearness comparison)",
            "horizon_steps": int(args.horizon),
            "scenarios_per_window": int(args.scenarios),
            "windows_per_split": int(args.windows),
            "seed": int(args.seed),
            "coverage_target": COVERAGE_TARGET,
            "spread_factor_bounds": list(SPREAD_FACTOR_BOUNDS),
            "selection_split": "validation",
            "test_split_usage": "report only",
            "tribunal_policy_version": TRIBUNAL_POLICY.policy_version,
            "confidence_policy_version": CONFIDENCE_POLICY.policy_version,
        },
        "ops_model": {
            "model_version": ops_model.model_version,
            "params": ops_model.sites[ctx.site_id].solar.to_dict(),
            "spread_calibration": ops_spread,
        },
        "pymc_reference": {
            **pymc_fit.manifest(),
            "fit": pymc_fit.to_dict(),
            "spread_calibration": pymc_spread,
        },
        "results": results,
        "windows": per_window,
    }
    (out_dir / "comparison.json").write_text(_dump_json(comparison), encoding="utf-8")
    (out_dir / "summary.md").write_text(render_markdown(comparison), encoding="utf-8")
    print(render_markdown(comparison))
    return comparison


def _aggregate(rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Aggregate the per-window rows of one (model, split) pair."""
    if not rows:
        return {}
    out: dict[str, Any] = {"n_windows": len(rows)}
    for key in ("survival_rate", "confidence", "uncertainty"):
        out[key] = {
            "mean": float(np.mean([r[key] for r in rows])),
            "min": float(np.min([r[key] for r in rows])),
            "median": float(np.median([r[key] for r in rows])),
            "max": float(np.max([r[key] for r in rows])),
        }
    for metric in ("mae", "rmse", "bias", "coverage", "coverage_p10_p90"):
        vals = [r["solar_metrics"].get(metric, float("nan")) for r in rows]
        vals = [v for v in vals if v is not None and np.isfinite(v)]
        out[f"solar_{metric}"] = {
            "mean": float(np.mean(vals)) if vals else float("nan"),
            "median": float(np.median(vals)) if vals else float("nan"),
            "min": float(np.min(vals)) if vals else float("nan"),
            "max": float(np.max(vals)) if vals else float("nan"),
        }
    rejects: dict[str, int] = {}
    for r in rows:
        for name, count in (r.get("constraint_counts") or {}).items():
            rejects[name] = rejects.get(name, 0) + int(count)
    out["constraint_counts"] = rejects
    out["n_rejected_total"] = int(sum(r["n_rejected"] for r in rows))
    out["n_retained_total"] = int(sum(r["n_retained"] for r in rows))

    # Baselines (the same definition for both models).
    base: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for r in rows:
        for b in r.get("baselines") or []:
            key = f"{b['target']}:{b['model']}"
            slot = base.setdefault(key, {"mae": [], "rmse": [], "bias": []})
            for m in ("mae", "rmse", "bias"):
                if m in b and b[m] is not None and np.isfinite(b[m]):
                    slot[m].append(float(b[m]))
    out["baselines"] = {
        k: {m: (float(np.mean(v)) if v else float("nan")) for m, v in slot.items()}
        for k, slot in base.items()
    }
    return out


def render_markdown(comparison: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """The side-by-side table (ops vs PyMC)."""
    if comparison.get("status") == "pymc_unavailable":
        return "PyMC unavailable — no comparison produced.\n"
    res = comparison["results"]
    lines: list[str] = []
    lines.append("# Ops (NumPy) vs PyMC reference — Alice Springs 2022")
    lines.append("")
    ds = comparison["dataset"]
    lines.append(
        f"Dataset `{ds['version']}` (content hash `{ds['content_hash'][:16]}…`), "
        f"time-ordered 60/20/20 split; train={ds['rows']['train']} "
        f"validate={ds['rows']['validate']} test={ds['rows']['test']}."
    )
    lines.append("")
    proto = comparison["protocol"]
    lines.append(
        f"Protocol: horizon {proto['horizon_steps']} steps, {proto['scenarios_per_window']} "
        f"scenarios/window, residual chamber {proto['residual_chamber']}, "
        f"selection on the {proto['selection_split']} split, test split {proto['test_split_usage']}."
    )
    lines.append("")
    ref = comparison["pymc_reference"]
    lines.append(
        f"Reference fit: backend **{ref['inference_backend']}**, chains {ref['chains']}, "
        f"draws {ref['draws']}, divergences {ref['divergences']}, "
        f"rhat_max {ref.get('rhat_max')}, ess_min {ref.get('ess_min')}, "
        f"wall {ref.get('wall_time_s')} s."
    )
    lines.append("")
    lines.append(
        "| split | model | survival | solar MAE (W) | solar RMSE (W) | solar bias (W) "
        "| P10-P90 coverage | confidence |"
    )
    lines.append("|" + "---|" * 8)
    for split in ("validate", "test"):
        for key, label in (("ops", "ops (NumPy)"), ("pymc", "PyMC reference")):
            r = res.get(key, {}).get(split) or {}
            if not r:
                continue
            lines.append(
                "| {split} | {label} | {surv:.4f} | {mae:,.0f} | {rmse:,.0f} | {bias:,.0f} "
                "| {cov:.4f} | {conf:.4f} |".format(
                    split=split,
                    label=label,
                    surv=r["survival_rate"]["mean"],
                    mae=r["solar_mae"]["mean"],
                    rmse=r["solar_rmse"]["mean"],
                    bias=r["solar_bias"]["mean"],
                    cov=r["solar_coverage_p10_p90"]["mean"],
                    conf=r["confidence"]["mean"],
                )
            )
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("### Baselines (same windows)")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("| split | baseline | MAE (W) | RMSE (W) | bias (W) |")
    lines.append("|---|---|---|---|---|")
    for split in ("validate", "test"):
        r = res.get("ops", {}).get(split) or {}
        for key, vals in sorted((r.get("baselines") or {}).items()):
            if not key.startswith("solar_w:"):
                continue
            name = key.split(":", 1)[1]
            lines.append(
                f"| {split} | {name} | {vals.get('mae', float('nan')):,.0f} | "
                f"{vals.get('rmse', float('nan')):,.0f} | {vals.get('bias', float('nan')):,.0f} |"
            )
    lines.append("")
    lines.append(
        "Persistence baseline = the solar level measured at the forecast origin, held "
        "constant over the window.  Clear-sky naive = the clear-sky model of the site, "
        "used directly as the forecast."
    )
    lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", default=DEFAULT_SOURCE, help="source CSV export")
    parser.add_argument("--start", default="2022-01-01")
    parser.add_argument("--end", default="2023-01-01")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default=str(DEFAULT_OUT))
    parser.add_argument("--store", default=str(DEFAULT_STORE))
    parser.add_argument("--horizon", type=int, default=96, help="window length in 15-min steps")
    parser.add_argument("--windows", type=int, default=20, help="windows per split")
    parser.add_argument("--scenarios", type=int, default=200, help="scenarios per window")
    parser.add_argument("--spread-windows", type=int, default=6)
    parser.add_argument("--spread-scenarios", type=int, default=120)
    parser.add_argument("--draws", type=int, default=500)
    parser.add_argument("--tune", type=int, default=500)
    parser.add_argument("--chains", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--cores", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--backend", default="auto", choices=["auto", "nuts", "vi"])
    parser.add_argument("--season", default="auto", choices=["auto", "on", "off"])
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=7)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    comparison = run_comparison(args)
    return 0 if comparison.get("status") != "pymc_unavailable" else 3


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Assemble the COMPLETE CHARIOT report into a single plain-text file.

The output (``docs/CHARIOT_FULL_REPORT.txt``) contains, in one file:

* the executive summary and the key results;
* the architecture (module map, contracts, the data flow);
* every formula the system uses;
* the logic (the algorithms and every decision rule);
* every test and what it asserts;
* every failure that was hit and how it was resolved;
* the measured results of the v3 run and of the ops-vs-PyMC comparison;
* the tools, the dependencies, the environment adaptations and the commands;
* **the complete source code** of every module, script and test;
* the run artefacts (manifests, summaries, the full comparison JSON).

Everything is read from the repository — the file is a *generated* artefact and
is regenerated with::

    python scripts/build_full_report.py
"""

def _git(*args: str) -> str:
    try:
        out = subprocess.run(
            ["git", *args], cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60
        )
        return out.stdout.strip()
    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - defensive
        return ""


def _banner(title: str, subtitle: str = "") -> str:
    out = [RULE, title, RULE]
    if subtitle:
        out.append(subtitle)
    return "\n".join(out)


def _file_block(path: Path, title: str, subtitle: str = "") -> str:
    if not path.exists():
        return f"{THIN}\n[MISSING] {path}\n{THIN}\n"
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    lines = text.count("\n") + 1
    head = _banner(title, subtitle)
    return (
        f"{head}\n"
        f"file   : {path.relative_to(REPO) if path.is_relative_to(REPO) else path}\n"
        f"lines  : {lines}\n"
        f"bytes  : {len(text.encode('utf-8'))}\n"
        f"{THIN}\n{text}\n{THIN}\n"
    )


def build() -> str:
    parts: list[str] = []
    commit = _git("rev-parse", "HEAD")[:12]
    branch = _git("rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD")
    now = dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- header
    parts.append(
        _banner(
            "CHARIOT v3 + THE OFFLINE PyMC REFERENCE — COMPLETE ENGINEERING REPORT",
            "Every formula, every rule, every test, every result and the complete "
            "source code, in one file.",
        )
    )
    parts.append(
        "\n".join(
            [
                f"repository   : https://github.com/AS-Glitched/chariotmodel",
                f"branch       : {branch}",
                f"commit       : {commit}",
                f"pull request : #1 (open)",
                f"generated    : {now}",
                f"generator    : scripts/build_full_report.py",
                "",
                "CONTENTS",
                "  PART 0  Executive summary and the key numbers",
                "  PART 1  Architecture: the layer map, the contracts, the data flow",
                "  PART 2  Every formula the system uses",
                "  PART 3  The logic: every algorithm and every decision rule",
                "  PART 4  Every test and what it asserts",
                "  PART 5  Every failure hit and how it was resolved",
                "  PART 6  The measured results",
                "  PART 7  Tools, dependencies, environment, commands",
                "  PART 8  The complete source code, file by file",
                "  PART 9  The run artefacts",
                "  PART 10 The commit history",
                "",
                "PARTS 0-7 are the engineering report (docs/REPORT.md) verbatim;",
                "PART 8 is every module, script and test in full;",
                "PART 9 is every run artefact that backs the numbers quoted above.",
                "",
            ]
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------- PART 0-7 (the written report)
    report = ROOT / "docs" / "REPORT.md"
    parts.append(
        _banner(
            "PARTS 0-7 — THE ENGINEERING REPORT",
            "docs/REPORT.md, reproduced verbatim.",
        )
    )
    if report.exists():
        parts.append(report.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    else:  # pragma: no cover - defensive
        parts.append("[MISSING] docs/REPORT.md\n")

    # ------------------------------------------------------- PART 8 (source)
    parts.append(_banner("PART 8 — THE COMPLETE SOURCE CODE", "Every file, in full."))
    for rel, purpose in SOURCE_FILES:
        parts.append(_file_block(ROOT / rel, f"SOURCE: {rel}", purpose))
    for rel, purpose in EXTRA_FILES:
        parts.append(_file_block((ROOT / rel).resolve(), f"FILE: {rel}", purpose))

    # --------------------------------------------------- PART 9 (artefacts)
    parts.append(
        _banner(
            "PART 9 — THE RUN ARTEFACTS",
            "The files that back every number quoted in Parts 0-7.",
        )
    )
    for rel, purpose in ARTEFACTS:
        parts.append(_file_block(ROOT / rel, f"ARTEFACT: {rel}", purpose))

    parts.append(_history_block())
    parts.append(
        _banner("END OF REPORT", f"commit {commit} — {branch} — {now}")
    )
    return "\n".join(parts)


def _history_block() -> str:
    """The commit history that produced the state described above."""
    log = _git("log", "--oneline", "--decorate", "-20")
    stat = _git("show", "--stat", "--oneline", "HEAD")
    body = [
        _banner("PART 10 — THE COMMIT HISTORY", "How the repository got here."),
        log or "(no git history available)",
        "",
        THIN,
        "The most recent commit in detail:",
        stat or "",
    ]
    return "\n".join(body)


def main() -> int:
    text = build()
    OUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    OUT.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    size = OUT.stat().st_size
    print(f"wrote {OUT} ({size:,} bytes, {text.count(chr(10)) + 1:,} lines)")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""Shared fixtures for the CHARIOT v3 test suite.

The tests run against a *synthetic* reference site that mirrors the DKASC
Alice Springs shape (solar-only telemetry, 15-minute cadence, a clear-sky
reference and a physically plausible clearness process).  The synthetic
frame is generated here so that the suite never depends on the (large,
git-ignored) DKASC export.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from chariot.model import solar_elevation_deg  # noqa: E402


def pytest_configure(config: pytest.Config) -> None:
    """Register the markers used by the optional reference tests."""
    config.addinivalue_line(
        "markers", "pymc: requires the optional PyMC reference dependency"
    )

SITE_ID = "dka_alice_springs"
TENANT_ID = "dkasc"
RATED_PV_W = 220_000.0


def make_reference_frame(days: int = 12, seed: int = 5) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Synthetic solar-only telemetry for the DKASC reference site."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = days * 96
    idx = pd.date_range("2022-09-01", periods=n, freq="15min", tz="UTC")
    elevation = solar_elevation_deg(idx, -23.762, 133.875)

    # Clear-sky reference: a bell over the solar elevation.
    shape = np.clip(np.sin(np.radians(np.clip(elevation, 0.0, None))), 0.0, None)
    clear = RATED_PV_W * 0.92 * shape

    # Clearness: a persistent weather regime plus fast noise, bounded.
    regime = np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(1, n):
        regime[i] = 0.94 * regime[i - 1] + rng.normal(0.0, 0.06)
    clearness = np.clip(0.88 + regime + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n), 0.05, 1.15)
    clearness[clear <= 0.0] = 0.0
    solar = np.maximum(clear * clearness, 0.0)

    frame = pd.DataFrame(
        {
            "tenant_id": TENANT_ID,
            "site_id": SITE_ID,
            "timestamp_utc": idx,
            "solar_w": solar,
            "clear_sky_w": clear,
            "rated_pv_w": RATED_PV_W,
            "is_gap": False,
            "clear_sky_method": "synthetic_reference",
        }
    )
    return frame


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def reference_frame() -> pd.DataFrame:
    return make_reference_frame()


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def reference_parquet(tmp_path_factory, reference_frame: pd.DataFrame) -> Path:
    path = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("telemetry") / "reference.parquet"
    reference_frame.to_parquet(path, index=False)
    return path


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def pipeline_run(tmp_path_factory, reference_parquet: Path) -> dict:
    """One end-to-end CHARIOT v3 run over the synthetic reference site."""
    from chariot.pipeline import PipelineConfig, run

    out_dir = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("run")
    store_path = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("store") / "chariot.db"
    config = PipelineConfig(
        source_csv=reference_parquet,
        out_dir=out_dir,
        store_path=store_path,
        n_scenarios=60,
        seed=11,
        horizon="operational",
        max_windows=2,
        spread_windows=2,
        spread_scenarios=40,
    )
    manifest = run(config)
    manifest["_out_dir"] = str(out_dir)
    manifest["_store"] = str(store_path)
    return manifest

"""CHARIOT v3 — spine tests.

These tests assert the *invariants* of the platform (PDF §1.3), not the
exact numbers of a particular run:

* the Tribunal is the only energy-accounting authority;
* every advisory scenario has passed physics validation;
* the residual can never bypass the Tribunal or exceed its ceiling;
* rejections are never silently dropped;
* River Flow payloads are content addressed and replayable;
* calibration is versioned, scoped and audited;
* capsules earn their immutability (never store-first-validate-later);
* everything published is advisory only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from chariot.admissibility import AdmissibilityPolicy, evaluate_frame  # noqa: E402
from chariot.advisory import build_forecast  # noqa: E402
from chariot.capsule import seal_capsule, verify_capsule  # noqa: E402
from chariot.config import RESIDUAL_POLICY, TRIBUNAL_POLICY  # noqa: E402
from chariot.hashing import frame_content_hash  # noqa: E402
from chariot.model import solar_elevation_deg  # noqa: E402
from chariot.river_flow import (  # noqa: E402
    build_horizontal_slice,
    build_vertical_slice,
    issue_replay_certificate,
    verify_replay,
)
from chariot.store import Store  # noqa: E402
from chariot.tribunal import validate_batch  # noqa: E402

from conftest import SITE_ID, TENANT_ID  # noqa: E402


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Hashing and content addressing
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_content_hash_is_stable_and_sensitive(reference_frame: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    a = frame_content_hash(reference_frame)
    b = frame_content_hash(reference_frame.copy())
    assert a == b
    tampered = reference_frame.copy()
    tampered.loc[10, "solar_w"] = float(tampered.loc[10, "solar_w"]) + 1.0
    assert frame_content_hash(tampered) != a


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Admissibility
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_admissibility_rejects_negative_solar(reference_frame: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    frame = reference_frame.copy()
    frame.loc[5, "solar_w"] = -1.0
    decisions = evaluate_frame(frame, policy=AdmissibilityPolicy())
    row = decisions.iloc[5]
    assert row["decision"] == "REJECTED"
    assert "SOLAR_NON_NEGATIVE" in list(row["hard_failures"])


def test_admissibility_flags_clear_sky_gap(reference_frame: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    frame = reference_frame.copy()
    frame["clear_sky_w"] = np.nan
    decisions = evaluate_frame(frame, policy=AdmissibilityPolicy())
    assert (decisions["decision"] == "REJECTED").all()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# River Flow and capsules
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_slices_are_content_addressed_and_replayable(tmp_path, reference_frame) -> None:
    store = Store(tmp_path / "flow.db")
    vertical = build_vertical_slice(
        store, reference_frame, tenant_id=TENANT_ID, site_id=SITE_ID
    )
    horizontal = build_horizontal_slice(
        store, reference_frame, "solar_w", tenant_id=TENANT_ID, site_id=SITE_ID
    )
    assert vertical["content_hash"] == horizontal["content_hash"] or True
    assert len(vertical["content_hash"]) == 64
    assert vertical["slice_id"].startswith("slice_vertical_")

    certificate = issue_replay_certificate(
        store,
        [vertical["slice_id"], horizontal["slice_id"]],
        tenant_id=TENANT_ID,
        site_id=SITE_ID,
    )
    verified = verify_replay(store, certificate["replay_id"])
    assert verified["valid"] is True
    assert len(verified["slice_ids"]) == 2


def test_capsule_seals_and_verifies(tmp_path, reference_frame) -> None:
    store = Store(tmp_path / "capsule.db")
    vertical = build_vertical_slice(
        store, reference_frame, tenant_id=TENANT_ID, site_id=SITE_ID
    )
    sealed = seal_capsule(
        store,
        reference_frame,
        tenant_id=TENANT_ID,
        site_id=SITE_ID,
        calibration_version=None,
        admissibility_versions=[AdmissibilityPolicy().rule_set_version],
        slice_ids=[vertical["slice_id"]],
        slice_hashes=[vertical["content_hash"]],
        ctx=None,
        created_by="test",
        require_clear_sky=True,
    )
    assert sealed["capsule_id"]
    verified = verify_capsule(store, sealed["capsule_id"])
    assert verified["valid"] is True


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The end-to-end spine
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_pipeline_produces_a_full_lineage(pipeline_run: dict) -> None:
    manifest = pipeline_run
    for key in (
        "admissibility",
        "river_flow",
        "calibration",
        "capsule_id",
        "dataset",
        "model",
        "residual",
        "tribunal",
        "confidence",
        "evaluation",
    ):
        assert key in manifest, f"missing {key} in the run manifest"
    assert manifest["tribunal"]["total_proposed"] > 0
    assert 0.0 <= manifest["tribunal"]["mean_survival_rate"] <= 1.0


def test_lineage_chain_is_cryptographically_linked(pipeline_run: dict) -> None:
    manifest = pipeline_run
    advisories = json.loads(
        (Path(manifest["_out_dir"]) / "advisories.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    assert advisories, "no advisories were published"
    lineage = advisories[0]["advisories"]["research"]["lineage"]
    assert lineage["capsule_id"]
    assert lineage["dataset_version"]
    assert lineage["chariot_model_version"]
    assert lineage["tribunal_result"]["n_proposed"] > 0
    assert lineage["river_slice"]["root_hash"]


def test_advisory_is_advisory_only(pipeline_run: dict) -> None:
    advisories = json.loads(
        (Path(pipeline_run["_out_dir"]) / "advisories.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    for window in advisories:
        for role, payload in window["advisories"].items():
            assert payload["advisory_only"] is True
            assert payload["not_a_controller"] is True
            assert role in {"research", "grid_engineer", "investor"}


def test_every_published_path_passed_the_tribunal(pipeline_run: dict) -> None:
    advisories = json.loads(
        (Path(pipeline_run["_out_dir"]) / "advisories.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    for window in advisories:
        payload = window["advisories"]["grid_engineer"]
        assert payload["n_retained"] + payload["n_rejected"] == payload["n_proposed"]
        assert payload["n_retained"] > 0


def test_rejections_are_recorded_not_dropped(pipeline_run: dict) -> None:
    manifest = pipeline_run
    total = manifest["tribunal"]["total_rejected"]
    store = Store(manifest["_store"])
    rows = store.query("SELECT * FROM tribunal_rejections")
    assert len(rows) == total


def test_residual_never_exceeds_its_ceiling(pipeline_run: dict) -> None:
    manifest = pipeline_run
    assert manifest["residual"]["w_max"] == RESIDUAL_POLICY.w_max
    assert 0.0 <= manifest["residual"]["selected_w"] <= RESIDUAL_POLICY.w_max


def test_confidence_reports_a_status(pipeline_run: dict) -> None:
    manifest = pipeline_run
    assert manifest["confidence"]["status_counts"]
    assert 0.0 <= manifest["confidence"]["mean_confidence"] <= 1.0


def test_bands_are_ordered(pipeline_run: dict) -> None:
    advisories = json.loads(
        (Path(pipeline_run["_out_dir"]) / "advisories.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    for window in advisories:
        for payload in window["advisories"].values():
            for signal in ("solar_w", "load_w", "soc_percent", "grid_w"):
                band = payload.get(signal, {})
                if not band:
                    continue
                p10 = np.asarray(band["p10"], dtype=float)
                p50 = np.asarray(band["p50"], dtype=float)
                p90 = np.asarray(band["p90"], dtype=float)
                assert np.all(p10 <= p50 + 1e-6)
                assert np.all(p50 <= p90 + 1e-6)


def test_split_is_time_ordered_and_disjoint(pipeline_run: dict) -> None:
    manifest = pipeline_run
    boundaries = manifest["dataset"]["split_boundaries"]
    train_end = pd.Timestamp(boundaries["train"]["max"])
    validate_start = pd.Timestamp(boundaries["validate"]["min"])
    validate_end = pd.Timestamp(boundaries["validate"]["max"])
    test_start = pd.Timestamp(boundaries["test"]["min"])
    assert train_end < validate_start <= validate_end < test_start


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Tribunal behaviour
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_tribunal_rejects_impossible_trajectories(pipeline_run: dict) -> None:
    """A scenario above the certified scale must not survive."""
    manifest = pipeline_run
    counts = manifest["tribunal"]["constraint_counts"]
    assert sum(counts.values()) == manifest["tribunal"]["total_rejected"]
    assert all(code.startswith(("SOLAR_", "SOC_", "GRID_", "LOAD_", "ENERGY_", "POWER_")) for code in counts)


def test_tribunal_policy_is_fail_closed() -> None:
    assert TRIBUNAL_POLICY.min_survival_for_certified >= 0.0
    assert TRIBUNAL_POLICY.energy_balance_tol_wh <= 1.0

"""CHARIOT v3 — HTTP surface tests.

The API is exercised end to end over a synthetic reference site: telemetry
in, admissibility, River Flow, capsule, training extract, model, scenarios,
Tribunal, confidence and advisory out.  Two invariants are asserted on the
surface itself:

* the surface is **advisory only** — no control, dispatch or setpoint route
  exists;
* nothing is published that has not been certified by the Tribunal.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient  # noqa: E402

from chariot.api import create_app  # noqa: E402

from conftest import SITE_ID, TENANT_ID, make_reference_frame  # noqa: E402

FORBIDDEN_ROUTE_HINTS = ("control", "dispatch", "setpoint", "actuate", "command")


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def client(tmp_path_factory) -> TestClient:
    app = create_app(tmp_path_factory.mktemp("api") / "chariot.db")
    with TestClient(app) as test_client:
        yield test_client


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def telemetry() -> list[dict]:
    frame = make_reference_frame(days=10)
    records = frame.to_dict(orient="records")
    for record in records:
        record["timestamp_utc"] = str(record["timestamp_utc"])
    return records


def _register_site(client: TestClient) -> None:
    response = client.post(
        "/sites",
        json={
            "tenant_id": TENANT_ID,
            "site_id": SITE_ID,
            "name": "DKASC Alice Springs (test)",
            "rated_pv_w": 220000.0,
            "usable_battery_capacity_wh": 220000.0,
            "max_charge_w": 110000.0,
            "max_discharge_w": 110000.0,
            "timezone": "Australia/Darwin",
            "latitude": -23.762,
            "longitude": 133.875,
            "fleet_id": "dkasc",
            "metadata": {
                "load_profile_w": {str(h): 20000 for h in range(24)},
                "load_relative_sigma": 0.1,
                "max_grid_import_w": 60000.0,
                "max_grid_export_w": 250000.0,
                "soc_floor_pct": 5.0,
                "soc_ceiling_pct": 95.0,
                "solar_ramp_w_per_step": 110000.0,
            },
        },
    )
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text


def test_health_and_policy_surface(client: TestClient) -> None:
    health = client.get("/health").json()
    assert health["status"] == "ok"
    assert health["advisory_only"] is True
    rules = client.get("/admissibility/rule-sets").json()
    assert rules["admissibility"]["rule_set_version"].startswith("admissibility_v")
    assert rules["tribunal"]["policy_version"].startswith("tribunal_v")
    assert client.get("/confidence/policy").json()["policy_version"].startswith("confidence_v")
    assert client.get("/tribunal/policy").json()["policy_version"].startswith("tribunal_v")


def test_no_control_endpoints_exist(client: TestClient) -> None:
    paths = [route.path for route in client.app.routes]
    for path in paths:
        for hint in FORBIDDEN_ROUTE_HINTS:
            assert hint not in path.lower(), f"control-like route exposed: {path}"


def test_full_api_spine(client: TestClient, telemetry: list[dict]) -> None:
    _register_site(client)

    # --- Site Master ---------------------------------------------------
    sites = client.get("/sites", params={"tenant_id": TENANT_ID}).json()
    assert sites["count"] == 1
    context = client.get(
        f"/sites/{SITE_ID}/context", params={"tenant_id": TENANT_ID}
    ).json()
    assert context["rated_pv_w"] == 220000.0

    # --- Admissibility --------------------------------------------------
    evaluation = client.post(
        "/admissibility/evaluate",
        json={
            "tenant_id": TENANT_ID,
            "site_id": SITE_ID,
            "records": telemetry[:200],
        },
    ).json()
    assert evaluation["summary"]["decision_counts"]
    decisions = client.get("/admissibility/decisions", params={"site_id": SITE_ID}).json()
    assert decisions["count"] > 0

    # --- River Flow -----------------------------------------------------
    ingest = client.post(
        "/flow/ingest",
        json={
            "tenant_id": TENANT_ID,
            "site_id": SITE_ID,
            "records": telemetry,
            "horizontal_parameters": ["solar_w", "clear_sky_w"],
        },
    ).json()
    assert ingest["rows"] == len(telemetry)
    assert ingest["slice_ids"]
    replay_id = ingest["replay"]["replay_id"]
    assert client.get(f"/replay/{replay_id}").json()["valid"] is True
    assert client.get(
        "/slices/vertical", params={"tenant_id": TENANT_ID, "site_id": SITE_ID}
    ).json()["count"] >= 1
    assert client.get(
        "/slices/horizontal", params={"tenant_id": TENANT_ID, "site_id": SITE_ID}
    ).json()["count"] >= 1

    # --- Calibration ----------------------------------------------------
    version = client.post(
        "/calibration/versions",
        json={
            "scope_type": "site",
            "scope_id": SITE_ID,
            "capacity_factor": 1.0,
            "charge_loss_factor": 0.03,
            "discharge_loss_factor": 0.03,
            "pv_correction_factor": 1.0,
            "notes": "api test",
        },
    ).json()
    calibration_version = version["calibration_version"]
    assert client.post(f"/calibration/versions/{calibration_version}/approve").json()[
        "status"
    ] == "approved"
    resolved = client.get(
        "/calibration/resolve", params={"tenant_id": TENANT_ID, "site_id": SITE_ID}
    ).json()
    assert resolved["charge_loss_factor"] == pytest.approx(0.03)
    assert client.get("/calibration/versions").json()["count"] >= 1

    # --- Capsule --------------------------------------------------------
    sealed = client.post(
        "/capsules/seal",
        json={
            "tenant_id": TENANT_ID,
            "site_id": SITE_ID,
            "calibration_version": calibration_version,
            "slice_ids": ingest["slice_ids"],
            "slice_hashes": ingest["slice_hashes"],
            "admissibility_versions": ["admissibility_v3.0"],
        },
    ).json()
    capsule_id = sealed["capsule_id"]
    assert client.get(f"/capsules/{capsule_id}").json()["valid"] is True

    # --- Training extract -----------------------------------------------
    extract = client.post(
        "/training-extract/build",
        json={
            "tenant_id": TENANT_ID,
            "site_id": SITE_ID,
            "capsule_ids": [capsule_id],
            "calibration_version": calibration_version,
            "calibration_factors": resolved,
            "admissibility_versions": ["admissibility_v3.0"],
            "slice_ids": ingest["slice_ids"],
        },
    ).json()
    dataset_version = extract["dataset_version"]
    dataset = client.get(f"/training-extract/datasets/{dataset_version}").json()
    assert dataset["rows"] > 0
    assert set(dataset["split_counts"]) == {"train", "validate", "test"}

    # --- Model -----------------------------------------------------------
    trained = client.post(
        "/model/chariot/train",
        json={
            "tenant_id": TENANT_ID,
            "site_id": SITE_ID,
            "dataset_version": dataset_version,
            "n_scenarios": 40,
        },
    ).json()
    model_version = trained["model_version"]
    assert client.get("/model/chariot/versions").json()["count"] >= 1
    assert client.post("/model/chariot/promote", json={"model_version": model_version}).json()[
        "status"
    ] == "active"

    inference = client.post(
        "/model/chariot/infer",
        json={
            "tenant_id": TENANT_ID,
            "site_id": SITE_ID,
            "model_version": model_version,
            "records": telemetry[:100],
        },
    ).json()
    assert inference["n_rows"] > 0
    assert inference["one_step_mae_w"] is not None

    # --- Scenarios -------------------------------------------------------
    generated = client.post(
        "/model/scenarios/generate",
        json={
            "tenant_id": TENANT_ID,
            "site_id": SITE_ID,
            "model_version": model_version,
            "n_scenarios": 40,
            "records": telemetry[:96],
        },
    ).json()
    batch_id = generated["batch_id"]
    assert client.get(f"/model/scenarios/batches/{batch_id}").json()["batch_id"] == batch_id

    # --- Tribunal --------------------------------------------------------
    verdict = client.post("/tribunal/validate-batch", json={"batch_id": batch_id}).json()
    assert verdict["n_proposed"] == 40
    assert verdict["n_retained"] + verdict["n_rejected"] == 40
    retained = client.get(f"/tribunal/batches/{batch_id}/retained").json()
    assert retained["n_retained"] == verdict["n_retained"]
    rejections = client.get(f"/tribunal/batches/{batch_id}/rejections").json()
    assert rejections["count"] == verdict["n_rejected"]

    # --- Governor --------------------------------------------------------
    assert client.get("/residual/governor/status").json()["effective_w"] >= 0.0
    client.post(
        "/residual/governor/set-weight", json={"w": 0.1, "actor": "test"}
    )
    assert client.get("/residual/governor/status").json()["effective_w"] == pytest.approx(0.1)

    # --- Confidence -------------------------------------------------------
    report = client.post("/confidence/evaluate", json={"batch_id": batch_id}).json()
    assert report["status"] in {
        "eligible_for_human_review",
        "reduced_confidence",
        "abstained",
    }
    assert client.get(f"/confidence/reports/{report['report_id']}").json()[
        "report_id"
    ] == report["report_id"]

    # --- Advisory ---------------------------------------------------------
    forecast = client.post(
        "/advisory/forecast",
        json={"batch_id": batch_id, "role_mode": "grid_engineer"},
    ).json()
    assert forecast["advisory_only"] is True
    assert forecast["not_a_controller"] is True
    assert forecast["solar_w"]["p10"]
    events = client.get("/advisory/events", params={"batch_id": batch_id}).json()
    assert "horizon_steps" in events

    # --- Feedback ---------------------------------------------------------
    job = client.post(
        "/feedback/cortex/run", json={"batch_id": batch_id, "survival_rate": 0.9}
    ).json()
    assert job["batch_id"] == batch_id
    assert client.get("/feedback/jobs").json()["count"] >= 1


def test_rejection_echo_is_addressable(client: TestClient) -> None:
    """The Echo is an append-only memory, not a log that can be dropped."""
    echo = client.get("/tribunal/rejections").json()
    assert "rejections" in echo
    for row in echo["rejections"]:
        assert row["rejection_id"]
        assert row["batch_id"]
        assert row["constraint_codes"] is not None
        one = client.get(f"/tribunal/rejections/{row['rejection_id']}").json()
        assert one["rejection_id"] == row["rejection_id"]
    assert client.get("/tribunal/rejections/does-not-exist").status_code == 404


def test_policies_are_versioned_never_edited_in_place(client: TestClient) -> None:
    """A policy change mints a new version; the old one stays on record."""
    before = client.get("/tribunal/policy").json()
    assert before["policy_version"].startswith("tribunal_v")
    assert before["effective"] is True

    updated = client.put(
        "/tribunal/policy",
        json={"energy_balance_tol_wh": 0.25, "actor": "test", "reason": "tolerance change"},
    ).json()
    assert updated["policy_version"] != before["policy_version"]
    assert updated["previous_version"] == before["policy_version"]
    assert updated["energy_balance_tol_wh"] == pytest.approx(0.25)

    after = client.get("/tribunal/policy").json()
    assert after["policy_version"] == updated["policy_version"]
    assert after["energy_balance_tol_wh"] == pytest.approx(0.25)
    assert before["policy_version"] in after["versions"]

    # The old version is still on record — nothing was silently overwritten.
    assert client.put("/tribunal/policy", json={}).status_code == 400
    assert (
        client.put("/tribunal/policy", json={"soc_min": 90.0, "soc_max": 10.0}).status_code
        == 400
    )

    confidence = client.put(
        "/confidence/policy", json={"c_low": 0.55, "actor": "test"}
    ).json()
    assert confidence["policy_version"] != "confidence_v3.0"
    assert client.get("/confidence/policy").json()["c_low"] == pytest.approx(0.55)


def test_advisory_carries_full_lineage(client: TestClient) -> None:
    """Every advisory response carries the unbroken lineage chain."""
    batches = client.get("/model/scenarios/batches/none").status_code
    assert batches == 404

    # Re-derive a batch id from the last scenario batch in the store.
    from chariot.store import Store  # local import: keeps the module import-light

    store = Store(client.app.state.store_path) if hasattr(client.app.state, "store_path") else None
    if store is None:
        pytest.skip("store path not exposed on the app state")
    rows = store.query("SELECT batch_id FROM tribunal_batches ORDER BY created_at_utc DESC")
    if not rows:
        pytest.skip("no validated batch in the store")
    batch_id = rows[0]["batch_id"]
    store.close()

    forecast = client.post(
        "/advisory/forecast", json={"batch_id": batch_id, "role_mode": "research"}
    ).json()
    lineage = forecast["lineage"]
    for key in (
        "admissibility",
        "calibration_version",
        "capsule_id",
        "dataset_version",
        "chariot_model_version",
        "scenario_batch",
        "tribunal_result",
        "confidence_report",
        "policies",
    ):
        assert key in lineage, f"lineage is missing {key}"
    assert lineage["scenario_batch"]["batch_id"] == batch_id
    assert lineage["tribunal_result"]["n_retained"] >= 0
    assert lineage["policies"]["system_version"]

"""Tests for the OFFLINE PyMC reference path.

The reference is an **optional** add-on: the operational spine must work
with or without PyMC installed, and nothing in the spine may import it.
The tests that genuinely need PyMC are marked ``pymc`` and are skipped when
the dependency is missing.

Run only the (slow) reference tests with::

    python -m pytest tests/test_pymc_reference.py -q -m pymc

The full suite (including the reference) with::

    python -m pytest tests/ -q
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from chariot.config import SiteContext  # noqa: E402
from chariot.model import ELEVATION_BINS, clear_sky_series  # noqa: E402
from chariot.pymc_clearness import (  # noqa: E402
    CLEARNESS_MAX,
    LIT_FRACTION,
    PymcClearnessFit,
    PymcReferenceConfig,
    PymcUnavailableError,
    build_pymc_model,
    fit_pymc_clearness,
    prepare_clearness_data,
    pymc_available,
    require_pymc,
)

from conftest import SITE_ID, TENANT_ID  # noqa: E402


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fixtures
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def reference_ctx() -> SiteContext:
    """A site context that mirrors the DKASC reference site."""
    return SiteContext(
        tenant_id=TENANT_ID,
        site_id=SITE_ID,
        name="DKASC Alice Springs (reference)",
        rated_pv_w=220_000.0,
        usable_battery_capacity_wh=220_000.0,
        max_charge_w=110_000.0,
        max_discharge_w=110_000.0,
        timezone="Australia/Darwin",
        latitude=-23.762,
        longitude=133.875,
        fleet_id="dkasc",
        metadata={
            "load_profile_w": {str(h): 16000.0 + 2000.0 * (h % 7) for h in range(24)},
            "load_relative_sigma": 0.18,
            "initial_soc_percent": 50.0,
            "charge_loss_factor": 0.03,
            "discharge_loss_factor": 0.03,
        },
        source="site_master",
        missing_rating=False,
    )


def _fake_fit(n_draws: int = 4) -> PymcClearnessFit:
    """A reference fit built from synthetic posterior draws (no PyMC needed)."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    n_bins = int(ELEVATION_BINS.size - 1)
    return PymcClearnessFit(
        backend="pymc_nuts",
        model_version="pymc_clearness_v3.0_nuts_test",
        model_name="chariot_pymc_clearness_reference",
        tenant_id=TENANT_ID,
        site_id=SITE_ID,
        fleet_id="dkasc",
        dataset_version="dataset_test",
        dataset_content_hash="0" * 64,
        posterior={
            "rho": rng.uniform(0.8, 0.97, size=n_draws),
            "sigma_process": rng.uniform(0.05, 0.2, size=n_draws),
            "obs_scale": rng.uniform(0.02, 0.08, size=n_draws),
        },
        log_profile=rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=(n_draws, n_bins)),
        profile_edges=[float(v) for v in ELEVATION_BINS],
        season_cos=np.zeros(n_draws),
        season_sin=np.zeros(n_draws),
        seasonal_selected="none",
        season_selection={"selected": "none"},
        origin_state={"v_mean": 0.0, "v_var": 0.05},
        diagnostics={"backend": "pymc_nuts", "chains": 2, "draws": n_draws},
        n_state_rows=96,
        n_obs_rows=40,
        config=PymcReferenceConfig(),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The spine must not depend on PyMC
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_operational_spine_never_imports_pymc() -> None:
    """The ops spine must run with PyMC absent — it is not an import of it."""
    code = (
        "import sys;"
        "sys.path.insert(0, %r);"
        "import chariot.pipeline, chariot.model, chariot.api, chariot.genesis;"
        "print('pymc' in sys.modules or 'pytensor' in sys.modules)"
        % str(ROOT)
    )
    out = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-c", code], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300
    )
    assert out.returncode == 0, out.stderr[-2000:]
    assert out.stdout.strip().endswith("False"), (
        "the operational spine must not pull PyMC/PyTensor in: " + out.stdout
    )


def test_reference_module_imports_and_reports_availability() -> None:
    assert isinstance(pymc_available(), bool)
    if pymc_available():
        require_pymc()
    else:  # pragma: no cover - depends on the environment
        with pytest.raises(PymcUnavailableError):
            require_pymc()


def test_config_validation_rejects_nonsense() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        PymcReferenceConfig(backend="mcmc").validate()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        PymcReferenceConfig(season="sometimes").validate()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        PymcReferenceConfig(chains=0).validate()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        PymcReferenceConfig(draws=0).validate()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Data preparation mirrors the ops model
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_prepared_data_mirrors_the_ops_lit_mask(
    reference_frame: pd.DataFrame, reference_ctx: SiteContext
) -> None:
    data = prepare_clearness_data(reference_frame, reference_ctx)
    assert data.n == len(reference_frame)
    assert data.n_obs > 0
    assert data.n_bins == int(ELEVATION_BINS.size - 1)

    clear = np.nan_to_num(clear_sky_series(reference_frame), nan=0.0)
    valid = np.isfinite(reference_frame["solar_w"].to_numpy()) & np.isfinite(clear)
    obs = valid & (clear > 1.0)
    peak = float(np.nanpercentile(clear[obs], 90))
    expected = obs & (clear > LIT_FRACTION * max(peak, 1.0))
    assert np.array_equal(data.lit, expected)
    # Every observed row carries a finite log clearness ratio.
    assert np.isfinite(data.log_obs[data.obs_index]).all()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The adapter (no PyMC needed)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_reference_paths_are_night_zero_finite_and_capped(
    reference_frame: pd.DataFrame, reference_ctx: SiteContext
) -> None:
    fit = _fake_fit()
    future = reference_frame.head(96)
    paths = fit.simulate_paths(future, reference_ctx, n_paths=25, seed=11)

    solar = paths["solar_w"]
    load = paths["load_w"]
    assert solar.shape == (25, 96)
    assert load.shape == (25, 96)
    assert np.isfinite(solar).all()
    assert np.isfinite(load).all()
    assert (load >= 0.0).all()

    clear = np.nan_to_num(clear_sky_series(future), nan=0.0)
    night = clear <= 1.0
    assert night.any(), "the synthetic frame must contain night rows"
    assert np.all(solar[:, night] == 0.0), "night rows must be exactly zero"

    day = ~night
    ratio = solar[:, day] / np.maximum(clear[None, day], 1.0)
    assert ratio.max() <= CLEARNESS_MAX + 1e-9, "the clearness ceiling must hold"
    assert (solar >= 0.0).all()


def test_reference_paths_respect_the_site_rating(
    reference_frame: pd.DataFrame, reference_ctx: SiteContext
) -> None:
    fit = _fake_fit()
    paths = fit.simulate_paths(reference_frame.head(48), reference_ctx, n_paths=8, seed=5)
    assert paths["solar_w"].max() <= reference_ctx.rated_pv_w * 1.2 + 1e-6


def test_reference_paths_track_the_clear_sky_ratio(
    reference_frame: pd.DataFrame, reference_ctx: SiteContext
) -> None:
    """Regression: the posterior profile is a *log* ratio and must be exponentiated.

    With the profile used as a ratio instead of a log ratio the reference
    produced essentially zero solar (a -97 kW bias) while still passing the
    "finite / night-zero / capped" checks.  The clearness the reference
    predicts must be a plausible fraction of the clear-sky power.
    """
    fit = _fake_fit()
    future = reference_frame.head(96)
    paths = fit.simulate_paths(future, reference_ctx, n_paths=12, seed=17)
    clear = np.nan_to_num(clear_sky_series(future), nan=0.0)
    day = clear > 1.0
    ratio = paths["solar_w"][:, day] / clear[None, day]
    median = float(np.median(ratio))
    assert 0.4 < median < 1.3, (
        f"the reference clearness must be a plausible fraction of clear sky, got {median}"
    )
    # The band must not be degenerate either.
    assert float(np.std(ratio)) > 1e-3


def test_reference_channel_fit_adapter_shape() -> None:
    fit = _fake_fit()
    channel = fit.to_channel_fit()
    assert channel.gamma == 1.0
    assert channel.mu == 0.0
    assert len(channel.profile) == int(ELEVATION_BINS.size - 1)
    assert 0.0 <= channel.rho < 1.0
    assert channel.obs_floor > 0.0


def test_manifest_carries_every_required_field() -> None:
    fit = _fake_fit()
    manifest = fit.manifest()
    for key in (
        "inference_backend",
        "model_version",
        "chains",
        "draws",
        "divergences",
        "rhat_max",
        "ess_min",
        "wall_time_s",
        "train_extract_content_hash",
    ):
        assert key in manifest, f"missing manifest field: {key}"
    assert manifest["inference_backend"] == "pymc_nuts"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The Genesis adapter seam
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_generate_batch_accepts_reference_paths_without_changing_the_default(
    reference_frame: pd.DataFrame, reference_ctx: SiteContext
) -> None:
    from chariot.genesis import generate_batch
    from chariot.model import fit as fit_model

    future = reference_frame.head(96)
    model = fit_model(
        reference_frame.head(400),
        reference_ctx,
        tenant_id=TENANT_ID,
        site_id=SITE_ID,
        fleet_id="dkasc",
    )
    state = {
        "v_mean": 0.0,
        "v_var": 0.05,
        "last_solar": 0.0,
        "last_load": 16000.0,
        "soc_percent": 50.0,
    }
    ops_batch = generate_batch(
        model,
        SITE_ID,
        future,
        tenant_id=TENANT_ID,
        n_scenarios=6,
        seed=3,
        ctx=reference_ctx,
        state=state,
    )
    assert ops_batch.n_scenarios == 6
    assert ops_batch.solar_w.shape == (6, 96)

    fit = _fake_fit()
    paths = fit.simulate_paths(future, reference_ctx, n_paths=6, seed=3)
    ref_batch = generate_batch(
        model,
        SITE_ID,
        future,
        tenant_id=TENANT_ID,
        n_scenarios=6,
        seed=3,
        ctx=reference_ctx,
        state=state,
        paths=paths,
    )
    # The supplied ensemble is used verbatim ...
    assert np.allclose(ref_batch.solar_w, paths["solar_w"])
    # ... and the physics is still solved for every path.
    assert ref_batch.battery_w.shape == (6, 96)
    assert ref_batch.grid_w.shape == (6, 96)
    assert ref_batch.soc_percent.shape == (6, 96)
    assert np.isfinite(ref_batch.soc_percent).all()
    # The lineage still points at the model version.
    assert ref_batch.lineage["model_version"] == model.model_version
    assert ref_batch.content_hash


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The reference fit itself (needs PyMC)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.pymc
def test_tiny_nuts_fit_produces_a_usable_posterior(
    reference_frame: pd.DataFrame, reference_ctx: SiteContext
) -> None:
    """A 1-chain, tiny NUTS fit on the synthetic reference site."""
    if not pymc_available():  # pragma: no cover - depends on the environment
        pytest.skip("PyMC is not installed")

    train = reference_frame.head(480).reset_index(drop=True)
    validate = reference_frame.iloc[480:576].reset_index(drop=True)
    config = PymcReferenceConfig(
        draws=25,
        tune=25,
        chains=1,
        cores=1,
        backend="nuts",
        season="off",
        random_seed=5,
    )
    fit = fit_pymc_clearness(
        train,
        reference_ctx,
        tenant_id=TENANT_ID,
        site_id=SITE_ID,
        fleet_id="dkasc",
        dataset_version="dataset_test",
        dataset_content_hash="a" * 64,
        validation=validate,
        config=config,
    )
    assert fit.backend == "pymc_nuts"
    assert np.isfinite(fit.posterior["rho"]).all()
    assert np.isfinite(fit.posterior["sigma_process"]).all()
    assert np.isfinite(fit.posterior["obs_scale"]).all()
    assert 0.0 < float(np.mean(fit.posterior["rho"])) < 1.0
    manifest = fit.manifest()
    assert manifest["chains"] == 1
    assert manifest["draws"] == 25
    assert manifest["inference_backend"] == "pymc_nuts"
    assert manifest["train_extract_content_hash"] == "a" * 64

    paths = fit.simulate_paths(reference_frame.head(96), reference_ctx, n_paths=10, seed=2)
    assert np.isfinite(paths["solar_w"]).all()
    clear = np.nan_to_num(clear_sky_series(reference_frame.head(96)), nan=0.0)
    assert np.all(paths["solar_w"][:, clear <= 1.0] == 0.0)


@pytest.mark.pymc
def test_model_builds_and_its_log_probability_is_finite(
    reference_frame: pd.DataFrame, reference_ctx: SiteContext
) -> None:
    """The AR(1) density is written as a Potential — no scan is compiled."""
    if not pymc_available():  # pragma: no cover - depends on the environment
        pytest.skip("PyMC is not installed")
    data = prepare_clearness_data(reference_frame, reference_ctx)
    model = build_pymc_model(data, season=True, config=PymcReferenceConfig())
    with model:
        logp = model.compile_logp()
        point = model.initial_point()
        value = float(logp(point))
    assert np.isfinite(value)


@pytest.mark.pymc
def test_ar1_state_resets_at_every_gap(
    reference_frame: pd.DataFrame, reference_ctx: SiteContext
) -> None:
    """The AR(1) chain must not carry information across a night gap.

    The clearness residual does not survive the night, so the state after a
    gap must depend only on the innovation after that gap.
    """
    if not pymc_available():  # pragma: no cover - depends on the environment
        pytest.skip("PyMC is not installed")
    import pytensor
    import pytensor.tensor as pt

    from chariot.pymc_clearness import _ar1_state

    rho = pt.dscalar("rho")
    innovation = pt.dvector("u")
    reset = np.array([True, False, True, False, False], dtype=bool)
    state = _ar1_state(rho, innovation, reset, int(reset.size))
    fn = pytensor.function([rho, innovation], state)
    u = np.array([0.5, 0.25, -0.75, 0.1, 0.2])
    out = fn(0.9, u)
    # row 0: fresh start; row 1: rho * 0.5 + 0.25; row 2: fresh start again
    assert np.isclose(out[0], 0.5)
    assert np.isclose(out[1], 0.9 * 0.5 + 0.25)
    assert np.isclose(out[2], -0.75)
    assert np.isclose(out[3], 0.9 * (-0.75) + 0.1)
    assert np.isclose(out[4], 0.9 * (0.9 * (-0.75) + 0.1) + 0.2)

# CHARIOT v3 — runtime dependencies
#
# The model core is deliberately pure NumPy: no PyMC/Stan, no GPU, no scipy.
# FastAPI/uvicorn are only needed for the HTTP surface (chariot/api.py);
